# ── Paths ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import sys as _sys

def _get_base_dir():
    """Return the folder that contains the EXE (or the script when running normally).
    PyInstaller sets sys.frozen and sys._MEIPASS when running as a bundled EXE."""
    if getattr(_sys, 'frozen', False):
        # Running as PyInstaller EXE — use the folder of the EXE itself
        return os.path.dirname(_sys.executable)
    # Running as a plain Python script
    return os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

BASE_DIR   = _get_base_dir()
KEY_FILE   = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "firebase_key.json")

# LICENSE CLIENT — pure stdlib, no Firebase SDK, no secrets
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime    import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib     import Path
import sys as _sys

LICENSE_CHARS     = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"
LICENSE_PREFIX    = "GP-"
LICENSE_BLOCK_LEN = 4
LICENSE_BLOCKS    = 4
LICENSE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000   # re-check every 30 min

_LICENSE_PROJECT_ID = "garage-pro-874c6"   # public — safe to hard-code
_GRACE_DAYS         = 7


def _lc_cache_path() -> Path:
    """OS-appropriate cache location."""
    if os.name == "nt":
        base = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", "~")).expanduser() / "GaragePro"
    elif _sys.platform == "darwin":
        base = Path("~/Library/Application Support/GaragePro").expanduser()
    else:
        base = Path(os.environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", "~/.config")).expanduser() / "GaragePro"
    return base / "license.json"


def _lc_flatten(field: dict):
    """Convert a Firestore REST {typeName: value} entry to a plain Python value."""
    if not field: return None
    if "stringValue"    in field: return field["stringValue"]
    if "integerValue"   in field: return int(field["integerValue"])
    if "doubleValue"    in field: return float(field["doubleValue"])
    if "booleanValue"   in field: return field["booleanValue"]
    if "timestampValue" in field:
        ts = field["timestampValue"]
        return datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if "nullValue"      in field: return None
    if "arrayValue"     in field:
        return [_lc_flatten(v) for v in field["arrayValue"].get("values", [])]
    if "mapValue"       in field:
        return {k: _lc_flatten(v) for k, v in
                field["mapValue"].get("fields", {}).items()}
    return None


def _lc_ssl_ctx():
    """Build an SSL context that works on Mac + Windows."""
    import ssl as _ssl
    try:
        import certifi as _cert
        return _ssl.create_default_context(cafile=_cert.where())
    except ImportError:
        pass
    try:
        ctx = _ssl.create_default_context()
        ctx.load_verify_locations("/etc/ssl/cert.pem")
        return ctx
    except Exception:
        pass
    ctx = _ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode    = _ssl.CERT_NONE
    return ctx


def _lc_fetch(license_key: str) -> dict | None:
    """
    Fetch licenses/{key} from Firestore REST.
    Returns flattened dict of fields, or None on 404, or raises on other errors.
    """
    import urllib.request as _ur, urllib.error as _ue
    url = (
        f"https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/"
        f"projects/{_LICENSE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/"
        f"licenses/{license_key}"
    )
    try:
        req = _ur.Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json"})
        with _ur.urlopen(req, timeout=8, context=_lc_ssl_ctx()) as resp:
            raw = json.loads(resp.read().decode())
    except _ue.HTTPError as e:
        if e.code == 404: return None
        raise
    fields = raw.get("fields", {})
    return {k: _lc_flatten(v) for k, v in fields.items()}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubscriptionStatus:
    active:        bool
    status:        str
    plan:          str
    plan_name:     str
    billing_cycle: str
    expiry:        datetime | None
    indefinite:    bool
    license_key:   str
    email:         str
    name:          str          # customer full name
    garage_name:   str          # workshop / company name — shown in app header
    max_bays:      int
    max_workers:   int
    max_bookings:  int
    is_trial:      bool
    trial_days:    int
    checked_at:    datetime
    reason:        str

    @property
    def trial_days_left(self) -> int | None:
        if not self.is_trial or not self.expiry: return None
        return max(0, (self.expiry - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).days)

    def header_pill(self) -> str | None:
        if not self.active or not self.is_trial: return None
        d = self.trial_days_left or 0
        return f"FREE TRIAL · {d} day{'s' if d != 1 else ''} left"

    def unlimited(self, val: int) -> bool: return val >= 999

    @property
    def days_remaining(self) -> int | None:
        if self.indefinite or not self.expiry: return None
        diff = self.expiry - datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        return max(0, diff.days)

    @property
    def app_brand(self) -> str:
        """Return garage name for header, fallback to Garage Pro."""
        return self.garage_name or "Garage Pro"


_INACTIVE_STATUS = SubscriptionStatus(
    active=False, status="not_activated", plan="", plan_name="",
    billing_cycle="", expiry=None, indefinite=False,
    license_key="", email="", name="", garage_name="",
    max_bays=0, max_workers=0, max_bookings=0,
    is_trial=False, trial_days=0,
    checked_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), reason="No license activated."
)


class LicenseClient:
    """
    Pure-stdlib license client.
    No Firebase SDK, no secrets, no writes to Firestore.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self._path = _lc_cache_path()
        self._path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def activate(self, license_key: str) -> SubscriptionStatus:
        """Fetch the license doc. Does NOT save cache — call confirm() after user confirms."""
        key = license_key.strip().upper()
        try:
            doc = _lc_fetch(key)
        except Exception as e:
            return self._make_status(active=False, status="offline",
                                     reason="Could not reach the server. Check your connection.",
                                     key=key)

        if doc is None:
            return self._make_status(active=False, status="not_found",
                                     reason="License key not recognised.",
                                     key=key)
        return self._process_doc(doc, key, save=False)   # save=False until confirmed

    def confirm(self, sub: SubscriptionStatus):
        """Called after user confirms the license details — saves cache."""
        cache_data = {
            "license_key":   sub.license_key,
            "email":         sub.email,
            "name":          sub.name,
            "garage_name":   sub.garage_name,
            "status":        "active",
            "plan":          sub.plan,
            "plan_name":     sub.plan_name,
            "billing_cycle": sub.billing_cycle,
            "indefinite":    sub.indefinite,
            "expiry":        sub.expiry.isoformat() if sub.expiry else "",
            "max_bays":      sub.max_bays,
            "max_workers":   sub.max_workers,
            "max_bookings":  sub.max_bookings,
            "is_trial":      sub.is_trial,
            "trial_days":    sub.trial_days,
            "checked_at":    datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        }
        self._save_cache(cache_data)
        LIMITS.update_from_status(cache_data)

    def verify(self, force_online: bool = False) -> SubscriptionStatus:
        """Load cache; optionally re-fetch online."""
        cache = self._load_cache()
        if not cache.get("license_key"):
            return _INACTIVE_STATUS

        key = cache["license_key"]
        if force_online or self._should_check_online(cache):
            try:
                doc = _lc_fetch(key)
                if doc is None:
                    return self._make_status(active=False, status="not_found",
                                             reason="License no longer exists.", key=key)
                return self._process_doc(doc, key, save=True)
            except Exception:
                pass  # fall through to cached status

        return self._status_from_cache()

    def status(self) -> SubscriptionStatus:
        """Return cached status without hitting the network."""
        return self._status_from_cache()

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Delete the license cache."""
        try: self._path.unlink()
        except FileNotFoundError: pass

    # ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _load_cache(self) -> dict:
        try:
            return json.loads(self._path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def _save_cache(self, data: dict):
        tmp = self._path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
        os.replace(tmp, self._path)

    def _should_check_online(self, cache: dict) -> bool:
        checked = cache.get("checked_at", "")
        if not checked: return True
        try:
            ts = datetime.fromisoformat(checked.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            if ts.tzinfo is None: ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            return (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - ts) > timedelta(hours=1)
        except Exception:
            return True

    def _process_doc(self, doc: dict, key: str, save: bool) -> SubscriptionStatus:
        status_str  = str(doc.get("status", ""))
        indefinite  = bool(doc.get("indefinite", False))
        expiry_raw  = doc.get("expiry")
        expiry      = expiry_raw if isinstance(expiry_raw, datetime) else None
        now         = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        if status_str == "revoked":
            if save: self._save_cache({**self._doc_to_cache(doc, key), "status": "revoked"})
            return self._make_status(active=False, status="revoked",
                                     reason="License revoked. Contact support@garagepro.app",
                                     doc=doc, key=key)
        # Only check expiry if NOT indefinite
        if not indefinite and expiry and now > expiry:
            if save: self._save_cache({**self._doc_to_cache(doc, key), "status": "expired"})
            return self._make_status(active=False, status="expired",
                                     reason="Subscription expired. Renew at garagepro.app/account",
                                     doc=doc, key=key)
        if status_str != "active":
            return self._make_status(active=False, status=status_str or "inactive",
                                     reason=f"License status: {status_str}", doc=doc, key=key)

        cache_data = {**self._doc_to_cache(doc, key),
                      "checked_at": now.isoformat()}
        if save:
            self._save_cache(cache_data)
            LIMITS.update_from_status(cache_data)

        return self._make_status(active=True, status="active",
                                 reason="", doc=doc, key=key,
                                 checked_at=now)

    def _doc_to_cache(self, doc: dict, key: str) -> dict:
        indefinite = bool(doc.get("indefinite", False))
        return {
            "license_key":   key,
            "uid":           str(doc.get("uid","") or ""),
            "email":         str(doc.get("email","") or ""),
            "name":          str(doc.get("name","") or ""),
            "garage_name":   str(doc.get("garageName","") or doc.get("garage_name","") or ""),
            "status":        str(doc.get("status","") or ""),
            "plan":          str(doc.get("plan","") or ""),
            "plan_name":     str(doc.get("planName","") or doc.get("plan_name","") or doc.get("plan","") or ""),
            "billing_cycle": str(doc.get("billingCycle","") or doc.get("billing_cycle","") or "monthly"),
            "indefinite":    indefinite,
            "expiry":        doc.get("expiry","") if not indefinite else "",
            "max_bays":      int(doc.get("maxBays", 999) or 999),
            "max_workers":   int(doc.get("maxWorkers", 999) or 999),
            "max_bookings":  int(doc.get("maxBookings", 999) or 999),
            "is_trial":      bool(doc.get("isTrial", False)),
            "trial_days":    int(doc.get("trialDays", 0) or 0),
        }

    def _status_from_cache(self, offline_reason: str = "") -> SubscriptionStatus:
        cache = self._load_cache()
        if not cache.get("license_key"):
            return _INACTIVE_STATUS

        checked_str = cache.get("checked_at", "")
        if checked_str:
            try:
                ts = datetime.fromisoformat(checked_str.replace("Z","+00:00"))
                if ts.tzinfo is None: ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                days_old = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - ts).days
                if days_old > _GRACE_DAYS and cache.get("status") == "active":
                    return self._make_status(
                        active=False, status="grace_expired",
                        reason=f"Please reconnect to verify your subscription "
                               f"(last verified {days_old} days ago).",
                        cache=cache)
            except Exception:
                pass

        cached_status = cache.get("status","")
        if cached_status == "revoked":
            return self._make_status(active=False, status="revoked",
                                     reason="License revoked.", cache=cache)
        if cached_status in ("active",):
            reason = f"Offline mode — {offline_reason}" if offline_reason else "offline"
            return self._make_status(active=True,
                                     status="offline" if offline_reason else "active",
                                     reason=reason, cache=cache)
        return self._make_status(active=False, status=cached_status or "not_activated",
                                 reason=offline_reason or "Cannot verify subscription.",
                                 cache=cache)

    def _make_status(self, active: bool, status: str, reason: str,
                     doc: dict = None, cache: dict = None,
                     key: str = "", checked_at: datetime = None) -> SubscriptionStatus:
        src   = doc or cache or {}
        now   = checked_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        expiry_raw = src.get("expiry")
        if isinstance(expiry_raw, str) and expiry_raw:
            try:
                expiry_raw = datetime.fromisoformat(expiry_raw.replace("Z","+00:00"))
            except Exception:
                expiry_raw = None
        elif not isinstance(expiry_raw, datetime):
            expiry_raw = None
        indef = bool(src.get("indefinite", False))
        return SubscriptionStatus(
            active        = active,
            status        = status,
            plan          = str(src.get("plan","") or ""),
            plan_name     = str(src.get("planName","") or src.get("plan_name","") or src.get("plan","") or ""),
            billing_cycle = str(src.get("billingCycle","") or src.get("billing_cycle","") or "monthly"),
            expiry        = None if indef else expiry_raw,
            indefinite    = indef,
            license_key   = key or str(src.get("license_key","") or src.get("key","") or ""),
            email         = str(src.get("email","") or ""),
            name          = str(src.get("name","") or ""),
            garage_name   = str(src.get("garageName","") or src.get("garage_name","") or ""),
            max_bays      = int(src.get("maxBays",src.get("max_bays",999)) or 999),
            max_workers   = int(src.get("maxWorkers",src.get("max_workers",999)) or 999),
            max_bookings  = int(src.get("maxBookings",src.get("max_bookings",999)) or 999),
            is_trial      = bool(src.get("isTrial",src.get("is_trial",False))),
            trial_days    = int(src.get("trialDays",src.get("trial_days",0)) or 0),
            checked_at    = now,
            reason        = reason,
        )


# Global client instance
_lc = LicenseClient()


# ── PlanLimits singleton — updated from SubscriptionStatus ───────────────────
class PlanLimits:
    """Global plan limits — read anywhere via LIMITS.*"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.plan         = "Solo"
        self.billing      = "monthly"
        self.max_bays     = 999
        self.max_workers  = 999
        self.max_bookings = 999
        self.expiry       = None
        self.garage_name  = ""
        self._listeners   = []

    def update_from_status(self, src: dict):
        self.plan         = str(src.get("plan","") or src.get("plan_name","") or "")
        self.billing      = str(src.get("billingCycle","") or src.get("billing_cycle","") or "monthly")
        self.garage_name  = str(src.get("garage_name","") or src.get("garageName","") or "")
        try: self.max_bays     = int(src.get("max_bays", src.get("maxBays",999)) or 999)
        except: pass
        try: self.max_workers  = int(src.get("max_workers", src.get("maxWorkers",999)) or 999)
        except: pass
        try: self.max_bookings = int(src.get("max_bookings", src.get("maxBookings",999)) or 999)
        except: pass
        expiry = src.get("expiry")
        if isinstance(expiry, str):
            try: expiry = datetime.fromisoformat(expiry.replace("Z","+00:00"))
            except: expiry = None
        self.expiry = expiry if isinstance(expiry, datetime) else None
        for fn in list(self._listeners):
            try: fn()
            except Exception: pass

    def update_from_sub(self, sub: SubscriptionStatus):
        self.plan         = sub.plan_name or sub.plan
        self.billing      = sub.billing_cycle
        self.max_bays     = sub.max_bays
        self.max_workers  = sub.max_workers
        self.max_bookings = sub.max_bookings
        self.expiry       = sub.expiry
        for fn in list(self._listeners):
            try: fn()
            except Exception: pass

    # Back-compat aliases
    def update(self, data: dict): self.update_from_status(data)

    def unlimited(self, val: int) -> bool: return val >= 999
    def add_listener(self, fn): self._listeners.append(fn)
    def remove_listener(self, fn):
        try: self._listeners.remove(fn)
        except ValueError: pass


LIMITS = PlanLimits()


def _load_limits_from_cache():
    cache = _lc._load_cache()
    if cache: LIMITS.update_from_status(cache)

# ── Firebase singleton ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_db = None

_firebase_was_connected = False

def _init_firebase():
    global _db, _firebase_was_connected
    if _db is not None: return True
    if not _FB_AVAILABLE or not os.path.exists(KEY_FILE): return False
    try:
        if not firebase_admin._apps:
            firebase_admin.initialize_app(credentials.Certificate(KEY_FILE))
        _db = firestore.client()
        _firebase_was_connected = True
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Firebase init failed: {e}"); return False

def firebase_connected() -> bool: return _db is not None

def _fb_load(collection, default):
    if not firebase_connected(): return default
    try:
        doc = _db.collection(collection).document("data").get()
        return doc.to_dict().get("items", default) if doc.exists else default
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Firebase load '{collection}' failed: {e}"); return default

def _fb_save(collection, data, callback=None):
    if not firebase_connected():
        if callback: callback(False); return
    def _run():
        try:
            # Always sanitise — converts tuples→lists recursively so Firestore
            # never sees an unhashable type
            safe = _make_json_safe(data)
            _db.collection(collection).document("data").set({"items": safe})
            if callback: callback(True)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Firebase save '{collection}' failed: {e}")
            if callback: callback(False)
    threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True).start()

C.update(DARK)

def apply_theme(dark: bool):
    C.clear(); C.update(DARK if dark else LIGHT)

# ── JSON / cloud helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def load_json(path, default):
    if os.path.exists(path):
        try:
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f: return json.load(f)
        except Exception: pass
    return default

def save_json(path, data):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

# Offline sync queue — collections modified while Firebase was offline
_PENDING_SYNC_FILE = os.path.join(_APP_DATA, "pending_sync.json")

def _load_pending_sync() -> set:
    try:
        return set(json.loads(Path(_PENDING_SYNC_FILE).read_text()))
    except Exception:
        return set()

def _mark_pending(collection: str):
    pending = _load_pending_sync()
    pending.add(collection)
    Path(_PENDING_SYNC_FILE).write_text(json.dumps(list(pending)))

def _clear_pending(collection: str):
    pending = _load_pending_sync()
    pending.discard(collection)
    Path(_PENDING_SYNC_FILE).write_text(json.dumps(list(pending)))

def cloud_load(collection, local_path, default):
    """Load from Firebase if connected (and save locally), else load from JSON."""
    if firebase_connected():
        data = _fb_load(collection, None)
        if data is not None:
            save_json(local_path, data)
            _clear_pending(collection)
            return data
    # Offline or Firebase failed — use local JSON
    return load_json(local_path, default)

def cloud_save(collection, local_path, data):
    """Always save locally. Push to Firebase if connected, else mark as pending."""
    save_json(local_path, data)
    if firebase_connected():
        _fb_save(collection, data)
        _clear_pending(collection)
    else:
        _mark_pending(collection)

def sync_pending_to_firebase(collections_map: dict):
    """
    Called when Firebase connection is restored.
    Uploads any collections that were modified while offline.
    collections_map: {collection_name: data_list}
    """
    pending = _load_pending_sync()
    if not pending or not firebase_connected():
        return
    for collection in list(pending):
        data = collections_map.get(collection)
        if data is not None:
            _fb_save(collection, data,
                     callback=lambda ok, c=collection: _clear_pending(c) if ok else None)
    print(f"[sync] Uploading {len(pending)} pending collection(s) to Firebase")

# ── Settings ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
    "dark_mode": False, "workshop_name": "My Garage",
    "workshop_address": "",   "invoice_logo": "",
    "bay_defs": DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS,
    "email_sender": "", "email_password": "", "last_backup": "",
    "manager_email": "",
    "server_port": 5000,
    # Invoice settings
    "garage_phone": "",
    "garage_email": "",
    "gstin": "",
    "tax_percent": 18.0,      # GST %
    # bank details are stored per-invoice in job["invoice_profile"]
}

def load_settings() -> dict:
    """Load settings from local file only — never from Firebase.
    Settings are machine-specific (logo path, dark mode, etc.) so they
    should not roam across devices via cloud."""
    raw = load_json(SETTINGS_FILE, {})
    s = _settings_from_json(raw) if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    for k, v in DEFAULT_SETTINGS.items():
        if k not in s: s[k] = v
    return s

def _make_json_safe(obj, _depth=0):
    """Recursively convert tuples→lists and strip any non-primitive types
    so Firestore never sees an invalid nested entity.
    Hard-limit depth to 20 to prevent runaway recursion."""
    if _depth > 20: return str(obj)
    if obj is None or isinstance(obj, bool): return obj
    if isinstance(obj, int):   return obj
    if isinstance(obj, float): return obj
    if isinstance(obj, str):   return obj
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {str(k): _make_json_safe(v, _depth+1) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [_make_json_safe(i, _depth+1) for i in obj]
    # Anything else (sets, custom objects, etc.) → stringify
    return str(obj)

def _settings_to_json(s: dict) -> dict:
    """Convert all tuples to lists recursively so Firestore accepts them."""
    return _make_json_safe(s)

def _settings_from_json(s: dict) -> dict:
    """Convert lists back to tuples for bay_defs after loading."""
    out = dict(s)
    if "bay_defs" in out:
        out["bay_defs"] = [tuple(x) if isinstance(x, list) else x
                           for x in out["bay_defs"]]
    return out

def save_settings(s: dict):
    """Save settings to local file only — never pushed to Firebase.
    Settings are machine-specific (paths, theme, PIN, etc.)."""
    save_json(SETTINGS_FILE, _make_json_safe(_settings_to_json(s)))

# ── Bay helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def make_bays(bay_defs: list) -> list:
    bays = []
    for bay_type, count in bay_defs:
        for i in range(1, count + 1):
            bays.append({"id": f"{bay_type}_{i}", "type": bay_type, "num": i,
                         "status": "Available", "plate": None, "booking_id": None,
                         "note": "", "maintenance": False})
    return bays

# ── Misc helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def uid() -> str: return str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]

def booking_ref() -> str:
    """Generate a human-readable booking reference like BK-8A3F."""
    return "BK-" + str(uuid.uuid4())[:4].upper()

def fmt_date(d: str) -> str:
    if not d: return "—"
    try: return datetime.strptime(d, "%Y-%m-%d").strftime("%d %b %Y")
    except: return d

def today_str() -> str: return date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
def now_str()   -> str: return datetime.now().strftime("%d %b %Y %H:%M")

# ── Backup ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_backup(settings: dict):
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    dest  = os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, stamp)
    os.makedirs(dest, exist_ok=True)
    for f in [BAYS_FILE, BOOKINGS_FILE, CUSTOMERS_FILE,
              INVENTORY_FILE, MECHANICS_FILE, JOBS_FILE]:
        if os.path.exists(f): shutil.copy2(f, dest)
    all_bk = sorted(os.listdir(BACKUP_DIR))
    for old in all_bk[:-30]:
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(BACKUP_DIR, old), ignore_errors=True)
    settings["last_backup"] = now_str()
    save_settings(settings)

# ── Email ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def send_email(settings: dict, to_addr: str, subject: str, body: str,
               cc_addr: str = "") -> str:
    """Send an email via Gmail SMTP. cc_addr is optional CC recipient."""
    sender = settings.get("email_sender", "").strip()
    pwd    = settings.get("email_password", "").strip()
    if not sender or not pwd:
        return "Email not configured. Add sender and app-password in Settings."
    if not to_addr or "@" not in to_addr:
        return "No valid recipient email address."
    try:
        msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
        msg["Subject"] = subject
        msg["From"]    = sender
        msg["To"]      = to_addr
        all_recipients = [to_addr]
        if cc_addr and "@" in cc_addr:
            msg["Cc"] = cc_addr
            all_recipients.append(cc_addr)
        msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain", "utf-8"))
        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL("smtp.gmail.com", 465, timeout=10) as srv:
            srv.login(sender, pwd)
            srv.sendmail(sender, all_recipients, msg.as_string())
        return ""
    except Exception as e:
        return str(e)

def send_email_async(settings, to_addr, subject, body, callback=None, cc_addr=""):
    def run():
        err = send_email(settings, to_addr, subject, body, cc_addr=cc_addr)
        if callback: callback(err)
    threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()

# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# REUSABLE WIDGETS
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class ScrollableFrame(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, **kw):
        super().__init__(parent, **kw)
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(self, highlightthickness=0, bg=C["bg"])
        self.vsb    = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="vertical", command=self.canvas.yview)
        self.inner  = tk.Frame(self.canvas, bg=C["bg"])
        self.inner.bind("<Configure>", lambda e: self.canvas.configure(
            scrollregion=self.canvas.bbox("all")))
        self._win = self.canvas.create_window((0, 0), window=self.inner, anchor="nw")
        self.canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=self.vsb.set)
        self.canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        self.vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        self.canvas.bind("<Configure>",
                         lambda e: self.canvas.itemconfig(self._win, width=e.width))
        def _on_mousewheel(event):
            if _OS == "Darwin":
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * event.delta), "units")
            elif _OS == "Windows":
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * (event.delta / 120)), "units")
            else:
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * event.delta), "units")

        self.canvas.bind("<Enter>", lambda e: (
            self.canvas.bind_all("<MouseWheel>", _on_mousewheel),
            self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-4>",
                                  lambda ev: self.canvas.yview_scroll(-1, "units")),
            self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-5>",
                                  lambda ev: self.canvas.yview_scroll(1, "units"))))
        self.canvas.bind("<Leave>", lambda e: (
            self.canvas.unbind_all("<MouseWheel>"),
            self.canvas.unbind_all("<Button-4>"),
            self.canvas.unbind_all("<Button-5>")))
        # (scroll binding complete)

    def _scroll(self, e):
        self.canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * (e.delta / 120)), "units")

    def _scroll_mac(self, e):
        self.canvas.yview_scroll(int(-1 * e.delta), "units")


class Btn(tk.Button):
    """Themed button — colours resolved from C[] at instantiation time."""
    _STYLES = {
        "default": ("surface",    "text",    "border"),
        "primary": ("accent",     "bg",      "accent"),
        "danger":  ("danger_bg",  "danger",  "danger"),
        "success": ("success_bg", "success", "success"),
        "warning": ("warning_bg", "warning", "warning"),
        "info":    ("info_bg",    "info",    "info"),
        "purple":  ("purple_bg",  "purple",  "purple"),
        "orange":  ("orange_bg",  "orange",  "orange"),
    }
    def __init__(self, parent, text, cmd=None, style="default", small=False, **kw):
        bk, fk, hk = self._STYLES.get(style, self._STYLES["default"])
        super().__init__(
            parent, text=text, command=cmd,
            font=F(9 if small else 10),
            padx=8 if small else 12, pady=3 if small else 5,
            cursor="hand2", relief="flat", bd=0,
            bg=C[bk], fg=C[fk],
            activebackground=C.get("tab_sel", C["border"]),
            activeforeground=C["text"],
            highlightbackground=C[hk], highlightthickness=1, **kw)
def lbl_entry(parent, label, row, col=0, width=18, colspan=1,
              default="", show="", bg_key="surface"):
    tk.Label(parent, text=label.upper(), font=F(8, "bold"),
             fg=C["muted"], bg=C[bg_key]).grid(
        row=row, column=col, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))
    e = tk.Entry(parent, font=F(11), width=width, show=show,
                 relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                 insertbackground=C["text"],
                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                 highlightcolor=C["accent"])
    e.grid(row=row+1, column=col, columnspan=colspan, sticky="ew",
           pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))
    if default: e.insert(0, default)
    return e


def lbl_combo(parent, label, row, col=0, values=None, width=18, bg_key="surface"):
    tk.Label(parent, text=label.upper(), font=F(8, "bold"),
             fg=C["muted"], bg=C[bg_key]).grid(
        row=row, column=col, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))
    cb = ttk.Combobox(parent, values=values or [], width=width,
                      state="readonly", font=F(11))
    cb.grid(row=row+1, column=col, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))
    return cb


# ── Calendar popup ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DatePicker:
    """Popup calendar. Call DatePicker.pick(root, entry_widget) to attach."""

    @staticmethod
    def attach(root, entry_widget):
        """Bind a calendar icon button next to an entry widget."""
        btn = tk.Button(
            entry_widget.master, text="📅",
            font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
            bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
            cursor="hand2", activebackground=C["border"],
            command=lambda: DatePicker._open(root, entry_widget))
        return btn

    @staticmethod
    def _open(root, entry_widget):
        # Parse existing value
        try:
            current = datetime.strptime(entry_widget.get().strip(), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except Exception:
            current = date.today()

        win = tk.Toplevel(root)
        win.title("Pick date")
        win.configure(bg=C["surface"])
        win.resizable(False, False)
        win.grab_set()
        win.attributes("-topmost", True)

        # Position near the entry
        try:
            x = entry_widget.winfo_rootx()
            y = entry_widget.winfo_rooty() + entry_widget.winfo_height() + 2
            win.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
        except Exception:
            pass

        state = {"year": current.year, "month": current.month}

        def build():
            for w in win.winfo_children(): w.destroy()

            hdr = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"])
            hdr.pack(fill="x", padx=8, pady=(8, 4))

            tk.Button(hdr, text="◀", font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                      bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"], cursor="hand2",
                      activebackground=C["border"],
                      command=lambda: prev_month()).pack(side="left")

            month_name = datetime(state["year"], state["month"], 1).strftime("%B %Y")
            tk.Label(hdr, text=month_name, font=F(11, "bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left", expand=True)

            tk.Button(hdr, text="▶", font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                      bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"], cursor="hand2",
                      activebackground=C["border"],
                      command=lambda: next_month()).pack(side="right")

            grid = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"])
            grid.pack(padx=8, pady=(0, 8))

            for ci, day_name in enumerate(["Mo","Tu","We","Th","Fr","Sa","Su"]):
                tk.Label(grid, text=day_name, font=F(9, "bold"),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                         width=3, anchor="center").grid(row=0, column=ci, padx=2, pady=2)

            cal = calendar.monthcalendar(state["year"], state["month"])
            today = date.today()

            for ri, week in enumerate(cal):
                for ci, day in enumerate(week):
                    if day == 0:
                        tk.Label(grid, text="", bg=C["surface"],
                                 width=3).grid(row=ri+1, column=ci, padx=2, pady=2)
                    else:
                        d = date(state["year"], state["month"], day)
                        is_today    = (d == today)
                        is_selected = (d == current)
                        if is_selected:
                            bg, fg = C["accent"], C["bg"]
                        elif is_today:
                            bg, fg = C["info_bg"], C["info"]
                        else:
                            bg, fg = C["surface"], C["text"]
                        tk.Button(
                            grid, text=str(day), font=F(10),
                            width=3, relief="flat", bd=0,
                            bg=bg, fg=fg, cursor="hand2",
                            activebackground=C["border"],
                            command=lambda dd=d: select(dd)
                        ).grid(row=ri+1, column=ci, padx=2, pady=2)

            # Today button
            tk.Button(win, text="Today", font=F(9),
                      relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                      cursor="hand2", pady=4,
                      command=lambda: select(date.today())).pack(fill="x", padx=8, pady=(0, 8))

        def prev_month():
            if state["month"] == 1:
                state["month"] = 12; state["year"] -= 1
            else:
                state["month"] -= 1
            build()

        def next_month():
            if state["month"] == 12:
                state["month"] = 1; state["year"] += 1
            else:
                state["month"] += 1
            build()

        def select(d):
            entry_widget.delete(0, "end")
            entry_widget.insert(0, d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"))
            win.destroy()

        build()


# ── PDF export ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

try:
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.units import mm
    from reportlab.platypus import (SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer,
                                    Table, TableStyle, HRFlowable, Image as RLImage)
    from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
    from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_RIGHT, TA_CENTER, TA_LEFT
    _RL = True
except ImportError:
    _RL = False

import base64, io as _io


def _calc_invoice_totals(job: dict):
    parts = job.get("parts", [])
    svcs  = job.get("service_charges", [])
    subtotal = sum(float(p.get("invoice_price", p.get("unit_price",0))) * int(p.get("qty",1))
                   for p in parts)
    subtotal += sum(float(s.get("price",0)) for s in svcs)
    return subtotal


def generate_invoice_pdf_bytes(job: dict, booking: dict, settings: dict) -> bytes:
    """Return a properly formatted A4 PDF invoice matching professional invoice style."""
    import io
    buf = io.BytesIO()

    ws        = settings.get("workshop_name", "My Garage")
    address   = settings.get("workshop_address", "")
    logo_path = settings.get("invoice_logo", "")
    ref       = (booking or {}).get("ref", job.get("id","")[:8].upper())
    bk        = booking or {}
    prof      = job.get("invoice_profile", {})
    disc_pct  = float(prof.get("discount_pct", job.get("discount_pct", 0)))
    tax_pct   = float(settings.get("tax_percent", 18))
    vin       = prof.get("vin","")
    bank_name = prof.get("bank_name","")
    bank_acc  = prof.get("bank_account","")
    bank_ifsc = prof.get("bank_ifsc","")
    bank_branch=prof.get("bank_branch","")
    bank_sort = prof.get("bank_sort","")
    pay_terms = prof.get("payment_terms","")
    inv_notes = prof.get("notes","")
    pay_status= prof.get("payment_status", job.get("payment_status","Outstanding"))
    gstin     = settings.get("gstin","")
    garage_ph = settings.get("garage_phone","")
    garage_em = settings.get("garage_email","")

    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(buf, pagesize=A4,
                            rightMargin=18*mm, leftMargin=18*mm,
                            topMargin=18*mm, bottomMargin=18*mm)

    # ── Colour palette ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    BLACK   = colors.HexColor("#111111")
    DGREY   = colors.HexColor("#444444")
    LGREY   = colors.HexColor("#F2F2F2")
    MGREY   = colors.HexColor("#CCCCCC")
    WHITE   = colors.white

    # ── Paragraph styles ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def ps(name, size=10, bold=False, align=TA_LEFT, color=BLACK, leading=None):
        return ParagraphStyle(name,
            fontName="Helvetica-Bold" if bold else "Helvetica",
            fontSize=size, textColor=color, alignment=align,
            leading=leading or (size * 1.35), spaceAfter=0, spaceBefore=0)

    S = {
        "ws_name":   ps("wn",  12, bold=True),
        "ws_addr":   ps("wa",  9,  color=DGREY),
        "inv_title": ps("it",  28, bold=True, align=TA_RIGHT),
        "section":   ps("sec", 9,  bold=True),
        "label":     ps("lb",  8,  color=DGREY),
        "value":     ps("vl",  10),
        "value_r":   ps("vr",  10, align=TA_RIGHT),
        "th":        ps("th",  9,  bold=True),
        "th_r":      ps("thr", 9,  bold=True, align=TA_RIGHT),
        "td":        ps("td",  9),
        "td_r":      ps("tdr", 9,  align=TA_RIGHT),
        "tot_lbl":   ps("tl",  10, bold=True),
        "tot_val":   ps("tv",  10, bold=True, align=TA_RIGHT),
        "foot":      ps("ft",  8,  color=DGREY),
    }

    elems = []
    CW = 170*mm   # content width

    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SECTION 1 — Header: company left, logo right
    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    logo_cell = ""
    if logo_path and os.path.exists(logo_path):
        try:
            logo_cell = RLImage(logo_path, width=35*mm, height=35*mm, kind="proportional")
        except Exception:
            logo_cell = Paragraph(ws, S["inv_title"])
    else:
        logo_cell = Paragraph("", S["inv_title"])

    company_lines = [Paragraph(ws, S["ws_name"])]
    for line in (address or "").splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            company_lines.append(Paragraph(line.strip(), S["ws_addr"]))
    sender_email = settings.get("email_sender","")
    if sender_email:
        company_lines.append(Paragraph(sender_email, S["ws_addr"]))

    from reportlab.platypus import KeepInFrame
    company_cell = [c for c in company_lines]

    hdr = Table([[company_cell, logo_cell]], colWidths=[110*mm, 60*mm])
    hdr.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("VALIGN",  (0,0),(-1,-1), "TOP"),
        ("ALIGN",   (1,0),(1,0),   "RIGHT"),
    ]))
    elems.append(hdr)
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 10*mm))

    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SECTION 2 — Bill To (left) / Invoice details (right)
    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    bill_to = [
        Paragraph("BILL TO", S["section"]),
        Spacer(1, 2*mm),
        Paragraph(bk.get("cust_name", job.get("cust_name","")), S["value"]),
        Paragraph(bk.get("cust_phone",""), S["ws_addr"]),
        Paragraph(bk.get("cust_email",""), S["ws_addr"]),
    ]

    vehicle_date = bk.get("date","")
    mechs_str = ", ".join(job.get("mechanics",[]) or ([job["mechanic"]] if job.get("mechanic") else ["—"]))
    inv_details = [
        Paragraph("INVOICE", S["section"]),
        Spacer(1, 2*mm),
        Table([
            [Paragraph("Invoice No:", S["label"]),  Paragraph(ref, S["value_r"])],
            [Paragraph("Issue Date:", S["label"]),  Paragraph(vehicle_date, S["value_r"])],
            [Paragraph("Vehicle:",    S["label"]),  Paragraph(bk.get("plate",""), S["value_r"])],
            [Paragraph("Service:",    S["label"]),  Paragraph(bk.get("service") or bk.get("type",""), S["value_r"])],
            [Paragraph("Mechanic:",   S["label"]),  Paragraph(job.get("mechanic","—"), S["value_r"])],
        ], colWidths=[28*mm, 47*mm],
        style=TableStyle([
            ("BOTTOMPADDING",(0,0),(-1,-1), 3),
            ("TOPPADDING",   (0,0),(-1,-1), 1),
        ])),
    ]

    bill_tbl = Table([[bill_to, inv_details]], colWidths=[85*mm, 85*mm])
    bill_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("VALIGN",(0,0),(-1,-1),"TOP"),
    ]))
    elems.append(bill_tbl)
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 8*mm))

    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SECTION 3 — Items table
    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    parts = job.get("parts", [])
    svcs  = job.get("service_charges", [])
    grand = 0.0

    # Columns: #, Description, HSN, Qty, Unit Price, Disc(INR ), Taxable, Parts Total
    rows = [[
        Paragraph("#",            S["th"]),
        Paragraph("Part Name",    S["th"]),
        Paragraph("HSN/SAC",      S["th_r"]),
        Paragraph("Qty",          S["th_r"]),
        Paragraph("Unit Price",   S["th_r"]),
        Paragraph(f"Disc({disc_pct:.0f}%)", S["th_r"]),
        Paragraph("Taxable (INR )",  S["th_r"]),
        Paragraph("Parts Total",  S["th_r"]),
    ]]

    parts_subtotal = 0.0
    for ri, p in enumerate(parts):
        qty     = int(p.get("qty",1))
        price   = float(p.get("invoice_price", p.get("unit_price",0)))
        hsn     = str(p.get("hsn",""))
        disc_a  = round(price * qty * disc_pct / 100, 2)
        taxable = price * qty - disc_a
        gst_a   = round(taxable * tax_pct / 100, 2)
        part_tot= taxable + gst_a
        parts_subtotal += part_tot; grand += part_tot
        rows.append([
            Paragraph(str(ri+1),          S["td"]),
            Paragraph(p.get("name","?"),  S["td"]),
            Paragraph(hsn,                S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(str(qty),           S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(f"INR {price:,.2f}",  S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(f"INR {disc_a:,.2f}", S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(f"INR {taxable:,.2f}",S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(f"INR {part_tot:,.2f}",S["td_r"]),
        ])

    labour_total = 0.0
    for sc in svcs:
        price = float(sc.get("price",0)); labour_total += price; grand += price
        rows.append([
            Paragraph("",                              S["td"]),
            Paragraph(sc.get("name","Labour charge"),  S["td"]),
            Paragraph("",  S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph("—", S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph("—", S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph("—", S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph("—", S["td_r"]),
            Paragraph(f"INR {price:,.2f}", S["td_r"]),
        ])

    if not parts and not svcs:
        rows.append([Paragraph("No items recorded", S["td"]),"","","","","","",""])

    items_tbl = Table(rows, colWidths=[8*mm,55*mm,18*mm,12*mm,22*mm,18*mm,20*mm,20*mm], repeatRows=1)
    items_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0,0), (-1,0),  LGREY),
        ("LINEBELOW",     (0,0), (-1,0),  0.8, BLACK),
        ("FONTNAME",      (0,0), (-1,0),  "Helvetica-Bold"),
        ("FONTSIZE",      (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 4),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 4),
        ("LINEBELOW",     (0,1), (-1,-1), 0.3, MGREY),
        ("ALIGN",         (2,0), (-1,-1), "RIGHT"),
        ("VALIGN",        (0,0), (-1,-1), "MIDDLE"),
        ("BOX",           (0,0), (-1,-1), 0.8, BLACK),
    ]))
    elems.append(items_tbl)
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 4*mm))

    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SECTION 4 — Totals breakdown + bank details
    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    gst_total   = round(grand - parts_subtotal - labour_total +
                        sum(round(float(p.get("invoice_price",p.get("unit_price",0)))*
                                  int(p.get("qty",1))*disc_pct/100,2) for p in parts), 2)
    # Recompute cleanly
    parts_taxable = sum(
        float(p.get("invoice_price",p.get("unit_price",0)))*int(p.get("qty",1))
        - round(float(p.get("invoice_price",p.get("unit_price",0)))*int(p.get("qty",1))*disc_pct/100,2)
        for p in parts)
    disc_total  = sum(round(float(p.get("invoice_price",p.get("unit_price",0)))*int(p.get("qty",1))*disc_pct/100,2) for p in parts)
    gst_total2  = round(parts_taxable * tax_pct / 100, 2)
    parts_tot   = parts_taxable + gst_total2
    grand_total = parts_tot + labour_total
    rounded     = round(grand_total)
    balance     = rounded

    tot_data = [
        [Paragraph("Parts Total",   S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {parts_tot:,.2f}",   S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("Labour Total",  S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {labour_total:,.2f}", S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("GST Total",     S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {gst_total2:,.2f}",   S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("Discount Total",S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {disc_total:,.2f}",   S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("Grand Total",   S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {grand_total:,.2f}",  S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("Round off",     S["label"]), Paragraph(f"INR {rounded:,.2f}",      S["value_r"])],
        [Paragraph("Balance",       S["tot_lbl"]),Paragraph(f"INR {balance:,.2f}",     S["tot_val"])],
    ]
    tot_tbl = Table([[""  , Table(tot_data, colWidths=[30*mm,42*mm],
        style=TableStyle([
            ("LINEABOVE",     (0,-1),(-1,-1), 0.8, BLACK),
            ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 3),
            ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 2),
        ]))]],
        colWidths=[98*mm, 72*mm])
    elems.append(tot_tbl)
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 5*mm))

    # Amount in words (Indian format)
    def _amount_words(n):
        import math
        n = int(round(n))
        if n == 0: return "Zero Rupees Only"
        ones = ["","One","Two","Three","Four","Five","Six","Seven","Eight","Nine",
                "Ten","Eleven","Twelve","Thirteen","Fourteen","Fifteen","Sixteen",
                "Seventeen","Eighteen","Nineteen"]
        tens = ["","","Twenty","Thirty","Forty","Fifty","Sixty","Seventy","Eighty","Ninety"]
        def _u100(x):
            if x < 20: return ones[x]
            return tens[x//10] + (" " + ones[x%10] if x%10 else "")
        def _u1000(x):
            if x < 100: return _u100(x)
            return ones[x//100] + " Hundred" + (" " + _u100(x%100) if x%100 else "")
        parts_w = []
        cr = n // 10000000; n %= 10000000
        lk = n // 100000;   n %= 100000
        th = n // 1000;     n %= 1000
        hu = n
        if cr: parts_w.append(_u1000(cr) + " Crore")
        if lk: parts_w.append(_u1000(lk) + " Lakh")
        if th: parts_w.append(_u1000(th) + " Thousand")
        if hu: parts_w.append(_u1000(hu))
        return "INR " + " ".join(parts_w) + " Rupees Only"

    elems.append(Paragraph(f"Amount (in Words): {_amount_words(balance)}", S["foot"]))
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 6*mm))

    # Bank details
    if bank_name or bank_acc:
        elems.append(Paragraph("PAY BY BANK TRANSFER", S["section"]))
        elems.append(Spacer(1, 2*mm))
        bd_rows = []
        if bank_name:  bd_rows.append([Paragraph("Bank Name:", S["label"]),   Paragraph(bank_name,  S["value"])])
        if bank_acc:   bd_rows.append([Paragraph("Account No:",S["label"]),   Paragraph(bank_acc,   S["value"])])
        if bank_ifsc:  bd_rows.append([Paragraph("IFSC Code:", S["label"]),   Paragraph(bank_ifsc,  S["value"])])
        if bank_branch:bd_rows.append([Paragraph("Branch:",    S["label"]),   Paragraph(bank_branch,S["value"])])
        if bank_sort:  bd_rows.append([Paragraph("Sort/SWIFT:",S["label"]),   Paragraph(bank_sort,  S["value"])])
        if pay_terms:  bd_rows.append([Paragraph("Terms:",     S["label"]),   Paragraph(pay_terms,  S["value"])])
        bd_tbl = Table(bd_rows, colWidths=[30*mm, 80*mm])
        bd_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([("BOTTOMPADDING",(0,0),(-1,-1),3),("TOPPADDING",(0,0),(-1,-1),2)]))
        elems.append(bd_tbl)
        elems.append(Spacer(1, 6*mm))

    # Payment status
    pclr = {"Paid": colors.HexColor("#16a34a"),
            "Partially Paid": colors.HexColor("#d97706")}.get(pay_status, colors.HexColor("#dc2626"))
    elems.append(Paragraph(f"Payment Status: <b>{pay_status}</b>",
        ParagraphStyle("ps", parent=S["foot"], textColor=pclr)))
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 5*mm))

    # Certification
    elems.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=0.5, color=MGREY))
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 3*mm))
    cert = ("I certify that the work has been done to my satisfaction and that I have taken "
            "delivery of the vehicle in good condition, with all items / valuables and parts intact.")
    elems.append(Paragraph(cert, S["foot"]))
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 10*mm))

    if inv_notes:
        elems.append(Paragraph("TERMS", S["section"]))
        elems.append(Paragraph(inv_notes, S["foot"]))
        elems.append(Spacer(1, 5*mm))
    elems.append(Spacer(1, 10*mm))

    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # SECTION 5 — Footer
    # ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    elems.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=0.5, color=MGREY, spaceAfter=4*mm))
    elems.append(Paragraph(
        f"Thank you for choosing {ws}. Please retain this invoice for your records.",
        S["foot"]))

    doc.build(elems)
    return buf.getvalue()


def generate_invoice_text(job: dict, booking: dict, settings: dict) -> str:
    """Plain-text invoice fallback when reportlab is not installed."""
    ws  = settings.get("workshop_name", "My Garage")
    ref = (booking or {}).get("ref", job.get("id","")[:8].upper())
    bk  = booking or {}
    W   = 64
    def hr(c="─"): return c * W
    def row(lbl, val):
        gap = W - len(lbl) - len(str(val)) - 4
        return f"  {lbl}{' ' * max(1,gap)}{val}"

    lines = [
        "═" * W, f"  {ws}".center(W),
        f"  {settings.get('workshop_address','')}'.center(W)" if settings.get("workshop_address") else "",
        "  INVOICE".center(W), "═" * W, "",
        row("Invoice ref :", ref), row("Date        :", now_str()[:11]),
        row("Mechanic(s) :", ", ".join(job.get("mechanics",[]) or ([job.get("mechanic","—")] if job.get("mechanic") else ["—"]))), "",
        hr(), "  CUSTOMER & VEHICLE", hr(),
        row("Customer    :", bk.get("cust_name", job.get("cust_name",""))),
        row("Phone       :", bk.get("cust_phone","—")),
        row("Email       :", bk.get("cust_email","—")),
        row("Plate       :", job.get("plate","")),
        row("Service     :", job.get("type","")), "",
        hr(), "  ITEMISED CHARGES", hr(),
        f"  {'Item':<34} {'Qty':>4}  {'Unit':>9}  {'Total':>9}",
        "  " + "─" * (W-2),
    ]
    grand = 0.0
    for p in job.get("parts", []):
        qty   = int(p.get("qty",1))
        price = float(p.get("invoice_price", p.get("unit_price",0)))
        total = price * qty; grand += total
        lines.append(f"  {p.get('name','?')[:34]:<34} {qty:>4}  INR {price:>8.2f}  INR {total:>8.2f}")
    for sc in job.get("service_charges", []):
        price = float(sc.get("price",0)); grand += price
        lines.append(f"  {sc.get('name','Service charge')[:34]:<34} {'—':>4}  {'—':>9}  INR {price:>8.2f}")
    if not job.get("parts") and not job.get("service_charges"):
        lines.append("  No items recorded")
    lines += [
        "  " + "─" * (W-2), row("TOTAL DUE :", f"INR {grand:,.2f}"), "",
        hr("═"), f"  Thank you for choosing {ws}.",
        "  Please retain this invoice for your records.", hr("═"),
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def send_invoice_email(job: dict, booking: dict, settings: dict,
                       include_photos: bool = False):
    """Email invoice PDF + optional photos PDF as attachments."""
    email = (booking or {}).get("cust_email","")
    if not email: return
    ws  = settings.get("workshop_name","")
    ref = (booking or {}).get("ref", job.get("id","")[:8].upper())
    subject = f"Invoice [{ref}] — {ws}"

    if _RL:
        try:
            pdf_bytes = generate_invoice_pdf_bytes(job, booking, settings)
            sndr = settings.get("email_sender","").strip()
            pwd  = settings.get("email_password","").strip()
            if not sndr or not pwd: return
            from email.mime.base import MIMEBase
            from email import encoders
            msg = MIMEMultipart()
            msg["Subject"] = subject
            msg["From"]    = sndr
            msg["To"]      = email
            body_text = (f"Dear {job.get('cust_name','')},\n\n"
                         f"Please find your invoice [{ref}] attached.\n\n"
                         f"Thank you for choosing {ws}.")
            msg.attach(MIMEText(body_text, "plain", "utf-8"))
            # Attach invoice
            att = MIMEBase("application","pdf")
            att.set_payload(pdf_bytes)
            encoders.encode_base64(att)
            att.add_header("Content-Disposition","attachment",
                           filename=f"Invoice_{ref}.pdf")
            msg.attach(att)
            # Optionally attach photos PDF
            if include_photos and job.get("photos"):
                try:
                    ph_bytes = generate_photos_pdf_bytes(job, booking, settings)
                    att2 = MIMEBase("application","pdf")
                    att2.set_payload(ph_bytes)
                    encoders.encode_base64(att2)
                    att2.add_header("Content-Disposition","attachment",
                                    filename=f"Photos_{ref}.pdf")
                    msg.attach(att2)
                except Exception as pe:
                    print(f"Photos PDF failed: {pe}")
            def _send():
                try:
                    import smtplib as _sl
                    with _sl.SMTP_SSL("smtp.gmail.com",465,timeout=10) as srv:
                        srv.login(sndr, pwd)
                        srv.sendmail(sndr, [email], msg.as_string())
                    print("Invoice email sent")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Invoice email failed: {e}")
            threading.Thread(target=_send, daemon=True).start()
            return
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"PDF generation failed: {e} — falling back to text")

    body = generate_invoice_text(job, booking, settings)
    send_email_async(settings, email, subject, body,
                     callback=lambda e: print(f"Invoice email: {'sent' if not e else e}"))


def generate_photos_pdf_bytes(job: dict, booking: dict, settings: dict) -> bytes:
    """Generate a PDF of before/after photos with descriptions."""
    import io as _bio
    buf = _bio.BytesIO()
    ws  = settings.get("workshop_name","My Garage")
    ref = (booking or {}).get("ref", job.get("id","")[:8].upper())

    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(buf, pagesize=A4,
                            rightMargin=18*mm, leftMargin=18*mm,
                            topMargin=18*mm, bottomMargin=18*mm)

    BLACK = colors.HexColor("#111111")
    LGREY = colors.HexColor("#F2F2F2")
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()

    def ps(name, size=10, bold=False, align=TA_LEFT, color=BLACK):
        return ParagraphStyle(name, fontName="Helvetica-Bold" if bold else "Helvetica",
                              fontSize=size, textColor=color, alignment=align,
                              leading=size*1.4, spaceAfter=0)

    elems = []
    # Header
    elems.append(Paragraph(ws, ps("h1",16,bold=True)))
    elems.append(Paragraph(f"Job Photo Report — {ref} — {job.get('plate','')}",
                            ps("h2",11,color=colors.HexColor("#666666"))))
    elems.append(Spacer(1,6*mm))
    elems.append(HRFlowable(width="100%",thickness=1,color=BLACK,spaceAfter=4*mm))

    photos = job.get("photos",[])
    if not photos:
        elems.append(Paragraph("No photos attached to this job.",
                                ps("no",10,color=colors.HexColor("#888888"))))
    else:
        for i, ph in enumerate(photos):
            label = ph.get("label","Photo")
            desc  = ph.get("description","")
            b64   = ph.get("data","")
            elems.append(Paragraph(f"{i+1}. {label}", ps(f"lbl{i}",12,bold=True)))
            if desc:
                elems.append(Paragraph(desc, ps(f"desc{i}",10,
                    color=colors.HexColor("#444444"))))
            elems.append(Spacer(1,3*mm))
            if b64:
                try:
                    import io as _bio2
                    raw = base64.b64decode(b64)
                    img_buf = _bio2.BytesIO(raw)
                    ri = RLImage(img_buf, width=130*mm, height=90*mm, kind="proportional")
                    elems.append(ri)
                except Exception as e:
                    elems.append(Paragraph(f"[Image error: {e}]",
                                           ps(f"err{i}",9,color=colors.HexColor("#cc0000"))))
            elems.append(Spacer(1,6*mm))
            elems.append(HRFlowable(width="100%",thickness=0.3,
                                     color=colors.HexColor("#cccccc"),spaceAfter=4*mm))

    elems.append(Spacer(1,6*mm))
    elems.append(Paragraph(
        f"Generated by {ws} — {ref}",
        ps("foot",8,color=colors.HexColor("#888888"))))
    doc.build(elems)
    return buf.getvalue()


def export_job_card_pdf(job: dict, booking: dict, settings: dict, parent):
    """Save invoice as PDF (or .txt fallback)."""
    ref = (booking or {}).get("ref","")
    if _RL:
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            parent=parent, defaultextension=".pdf",
            filetypes=[("PDF","*.pdf"),("All files","*.*")],
            title="Save invoice",
            initialfile=f"Invoice_{job.get('plate','')}_{ref}.pdf")
        if not path: return
        try:
            pdf_bytes = generate_invoice_pdf_bytes(job, booking, settings)
            with open(path,"wb") as f: f.write(pdf_bytes)
            messagebox.showinfo("Invoice saved", f"PDF saved to:\n{path}", parent=parent)
            return
        except Exception as e:
            messagebox.showerror("PDF error", str(e), parent=parent); return
    # Fallback
    path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
        parent=parent, defaultextension=".txt",
        filetypes=[("Text file","*.txt"),("All files","*.*")],
        title="Save invoice",
        initialfile=f"Invoice_{job.get('plate','')}_{ref}.txt")
    if not path: return
    with open(path,"w",encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(generate_invoice_text(job, booking, settings))
    messagebox.showinfo("Invoice saved", f"Saved to:\n{path}", parent=parent)


# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class LicenseScreen(tk.Tk):
    """Screen 1 — enter license key."""

    def __init__(self, on_activated):
        apply_theme(False)
        super().__init__()
        self._on_activated = on_activated
        self.title("Garage Pro — Activation")
        self.resizable(True, True)
        self.minsize(480, 440)
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
        self.update_idletasks()
        sw = self.winfo_screenwidth(); sh = self.winfo_screenheight()
        # Size to content but cap at screen size
        w = min(520, sw); h = min(520, sh)
        self.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{(sw-w)//2}+{(sh-h)//2}")
        self._build()

    def _build(self):
        # Use a centred column that stays centred as window resizes
        self.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)

        outer = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["bg"]); outer.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        outer.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        outer.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)

        # Centred content wrapper — fixed width, vertically centred
        wrap = tk.Frame(outer, bg=C["bg"]); wrap.grid(row=0, column=0)

        # Header
        hdr = tk.Frame(wrap, bg=C["bg"]); hdr.pack(pady=(40,24))
        tk.Label(hdr, text="🏁", font=("Helvetica",42),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack()
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Garage Pro", font=F(24,"bold"),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack()
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Enter your license key to activate",
                 font=F(12), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(pady=(4,0))

        # Card
        card  = tk.Frame(wrap, bg=C["surface"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        card.pack(fill="x", padx=48, pady=(0,8))
        inner = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"], padx=32, pady=28)
        inner.pack(fill="both")

        tk.Label(inner, text="LICENSE KEY", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
        tk.Label(inner, text="Format: GP-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX",
                 font=F(9), fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,6))

        self._key_var = tk.StringVar()
        self._key_ent = tk.Entry(
            inner, textvariable=self._key_var,
            font=F(14,"bold"), relief="flat", bd=0,
            bg=C["bg"], fg=C["success"],
            insertbackground=C["text"],
            highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
            justify="center")
        self._key_ent.pack(fill="x", ipady=12, pady=(0,14))
        self._key_var.trace_add("write", self._on_key_type)
        self._key_ent.insert(0, "GP-")

        self._err_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Label(inner, textvariable=self._err_var, font=F(10),
                 fg=C["danger"], bg=C["surface"],
                 wraplength=420, justify="center").pack(pady=(0,8))

        self._btn = Btn(inner, "Verify License",
                        cmd=self._verify, style="primary")
        self._btn.pack(fill="x", ipady=6)

        # Footer
        foot = tk.Frame(wrap, bg=C["bg"]); foot.pack(pady=(8,40))
        tk.Label(foot, text="Buy at ", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        lnk = tk.Label(foot, text="garagepro.app",
                       font=F(10), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["info"], cursor="hand2")
        lnk.pack(side="left")
        lnk.bind("<Button-1>", lambda e: __import__("webbrowser").open("https://garagepro.app"))

        self._key_ent.focus_set()
        self._key_ent.icursor("end")
        self.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._verify())

    def _on_key_type(self, *_):
        raw  = self._key_var.get().upper()
        body = "".join(c for c in raw if c in LICENSE_CHARS)
        if body.upper().startswith("GP"): body = body[2:]
        blocks = [body[i:i+LICENSE_BLOCK_LEN]
                  for i in range(0, min(len(body),
                                        LICENSE_BLOCK_LEN*LICENSE_BLOCKS),
                                  LICENSE_BLOCK_LEN)]
        fmt = LICENSE_PREFIX + "-".join(blocks)
        if fmt != self._key_var.get():
            self._key_var.trace_remove("write",
                self._key_var.trace_info()[0][1])
            self._key_var.set(fmt)
            self._key_ent.icursor("end")
            self._key_var.trace_add("write", self._on_key_type)

    def _verify(self):
        key   = self._key_var.get().strip()
        parts = key.split("-")
        if len(parts) != 5 or parts[0] != "GP" or                 not all(len(p) == 4 for p in parts[1:]):
            self._err_var.set("Invalid format — should be GP-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX")
            return
        self._btn.config(text="Checking… (up to 10s)", state="disabled")
        self._key_ent.config(state="disabled")
        self._err_var.set("")

        def _do():
            sub = _lc.activate(key)
            self.after(0, lambda: self._on_result(sub))

        threading.Thread(target=_do, daemon=True).start()

    def _on_result(self, sub: SubscriptionStatus):
        self._btn.config(text="Verify License", state="normal")
        self._key_ent.config(state="normal")

        if sub.active:
            # Show confirmation screen before saving
            self.withdraw()
            ConfirmScreen(sub, on_confirmed=self._confirmed,
                          on_wrong=self._wrong_key)
            return

        errors = {
            "not_found": "License key not recognised. Check the key or contact support.",
            "revoked":   "This license has been revoked. Contact support@garagepro.app",
            "expired":   (f"This subscription expired"
                          + (f" on {sub.expiry.strftime('%d %b %Y')}" if sub.expiry else "")
                          + ". Renew at garagepro.app/account"),
            "offline":   "Couldn't reach the verification server. Check your connection.",
        }
        self._err_var.set(errors.get(sub.status, sub.reason or "Activation failed."))

    def _confirmed(self, sub: SubscriptionStatus):
        """Called when user clicks 'Yes, that's me' on confirmation screen."""
        _lc.confirm(sub)
        LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub)
        self.destroy()
        self._on_activated()

    def _wrong_key(self):
        """Called when user clicks 'Wrong key' — go back to key entry."""
        self.deiconify()
        self._key_var.set("GP-")
        self._key_ent.icursor("end")
        self._err_var.set("")


class ConfirmScreen(tk.Toplevel):
    """Screen 2 — show license details and ask user to confirm."""

    def __init__(self, sub: SubscriptionStatus,
                 on_confirmed, on_wrong):
        super().__init__()
        self._sub          = sub
        self._on_confirmed = on_confirmed
        self._on_wrong     = on_wrong
        self.title("Confirm your license")
        self.resizable(True, True)
        self.minsize(500, 420)
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
        self.update_idletasks()
        sw = self.winfo_screenwidth(); sh = self.winfo_screenheight()
        w = min(560, sw); h = min(560, sh)
        self.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{(sw-w)//2}+{(sh-h)//2}")
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._wrong)
        self._build()
        self.grab_set()
        self.focus_force()

    def _build(self):
        # Grid: header row, scrollable middle, sticky footer buttons
        self.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.rowconfigure(1, weight=1)   # middle row expands

        # ── Header (fixed) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["bg"]); hdr.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", pady=(24,0))
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Is this your license?",
                 font=F(18,"bold"), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack()
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Check the details below before unlocking the app.",
                 font=F(10), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(pady=(4,0))

        # ── Scrollable middle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        mid = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["bg"]); mid.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="nsew", padx=36, pady=12)
        mid.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        sub = self._sub

        # Trial pill
        if sub.is_trial:
            pill = tk.Frame(mid, bg=C["warning_bg"],
                            highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["warning"])
            pill.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,8))
            days_txt = f"{sub.trial_days_left} days" if sub.trial_days_left is not None else ""
            tk.Label(pill,
                     text=f"  🕐  FREE TRIAL{(' · ' + days_txt + ' left') if days_txt else ''}  ",
                     font=F(10,"bold"), bg=C["warning_bg"],
                     fg=C["warning"]).pack(pady=5)

        # Details card
        card  = tk.Frame(mid, bg=C["surface"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        card.pack(fill="x")
        inner = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"], padx=24, pady=20)
        inner.pack(fill="both")

        def row(label, value, val_fg=None, val_font=None):
            r = tk.Frame(inner, bg=C["surface"]); r.pack(fill="x", pady=4)
            tk.Label(r, text=label, font=F(9,"bold"), bg=C["surface"],
                     fg=C["muted"], width=14, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
            tk.Label(r, text=str(value or "—"),
                     font=val_font or F(11),
                     bg=C["surface"],
                     fg=val_fg or C["text"]).pack(side="left")

        row("License key", sub.license_key,
            val_fg=C["info"], val_font=("Courier", 11, "bold"))
        if sub.name:
            row("Name",    sub.name)
        if sub.garage_name:
            row("Garage",  sub.garage_name,
                val_fg=C["success"], val_font=F(12,"bold"))
        row("Product", sub.plan_name or sub.plan or "—")
        if sub.indefinite:
            row("Expires", "Never expires", val_fg=C["success"])
        elif sub.is_trial and sub.expiry:
            row("Trial ends", sub.expiry.strftime("%d %b %Y"), val_fg=C["warning"])
        elif sub.expiry:
            row("Renews on", sub.expiry.strftime("%d %b %Y"))
            row("Renewal",   (sub.billing_cycle or "monthly").capitalize())

        tk.Frame(inner, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(12,0))

        # Trial note
        if sub.is_trial and sub.trial_days:
            tk.Label(mid,
                     text=(f"Your trial gives you full access for {sub.trial_days} days. "
                           f"After that the app locks — subscribe any time at "
                           f"garagepro.app/account to keep your data."),
                     font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"],
                     wraplength=460, justify="center").pack(pady=(10,0))

        # ── Sticky footer buttons (always visible) ────────────────────────────
        foot = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["bg"])
        foot.grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=36, pady=(0,24))
        foot.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        foot.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        Btn(foot, "✓  Yes, that's me",
            cmd=self._confirm, style="success").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=(0,8), ipady=4)
        Btn(foot, "✕  Wrong key",
            cmd=self._wrong,   style="danger").grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", ipady=4)

    def _confirm(self):
        cb = self._on_confirmed; sub = self._sub
        self.destroy()
        cb(sub)

    def _wrong(self):
        cb = self._on_wrong
        self.destroy()
        cb()
