class GarageApp(tk.Tk):

    def __init__(self):
        # Load settings first so we know the theme before window is created
        _settings_pre = load_json(SETTINGS_FILE, {})
        apply_theme(_settings_pre.get("dark_mode", False))

        super().__init__()
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])   # set bg immediately after creation

        # ── Windows: DPI awareness + taskbar icon ────────────────────────────
        if _OS == "Windows":
            try:
                from ctypes import windll
                windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(1)
            except Exception:
                pass
            # Set window icon — shown in taskbar and title bar
            try:
                _base = (os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
                         if getattr(sys, "frozen", False)
                         else os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
                _ico  = os.path.join(_base, "icon.ico")
                if os.path.exists(_ico):
                    self.iconbitmap(_ico)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Load settings immediately (local file — instant)
        self.settings = load_settings()
        apply_theme(self.settings.get("dark_mode", False))
        # Init Firebase in background so UI shows instantly
        threading.Thread(target=_init_firebase, daemon=True).start()

        # Use garage name from license cache if available, else workshop settings
        _brand = _lc.status().app_brand
        _ws    = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "")
        self.title((_brand if _brand != "Garage Pro" else _ws or "Garage Pro") + f" — Garage Pro v{APP_VERSION}")
        self._brand = _brand   # stored for header refresh
        if _OS == "Darwin":
            self.geometry("1440x880"); self.minsize(1100, 700)
        else:
            self.geometry("1280x780"); self.minsize(1000, 640)
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._quit)

        style = ttk.Style(self)
        try:
            style.theme_use("clam")
        except Exception:
            pass
        self._apply_ttk_styles(style)

        # ── Mac: force proper window rendering ────────────────────────────────
        if _OS == "Darwin":
            self.update_idletasks()
            self.update()

        self.bays:      list = []
        self.bookings:  list = []
        self.customers: list = []
        self.inventory: list = []
        self.mechanics: list = []
        self.jobs:      list = []
        self.queue:     list = []   # waiting queue
        # Customer search state
        self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = True
        self._nb_selected_cust_idx   = 0
        self._nb_cust_popup          = None

        self._load_all()
        self._maybe_auto_backup()
        self._check_low_stock_email()
        self.after(3000, self._send_morning_briefing)
        # Load plan limits from cached license immediately (offline-safe)
        _load_limits_from_cache()
        # Register limits listener to refresh UI on plan change
        LIMITS.add_listener(lambda: self.after(0, self._on_limits_changed))
        # Check license online in background right away (non-blocking)
        self.after(1500, self._background_license_check)
        # Then re-check every 30 min
        self.after(LICENSE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS, self._start_license_checker)
        # Show trial banner if applicable
        self.after(500, self._refresh_trial_banner)
        # Mac: force complete redraw after everything is built
        if _OS == "Darwin":
            def _mac_redraw():
                self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
                self.update_idletasks()
                self.update()
                self.lift()
                self.focus_force()
                # Flash topmost to force compositing
                self.attributes("-topmost", True)
                self.after(100, lambda: self.attributes("-topmost", False))
            self.after(200, _mac_redraw)
        if not _PIL:
            self.after(2000, lambda: self._toast(
                "Pillow not installed — photos disabled. Run: pip install Pillow",
                "warning"))
        self._build_ui()
        self.refresh_all_tabs()   # full refresh on startup

    # ── Persistence ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _load_all(self):
        """
        Two-phase load:
        Phase 1 (instant, on main thread): load from local JSON files.
        Phase 2 (async, 2s later): pull from Firebase if connected, merge, refresh.
        """
        bay_defs  = self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS)
        # Phase 1 — local JSON (instant)
        saved_bays = load_json(BAYS_FILE, None)
        self.bays      = saved_bays if saved_bays else make_bays(bay_defs)
        self.bookings  = load_json(BOOKINGS_FILE,  [])
        self.customers = load_json(CUSTOMERS_FILE, [])
        self.inventory = load_json(INVENTORY_FILE, [])
        self.mechanics = load_json(MECHANICS_FILE, [])
        self.jobs      = load_json(JOBS_FILE,      [])
        self.queue     = load_json(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "queue.json"), [])
        self.suppliers = load_json(SUPPLIERS_FILE, [])

        # Phase 2 — pull from Firebase in background (non-blocking)
        self.after(2000, self._pull_from_firebase)

    def _pull_from_firebase(self):
        """Background Firebase sync — pull fresh data then refresh UI."""
        if not firebase_connected(): 
            self.after(0, self._sync_pending)
            return
        def _do():
            bay_defs  = self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS)
            fb_bays  = _fb_load("bays",      None)
            fb_bk    = _fb_load("bookings",  None)
            fb_cust  = _fb_load("customers", None)
            fb_inv   = _fb_load("inventory", None)
            fb_mech  = _fb_load("mechanics", None)
            fb_jobs  = _fb_load("jobs",      None)
            fb_queue = _fb_load("queue",     None)
            def _apply():
                changed = False
                if fb_bays  is not None: self.bays      = fb_bays;  save_json(BAYS_FILE,      fb_bays);  changed=True
                if fb_bk    is not None: self.bookings  = fb_bk;    save_json(BOOKINGS_FILE,  fb_bk);    changed=True
                if fb_cust  is not None: self.customers = fb_cust;  save_json(CUSTOMERS_FILE, fb_cust);  changed=True
                if fb_inv   is not None: self.inventory = fb_inv;   save_json(INVENTORY_FILE, fb_inv);   changed=True
                if fb_mech  is not None: self.mechanics = fb_mech;  save_json(MECHANICS_FILE, fb_mech);  changed=True
                if fb_jobs  is not None: self.jobs      = fb_jobs;  save_json(JOBS_FILE,      fb_jobs);  changed=True
                if fb_queue is not None: self.queue     = fb_queue; save_json(os.path.join(DATA_DIR,"queue.json"), fb_queue); changed=True
                if changed: self.refresh_all_tabs()
                self._sync_pending()
            self.after(0, _apply)
        threading.Thread(target=_do, daemon=True).start()

    def _save(self):
        """Save all data. Local write is immediate; Firebase push is debounced 800ms."""
        # Local JSON — always fast, synchronous
        save_json(BAYS_FILE,      self.bays)
        save_json(BOOKINGS_FILE,  self.bookings)
        save_json(CUSTOMERS_FILE, self.customers)
        save_json(INVENTORY_FILE, self.inventory)
        save_json(MECHANICS_FILE, self.mechanics)
        save_json(JOBS_FILE,      self.jobs)
        save_json(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "queue.json"), self.queue)
        save_json(SUPPLIERS_FILE, self.suppliers)
        # Mark pending in case Firebase is offline
        for col in ["bays","bookings","customers","inventory","mechanics","jobs","queue"]:
            _mark_pending(col)
        # Debounced Firebase push — cancels previous if called again within 800ms
        if hasattr(self, "_save_after_id") and self._save_after_id:
            try: self.after_cancel(self._save_after_id)
            except Exception: pass
        self._save_after_id = self.after(800, self._push_to_firebase)

    def _push_to_firebase(self):
        """Push all data to Firebase (runs after debounce delay)."""
        self._save_after_id = None
        if not firebase_connected(): return
        cols = {
            "bays":      self.bays,
            "bookings":  self.bookings,
            "customers": self.customers,
            "inventory": self.inventory,
            "mechanics": self.mechanics,
            "jobs":      self.jobs,
            "queue":     self.queue,
        }
        def _do():
            for col, data in cols.items():
                try:
                    _fb_save(col, data)
                    _clear_pending(col)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Firebase push {col}: {e}")
        threading.Thread(target=_do, daemon=True).start()

    def _send_morning_briefing(self):
        """Send daily morning briefing to manager email (once per day)."""
        manager = self.settings.get("manager_email","").strip()
        if not manager: return
        last = self.settings.get("morning_briefing_sent","")
        today = date.today().strftime("%d %b %Y")
        if last == today: return
        self.settings["morning_briefing_sent"] = today
        save_settings(self.settings)
        ws        = self.settings.get("workshop_name","")
        today_bks = [b for b in self.bookings if b.get("date")==today_str() and b["status"]=="Booked"]
        in_shop   = [b for b in self.bookings if b["status"]=="Arrived"]
        lines = [
            f"Good morning — Daily briefing for {ws}",
            f"Date: {today}", "",
            f"BOOKINGS TODAY: {len(today_bks)}",
        ] + [f"  {b.get('time','?')}  {b['plate']}  {b['cust_name']}" for b in sorted(today_bks, key=lambda b: b.get("time",""))]
        lines += ["", f"CARS IN WORKSHOP: {len(in_shop)}"]
        lines += [f"  Bay {b.get('bay_num','?')}  {b['plate']}  {b['cust_name']}" for b in in_shop]
        active_jobs = [j for j in self.jobs if j["status"] not in ("Delivered","Cancelled")]
        lines += ["", f"ACTIVE JOBS: {len(active_jobs)}"]
        lines += [f"  {j['plate']}  {j['cust_name']}  [{j['status']}]" for j in active_jobs]
        body = "\n".join(lines)
        subject = f"Daily briefing — {ws} — {today}"
        send_email_async(self.settings, manager, subject, body,
                         callback=lambda e: print(f"Morning briefing: {'sent' if not e else e}"))

    def _background_license_check(self):
        """Run once at startup — checks online if connected, silent on failure."""
        def _check():
            try:
                sub = _lc.verify(force_online=True)
                if sub.status == "revoked":
                    self.after(0, self._handle_revoked)
                elif sub.status == "expired":
                    self.after(0, lambda: self._toast(
                        "Subscription expired. Renew at garagepro.app/account", "warning"))
                elif sub.status == "grace_expired":
                    self.after(0, lambda: self._toast(
                        f"Cannot verify license — offline for too long. "
                        f"Please connect to the internet.", "warning"))
                elif sub.active:
                    self.after(0, lambda: LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub))
            except Exception:
                pass   # offline — silent, cached limits already loaded
        threading.Thread(target=_check, daemon=True).start()

    def _refresh_brand(self):
        """Update title bar when license/garage name changes."""
        sub    = _lc.status()
        brand  = sub.app_brand
        ws     = self.settings.get("workshop_name","")
        title  = (brand if brand != "Garage Pro" else ws or "Garage Pro") + " — Garage Pro"
        self.title(title)
        self._brand = brand
        # Update dashboard header label if it exists
        if hasattr(self, "_dash_brand_lbl"):
            self._dash_brand_lbl.config(text=brand)

    def _dismiss_trial_banner(self):
        self._trial_banner_dismissed = True
        self._trial_banner.pack_forget()

    def _refresh_trial_banner(self):
        """Show/update the trial banner based on current subscription."""
        if self._trial_banner_dismissed: return
        sub = _lc.status()
        if not sub.active or not sub.is_trial:
            self._trial_banner.pack_forget()
            return
        days = sub.trial_days_left or 0
        plan = sub.plan_name or sub.plan or "Garage Pro"
        exp  = sub.expiry.strftime("%d %b %Y") if sub.expiry else "soon"
        if days <= 2:
            msg  = f"⚠  Your trial ends in {days} day{'s' if days!=1 else ''}. Subscribe now to keep using Garage Pro."
            bg   = C["danger_bg"]; fg = C["danger"]
        else:
            msg  = f"You're on a free trial of {plan}. Subscribe before {exp} to keep your data."
            bg   = C["warning_bg"]; fg = C["warning"]
        self._trial_banner_lbl.config(text=msg, bg=bg, fg=fg)
        self._trial_banner.config(bg=bg, highlightbackground=fg)
        for w in self._trial_banner.winfo_children():
            if isinstance(w, tk.Frame):
                w.config(bg=bg)
        if not self._trial_banner_shown:
            # Insert banner between tab bar and content area
            self._trial_banner.pack(fill="x", before=self.content)
            self._trial_banner_shown = True

    def _on_limits_changed(self):
        """Called when plan limits update — refresh UI and show warnings."""
        # Refresh settings tab if open
        self._refresh_settings()
        # Check for downgrade warnings
        n_bays    = len(self.bays)
        n_mechs   = len(self.mechanics)
        active_bk = len([b for b in self.bookings
                         if b.get("status") in ("Booked","Arrived")])
        warnings  = []
        if not LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_bays) and n_bays > LIMITS.max_bays:
            warnings.append(
                f"Bays: {n_bays} configured but plan allows {LIMITS.max_bays}. "
                f"Please remove {n_bays - LIMITS.max_bays} bay(s).")
        if not LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_workers) and n_mechs > LIMITS.max_workers:
            warnings.append(
                f"Workers: {n_mechs} configured but plan allows {LIMITS.max_workers}. "
                f"Please remove {n_mechs - LIMITS.max_workers} worker(s).")
        if warnings:
            self._toast("Plan downgrade — " + " | ".join(warnings), "warning")
        # Refresh relevant tabs
        self.refresh_all()

    def _start_license_checker(self):
        """Re-check license every 30 min using LicenseClient."""
        def _check():
            sub = _lc.verify(force_online=True)
            if sub.status == "revoked":
                self.after(0, self._handle_revoked)
            elif sub.active:
                self.after(0, lambda: (LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub),
                                       self._refresh_brand(),
                                       self._refresh_trial_banner()))
            elif sub.status == "grace_expired":
                self.after(0, lambda: self._toast(
                    "Cannot verify subscription — please connect to the internet.", "warning"))
        threading.Thread(target=_check, daemon=True).start()
        self.after(LICENSE_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS, self._start_license_checker)

    def _handle_revoked(self):
        """Lock the app and show revoked screen."""
        self.withdraw()
        key = _lc.status().license_key or ""
        _lc.clear()
        def _on_new_key():
            _run_license_flow(_run_main_app)
        rev = RevokedScreen(key, _on_new_key)
        rev.mainloop()

    def _check_low_stock_email(self):
        """Send one daily email listing all items below low stock threshold."""
        low_items = [i for i in self.inventory if i["qty"] <= i.get("low_stock", 5)]
        if not low_items: return
        # Only send once per day — check last sent date in settings
        last = self.settings.get("low_stock_email_sent", "")
        today = date.today().strftime("%d %b %Y")
        if last == today: return
        email = self.settings.get("email_sender", "").strip()
        if not email: return  # no email configured
        self.settings["low_stock_email_sent"] = today
        save_settings(self.settings)
        ws = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "")
        lines = [f"Low stock alert — {ws}", f"Date: {now_str()}", "",
                 f"The following items need to be ordered:", ""]
        for item in sorted(low_items, key=lambda i: i.get("category","")):
            order_qty = max(item.get("low_stock",5)*2 - item["qty"], 1)
            loc = f" [{item['location']}]" if item.get("location") else ""
            lines.append(f"  • {item['name']}{loc}  —  In stock: {item['qty']}  |  Order: {order_qty}")
        lines += ["", "This email is sent automatically once per day when stock is low.", ws]
        body = "\n".join(lines)
        subject = f"⚠ Low stock alert — {len(low_items)} item(s) need ordering — {ws}"
        send_email_async(self.settings, email, subject, body,
                         callback=lambda e: print(f"Low stock email: {'sent' if not e else e}"))

    def _quit(self):
        self._save(); self.destroy()

    def _maybe_auto_backup(self):
        last  = self.settings.get("last_backup", "")
        today = date.today().strftime("%d %b %Y")
        if not last or today not in last:
            do_backup(self.settings)

    @property
    def all_bay_types(self) -> list:
        return [t for t, _ in self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS)]

    # ── Theme ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _toggle_dark(self):
        dark = not self.settings.get("dark_mode", False)
        self.settings["dark_mode"] = dark
        save_settings(self.settings)
        apply_theme(dark)
        for w in self.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        self._build_ui()
        # Re-apply ttk styles after rebuild since theme was reset
        self._apply_ttk_styles()
        self.refresh_all()

    # ── Shell ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.configure(bg=C["bg"])
        bar = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["surface"],
                       highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        bar.pack(fill="x", side="top")

        tk.Label(bar, text="⚙  " + self.settings.get("workshop_name", "My Garage"),
                 font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"], padx=16, pady=10).pack(side="left")
        tk.Frame(bar, width=1, bg=C["border"]).pack(side="left", fill="y", pady=6)

        tab_bar = tk.Frame(bar, bg=C["surface"])
        tab_bar.pack(side="left", fill="y")

        right = tk.Frame(bar, bg=C["surface"])
        right.pack(side="right", padx=8)

        dark = self.settings.get("dark_mode", False)
        tk.Button(right, text="☀" if dark else "🌙",
                  font=F(14), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                  relief="flat", bd=0, cursor="hand2", padx=8,
                  activebackground=C["border"],
                  command=self._toggle_dark).pack(side="right", pady=6)

        cloud_txt = "☁ Cloud sync ON" if firebase_connected() else "⚠ Offline"
        cloud_fg  = C["success"]       if firebase_connected() else C["warning"]
        tk.Label(right, text=cloud_txt, font=F(9),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=cloud_fg, padx=8).pack(side="right", pady=6)

        # Trial banner sits between tab bar and content
        self._trial_banner          = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["warning_bg"],
                                               highlightthickness=1,
                                               highlightbackground=C["warning"])
        self._trial_banner_shown    = False
        self._trial_banner_dismissed= False
        _tb_inner = tk.Frame(self._trial_banner, bg=C["warning_bg"])
        _tb_inner.pack(fill="x", padx=14, pady=6)
        self._trial_banner_lbl = tk.Label(_tb_inner, text="",
                                          font=F(10), bg=C["warning_bg"],
                                          fg=C["warning"])
        self._trial_banner_lbl.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        import webbrowser as _wb_t
        Btn(_tb_inner, "Subscribe now",
            cmd=lambda: _wb_t.open("https://garagepro.app/account"),
            style="warning", small=True).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        Btn(_tb_inner, "✕",
            cmd=self._dismiss_trial_banner,
            style="default", small=True).pack(side="right")

        self.content  = tk.Frame(self, bg=C["bg"])
        self.content.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.panels   = {}
        self.tab_btns = {}

        # ── Tab order: Schedule between New Booking and Job Cards ──
        tabs = [
            ("dashboard",   "🏠  Dashboard"),
            ("new_booking", "＋  New Booking"),
            ("bookings",    "📋  Bookings"),
            ("schedule",    "📅  Schedule"),
            ("checkin",     "🚗  Check-in"),
            ("jobs",        "🔧  Job Cards"),
            ("queue",       "⏳  Queue"),
            ("customers",   "👥  Customers"),
            ("mechanics",   "👷  Workers"),
            ("bays",        "🏗  Bays"),
            ("inventory",   "📦  Inventory"),
            ("reports",     "📊  Reports"),
            ("settings",    "⚙  Settings"),
        ]
        for key, label in tabs:
            btn = tk.Button(
                tab_bar, text=label,
                font=F(9), relief="flat", bd=0,
                bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                padx=9, pady=10, cursor="hand2",
                activebackground=C["tab_sel"],
                command=lambda k=key: self.show_tab(k))
            btn.pack(side="left")
            self.tab_btns[key] = btn
            self.panels[key]   = tk.Frame(self.content, bg=C["bg"])

        self._build_dashboard()
        self._build_new_booking()
        self._build_schedule()
        self._build_bookings()
        self._build_checkin()
        self._build_queue()
        self._build_jobs()
        self._build_customers()
        self._build_mechanics()
        self._build_bays()
        self._build_inventory()
        self._build_reports()
        self._build_settings()
        self.show_tab("dashboard")

    def _apply_ttk_styles(self, style=None):
        """Apply consistent colours to all ttk widgets regardless of OS theme."""
        if style is None:
            style = ttk.Style(self)
        bg   = C["bg"]
        surf = C["surface"]
        txt  = C["text"]
        muted= C["muted"]
        bdr  = C["border"]
        sel  = C["info_bg"]
        sel_fg = C["info"]

        # Treeview
        style.configure("Treeview",
            background=surf,
            foreground=txt,
            fieldbackground=surf,
            bordercolor=bdr,
            borderwidth=0,
            rowheight=28,
            font=F(10))
        style.configure("Treeview.Heading",
            background=bg,
            foreground=muted,
            borderwidth=1,
            relief="flat",
            font=F(9, "bold"))
        style.map("Treeview",
            background=[("selected", C["info_bg"])],
            foreground=[("selected", C["info"])])
        style.map("Treeview.Heading",
            background=[("active", bdr)])

        # Combobox
        style.configure("TCombobox",
            fieldbackground=bg,
            background=bg,
            foreground=txt,
            bordercolor=bdr,
            arrowcolor=txt,
            selectbackground=C["info_bg"],
            selectforeground=C["info"])
        style.map("TCombobox",
            fieldbackground=[("readonly", bg)],
            background=[("readonly", bg)],
            foreground=[("readonly", txt)],
            selectbackground=[("readonly", C["info_bg"])],
            selectforeground=[("readonly", C["info"])])

        # Scrollbar
        style.configure("TScrollbar",
            background=bdr,
            troughcolor=bg,
            bordercolor=bdr,
            arrowcolor=muted,
            relief="flat")
        style.map("TScrollbar",
            background=[("active", muted)])

        # Entry
        self.option_add("*Entry.relief", "flat")
        self.option_add("*Entry.background", bg)
        self.option_add("*Entry.foreground", txt)
        self.option_add("*Entry.insertBackground", txt)
        self.option_add("*Entry.selectBackground", C["info_bg"])
        self.option_add("*Entry.selectForeground", C["info"])

        # Listbox (used by Combobox dropdown popup)
        self.option_add("*Listbox.background", surf)
        self.option_add("*Listbox.foreground", txt)
        self.option_add("*Listbox.selectBackground", C["info_bg"])
        self.option_add("*Listbox.selectForeground", C["info"])
        self.option_add("*Listbox.borderWidth", 1)
        self.option_add("*Listbox.relief", "flat")
        self.option_add("*Listbox.font", F(10))

        # Text widget
        self.option_add("*Text.background", bg)
        self.option_add("*Text.foreground", txt)
        self.option_add("*Text.insertBackground", txt)
        self.option_add("*Text.selectBackground", C["info_bg"])
        self.option_add("*Text.selectForeground", C["info"])
        self.option_add("*Text.relief", "flat")

        # General
        self.option_add("*Background", bg)
        self.option_add("*Foreground", txt)
        self.option_add("*selectBackground", C["info_bg"])
        self.option_add("*selectForeground", C["info"])
        self.option_add("*activeBackground", C["border"])
        self.option_add("*activeForeground", txt)
        self.option_add("*disabledBackground", surf)
        self.option_add("*disabledForeground", muted)

    def show_tab(self, key):
        for p in self.panels.values(): p.pack_forget()
        for k, b in self.tab_btns.items():
            b.configure(fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"], font=F(9))
        self.panels[key].pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.tab_btns[key].configure(fg=C["text"], bg=C["tab_sel"], font=F(9, "bold"))
        # Track active tab — refresh_all uses this to only refresh visible tab
        self._active_tab = key
        self.refresh_all()

    def refresh_all(self):
        """Refresh dashboard always + the currently visible tab."""
        self._refresh_dashboard()
        # Always refresh these lightweight ones
        self._refresh_new_booking()
        self._refresh_checkin()
        self._refresh_queue()
        # Only refresh the currently active tab (not all 13)
        active = getattr(self, "_active_tab", "dashboard")
        _tab_refresh = {
            "bookings":  self._refresh_bookings,
            "schedule":  self._refresh_schedule,
            "jobs":      self._refresh_jobs,
            "customers": self._refresh_customers,
            "mechanics": self._refresh_mechanics,
            "bays":      self._refresh_bays,
            "inventory": self._refresh_inventory,
            "reports":   self._refresh_reports,
        }
        fn = _tab_refresh.get(active)
        if fn: fn()

    def refresh_tab(self, tab_key: str):
        """Force-refresh a specific tab by key."""
        _map = {
            "dashboard":  self._refresh_dashboard,
            "bookings":   self._refresh_bookings,
            "schedule":   self._refresh_schedule,
            "jobs":       self._refresh_jobs,
            "customers":  self._refresh_customers,
            "mechanics":  self._refresh_mechanics,
            "bays":       self._refresh_bays,
            "inventory":  self._refresh_inventory,
            "reports":    self._refresh_reports,
            "checkin":    self._refresh_checkin,
            "queue":      self._refresh_queue,
        }
        fn = _map.get(tab_key)
        if fn: fn()

    def refresh_all_tabs(self):
        """Force-refresh every tab — use sparingly."""
        self._refresh_dashboard()
        self._refresh_new_booking()
        self._refresh_schedule()
        self._refresh_bookings()
        self._refresh_checkin()
        self._refresh_queue()
        self._refresh_jobs()
        self._refresh_customers()
        self._refresh_mechanics()
        self._refresh_bays()
        self._refresh_inventory()
        self._refresh_reports()
        if hasattr(self, "_settings_built"):
            self._refresh_settings()

    # ── Layout helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _card(self, parent, padx=16, pady=12):
        outer = tk.Frame(parent, bg=C["border"])
        outer.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(6, 0))
        inner = tk.Frame(outer, bg=C["surface"])
        inner.pack(fill="x", padx=1, pady=1)
        inner.configure(padx=padx, pady=pady)
        return inner

    def _section(self, parent, title):
        row = tk.Frame(parent, bg=C["bg"])
        row.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(14, 2))
        tk.Label(row, text=title, font=F(11, "bold"),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
        tk.Frame(row, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(
            side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(10, 0), pady=5)

    def _toast(self, msg, kind="success"):
        colours = {"success": ("success_bg","success"), "error": ("danger_bg","danger"),
                   "info": ("info_bg","info"), "warning": ("warning_bg","warning")}
        durations = {"success": 2500, "error": 5000, "info": 3000, "warning": 4000}
        bk, fk = colours.get(kind, ("info_bg","info"))
        pop = tk.Toplevel(self)
        pop.overrideredirect(True); pop.attributes("-topmost", True)
        tk.Label(pop, text=msg, bg=C[bk], fg=C[fk],
                 font=F(10), padx=18, pady=10,
                 relief="flat", wraplength=500).pack()
        x = self.winfo_x() + self.winfo_width()  // 2 - 250
        y = self.winfo_y() + 38
        pop.geometry(f"+{x}+{y}")
        pop.after(4000, pop.destroy)

    def _modal(self, title, w=520, h=420):
        win = tk.Toplevel(self)
        win.title(title); win.configure(bg=C["surface"]); win.grab_set()
        sx = self.winfo_x() + (self.winfo_width()  - w) // 2
        sy = self.winfo_y() + (self.winfo_height() - h) // 2
        win.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{sx}+{sy}")
        return win

    def _stat_card(self, parent, label, value, color_key="text", click_cmd=None):
        f = tk.Frame(parent, bg=C["surface"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        f.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(0, 8))
        kw = dict(cursor="hand2") if click_cmd else {}
        tk.Label(f, text=str(value), font=F(28, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C[color_key], pady=8, **kw).pack()
        tk.Label(f, text=label, font=F(9),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"], pady=4).pack()
        if click_cmd:
            f.bind("<Button-1>", lambda e: click_cmd())

    # ── Date entry with calendar button ───────────────────────────────────────

    def _date_field(self, parent, row, col, label="Date", default=None):
        """Returns a date entry widget.
        Uses tkcalendar DateEntry (proper dropdown) if installed,
        falls back to plain Entry + popup button otherwise.
        The returned object always has a .get() method returning 'YYYY-MM-DD'."""
        bg_key = "surface"
        tk.Label(parent, text=label.upper(), font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C[bg_key]).grid(
            row=row, column=col, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))

        date_val = default or today_str()

        if _TKCAL:
            try:
                d_obj = datetime.strptime(date_val, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
            except Exception:
                d_obj = date.today()

            entry = DateEntry(
                parent,
                font=F(11),
                date_pattern="yyyy-mm-dd",
                year=d_obj.year, month=d_obj.month, day=d_obj.day,
                background=C["info"],
                foreground=C["bg"],
                headersbackground=C["surface"],
                headersforeground=C["text"],
                selectbackground=C["info"],
                selectforeground=C["bg"],
                normalbackground=C["bg"],
                normalforeground=C["text"],
                weekendbackground=C["bg"],
                weekendforeground=C["text"],
                othermonthbackground=C["surface"],
                othermonthforeground=C["muted"],
                bordercolor=C["border"],
                relief="flat",
                borderwidth=1,
                state="readonly",
                showweeknumbers=False,
                width=14,
            )
            entry.grid(row=row+1, column=col, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))

            # THE FIX: tkcalendar's get_date() calls self.get() internally.
            # Overriding .get on the instance causes infinite recursion because
            # get_date() → validate_date() → parse_date(self.get()) → get_date() → ...
            #
            # Solution: read entry._date directly — it's the actual date object
            # tkcalendar stores internally, set when user picks a date.
            # This completely bypasses the get/validate chain.
            def safe_get():
                try:
                    return entry._date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                except Exception:
                    return date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            entry.get = safe_get
            return entry
        else:
            # Fallback: plain entry + popup button
            holder = tk.Frame(parent, bg=C[bg_key])
            holder.grid(row=row+1, column=col, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))
            holder.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
            e = tk.Entry(holder, font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
            e.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
            e.insert(0, date_val)
            tk.Button(holder, text="📅", font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                      bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], cursor="hand2",
                      activebackground=C["border"],
                      command=lambda: DatePicker._open(self, e)
                      ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(2, 0))
            return e

    # ── Time dropdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _time_field(self, parent, row, col, label="Time", default="09:00"):
        bg_key = "surface"
        tk.Label(parent, text=label.upper(), font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C[bg_key]).grid(
            row=row, column=col, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))
        cb = ttk.Combobox(parent, values=TIME_OPTIONS, width=8,
                          font=F(11), state="readonly")
        cb.grid(row=row+1, column=col, sticky="ew", pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))
        cb.set(default if default in TIME_OPTIONS else "09:00")
        return cb

    # =========================================================================
    # DASHBOARD
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_dashboard(self):
        panel = self.panels["dashboard"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._dash_sf = sf

    def _refresh_dashboard(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "_dash_sf"): return
        p = self._dash_sf.inner
        for w in p.winfo_children(): w.destroy()

        today      = today_str()
        today_bks  = [b for b in self.bookings
                      if b.get("date") == today and b["status"] == "Booked"]
        arrived    = [b for b in self.bookings if b["status"] == "Arrived"]
        avail_bays = sum(1 for b in self.bays
                         if b["status"] == "Available" and not b.get("maintenance"))
        active_jobs= [j for j in self.jobs
                      if j["status"] not in ("Complete","Cancelled")]
        queue_len  = len(self.queue)

        # ── Brand header ──
        brand_row = tk.Frame(p, bg=C["bg"]); brand_row.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12,0))
        _brand_txt = _lc.status().app_brand
        self._dash_brand_lbl = tk.Label(
            brand_row, text=_brand_txt,
            font=F(20,"bold"), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"])
        self._dash_brand_lbl.pack(side="left")
        if _brand_txt != "Garage Pro":
            tk.Label(brand_row, text="  powered by Garage Pro",
                     font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left", pady=(8,0))

        # ── Stats row ──
        self._section(p, f"Dashboard — {datetime.today().strftime('%A %d %B %Y')}")
        stats = tk.Frame(p, bg=C["bg"])
        stats.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(0, 8))
        self._stat_card(stats, "Expected today",   len(today_bks),  "info")
        self._stat_card(stats, "Cars in workshop", len(arrived),    "warning")
        self._stat_card(stats, "Available bays",   avail_bays,      "success")
        self._stat_card(stats, "Active jobs",       len(active_jobs),"purple")
        self._stat_card(stats, "In queue",          queue_len,
                        "orange" if queue_len else "muted",
                        click_cmd=lambda: self.show_tab("queue"))


        # ── Expected today ──
        self._section(p, "Expected today")
        if today_bks:
            card = self._card(p, padx=0, pady=0)
            for bk in sorted(today_bks, key=lambda b: b.get("time","")):
                row = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"])
                row.pack(fill="x", padx=12, pady=5)
                tk.Label(row, text=bk.get("time","—"), font=F(10, "bold"),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["info"],
                         width=6, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                tk.Label(row, text=bk["plate"], font=F(10, "bold"),
                         bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                         padx=6, pady=1).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
                tk.Label(row, text=bk["cust_name"], font=F(10),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
                tk.Label(row, text=f"  —  {bk['type']}", font=F(10),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
                if bk.get("notes"):
                    tk.Label(row, text=f"  📝 {bk['notes'][:40]}",
                             font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        else:
            tk.Label(p, text="No bookings expected today.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(padx=16, pady=4, anchor="w")

        # ── Cars currently in workshop ──
        self._section(p, "Cars currently in workshop")
        if arrived:
            card = self._card(p, padx=0, pady=0)
            for bk in arrived:
                job = next((j for j in self.jobs
                            if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), None)
                has_invoice = bool(job and (job.get("parts") or job.get("service_charges")))

                row = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"])
                row.pack(fill="x", padx=12, pady=6)

                # Info labels
                info = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["surface"]); info.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
                top  = tk.Frame(info, bg=C["surface"]); top.pack(fill="x")
                tk.Label(top, text=bk["plate"], font=F(10,"bold"),
                         bg=C["warning_bg"], fg=C["warning"],
                         padx=6, pady=1).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
                tk.Label(top, text=bk["cust_name"], font=F(10),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
                tk.Label(top, text=f"  —  {bk.get('type','')} · Bay {bk.get('bay_num','?')}",
                         font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
                if job:
                    scol = {k:v for k,(v,_) in {
                        "In Progress":("info",""), "Awaiting Approval":("warning",""),
                        "Parts Ordered":("orange",""), "Parts Not Available":("danger",""),
                        "Ready for Delivery":("success",""), "Delivered":("muted",""),
                    }.items()}.get(job["status"],"muted")
                    # scol is wrong from above dict comp — simpler:
                    scol2 = JOB_STATUS_COLOURS.get(job["status"],("muted","muted"))[1]
                    tk.Label(top, text=f"  ● {job['status']}", font=F(9),
                             bg=C["surface"], fg=C[scol2]).pack(side="left")

                # Buttons row
                btns = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["surface"]); btns.pack(side="right")
                Btn(btns, "✏ Edit booking",
                    cmd=lambda b=bk: self._booking_edit_win(b),
                    style="default", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
                if has_invoice:
                    Btn(btns, "🧾 View invoice",
                        cmd=lambda b=bk, j=job: self._show_invoice_win(b, j),
                        style="purple", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
                    Btn(btns, "✏ Edit invoice",
                        cmd=lambda b=bk, j=job: self._open_invoice_editor(b, j),
                        style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
                else:
                    Btn(btns, "🧾 Create invoice",
                        cmd=lambda b=bk, j=job: self._open_invoice_editor(b, j),
                        style="success", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
                if job and job.get("photos"):
                    Btn(btns, "📷 Photos",
                        cmd=lambda j=job: self._preview_photos(j),
                        style="purple", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
                Btn(btns, "🚪 Check out",
                    cmd=lambda b=bk: self._checkout(b),
                    style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")
        else:
            tk.Label(p, text="No cars in the workshop right now.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(padx=16, pady=4, anchor="w")



    # =========================================================================
    # NEW BOOKING
    # =========================================================================

    def _searchable_combo(self, parent, row, col, label, all_values,
                             on_select=None, colspan=1, bg_key="surface"):
        """A combobox that also filters as you type — searchable dropdown."""
        tk.Label(parent, text=label.upper(), font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C[bg_key]).grid(
            row=row, column=col, columnspan=colspan, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))
        var = tk.StringVar()
        cb  = ttk.Combobox(parent, textvariable=var, values=all_values,
                            font=F(11), state="normal")
        cb.grid(row=row+1, column=col, columnspan=colspan,
                sticky="ew", pady=(0, 2), padx=(0, 6))
        def on_key(*_):
            q = var.get().lower()
            filtered = [v for v in all_values if q in v.lower()] if q else all_values
            cb["values"] = filtered
            cb.event_generate("<Down>")   # open dropdown to show filtered
        def on_sel(*_):
            if on_select: on_select()
        cb.bind("<KeyRelease>", on_key)
        cb.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", on_sel)
        return var, cb

    def _build_new_booking(self):
        panel = self.panels["new_booking"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self.nb_plate   = lbl_entry(self.nb_wi, "License plate",      2, 0, bg_key="surface")

            row=3, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="ew", pady=10)

        # ── Searchable service type ──

                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=4, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1), padx=(0, 6))
        self.nb_service_var = tk.StringVar()

                                relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],

        Btn(br, "⚡  Walk-in now", cmd=self._walkin_now,    style="orange").pack(side="left")

    # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    # CUSTOMER SEARCH (New Booking tab)
    # State:
    #   self._nb_cust_is_placeholder  bool   True = grey hint text showing
    #   self._nb_selected_cust_idx    int    0=walk-in, 1+=1-based index
    #   self._nb_cust_popup           Toplevel | None
    # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    def _nb_set_placeholder(self):
        """Show grey placeholder hint text in the customer entry."""
        self.nb_cust_entry.delete(0, "end")
        self.nb_cust_entry.insert(0, "Type name, phone, plate or email to search…")
        self.nb_cust_entry.config(fg=C["muted"])
        self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = True

    def _nb_cust_focus_in(self, _=None):
        """On click/focus: clear placeholder if showing, then open dropdown."""
        if self._nb_cust_is_placeholder:
            self.nb_cust_entry.delete(0, "end")
            self.nb_cust_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
            self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = False
        self._nb_cust_do_search()

    def _nb_cust_focus_out(self, _=None):
        # Slight delay so a listbox click registers before popup closes
        self.after(200, self._nb_cust_close_popup)

    def _nb_cust_close_popup(self):
        if self._nb_cust_popup:
            try: self._nb_cust_popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_cust_popup = None
        # If nothing typed / nothing selected, revert to placeholder + walk-in
        val = self.nb_cust_var.get().strip()
        if not val or self._nb_cust_is_placeholder:
            self._nb_selected_cust_idx = 0
            self._nb_set_placeholder()
            self._show_walkin_fields()

    def _nb_cust_search(self, _=None):
        """KeyRelease handler — only search when not in placeholder state."""
        if self._nb_cust_is_placeholder:
            return
        self._nb_cust_do_search()

    def _nb_cust_do_search(self):
        """Build filtered matches list and show dropdown popup."""
        q = self.nb_cust_var.get().strip().lower()
        matches = []
        for i, c in enumerate(self.customers):
            plates = [v.get("plate", "") for v in c.get("cars", [])]
            if (not q
                    or q in c["name"].lower()
                    or q in c.get("phone", "").lower()
                    or q in c.get("email", "").lower()
                    or any(q in p.lower() for p in plates)):
                info  = c.get("phone", "") or c.get("email", "") or "no contact"
                label = f"{c['name']}  ({info})"
                matches.append((i, label))
        self._nb_cust_show_popup(matches)

    def _nb_cust_show_popup(self, matches):
        """Show Toplevel listbox below the customer entry."""
        if self._nb_cust_popup:
            try: self._nb_cust_popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_cust_popup = None

        if not self.nb_cust_entry.winfo_ismapped():
            return

        # Fixed header items + matching customers
        items = [
            ("walk-in",  "🚶  Walk-in (fill in name & plate below)"),
            ("new-cust", "➕  Add new customer to database…"),
        ] + matches

        popup = tk.Toplevel(self)
        popup.overrideredirect(True)
        popup.attributes("-topmost", True)
        popup.configure(bg=C["border"])

        visible = min(len(items), 9)
        lb = tk.Listbox(
            popup, font=F(10),
            bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
            selectbackground=C["info_bg"], selectforeground=C["info"],
            relief="flat", bd=0,
            height=visible,
            highlightthickness=0, activestyle="none")
        lb.pack(fill="both", padx=1, pady=1)

        for _, label in items:
            lb.insert("end", label)
        # Colour the fixed header rows
        lb.itemconfig(0, fg=C["warning"])
        lb.itemconfig(1, fg=C["success"])

        self.nb_cust_entry.update_idletasks()
        x = self.nb_cust_entry.winfo_rootx()
        y = self.nb_cust_entry.winfo_rooty() + self.nb_cust_entry.winfo_height() + 1
        w = max(self.nb_cust_entry.winfo_width(), 320)
        popup.geometry(f"{w}x{visible * 27 + 4}+{x}+{y}")

        def on_select(_=None):
            sel = lb.curselection()
            if not sel: return
            kind, label = items[sel[0]]
            try: popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_cust_popup = None

            if kind == "walk-in":
                self.nb_cust_var.set("")
                self.nb_cust_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
                self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = False
                self._nb_selected_cust_idx = 0
                self._show_walkin_fields()

            elif kind == "new-cust":
                self._nb_quick_add_customer()

            else:
                # kind == integer index into self.customers
                self.nb_cust_var.set(label)
                self.nb_cust_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
                self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = False
                self._nb_selected_cust_idx = kind + 1  # 1-based
                self._show_customer_vehicles(self.customers[kind])

        lb.bind("<<ListboxSelect>>", on_select)
        lb.bind("<Return>", on_select)
        self._nb_cust_popup = popup

    def _nb_quick_add_customer(self):
        """Mini modal to add a brand-new customer directly from the New Booking tab."""
        win = self._modal("Add new customer", w=540, h=280)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0, 1, 2), weight=1, uniform="qac")

        tk.Label(f, text="New customer", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=(0, 10))

        e_name  = lbl_entry(f, "Full name *",      1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_phone = lbl_entry(f, "Phone",             1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_email = lbl_entry(f, "Email",             1, 2, bg_key="surface")
        e_plate = lbl_entry(f, "License plate *",  3, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_model = lbl_entry(f, "Vehicle make/model", 3, 1, bg_key="surface")

        tk.Label(f, text="* required", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"],
                 fg=C["muted"]).grid(row=5, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(6, 0))
        e_name.focus_set()

        def save():
            name  = e_name.get().strip()
            phone = e_phone.get().strip()
            email = e_email.get().strip()
            plate = e_plate.get().strip().upper()
            model = e_model.get().strip()
            if not name:
                self._toast("Please enter a name.", "error"); return
            if not plate:
                self._toast("Please enter a license plate.", "error"); return
            car  = dict(id=uid(), plate=plate, model=model, year="", colour="")
            cust = dict(id=uid(), name=name, phone=phone, email=email,
                        cars=[car], bookings=[], created_at=now_str())
            self.customers.append(cust)
            self._save()
            win.destroy()
            new_idx = len(self.customers) - 1
            self._nb_selected_cust_idx = new_idx + 1
            label = f"{name}  ({phone or email or 'no contact'})"
            self.nb_cust_var.set(label)
            self.nb_cust_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
            self._nb_cust_is_placeholder = False
            self._show_customer_vehicles(cust)
            self._toast(f'"{name}" added and selected.', "success")

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=6, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="e", pady=(14, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel",       cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(foot, "Add customer", cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _show_walkin_fields(self):
        """Show manual name/plate fields for a walk-in."""
        self.nb_wi.grid()
        self.nb_car_cb["values"] = ["— enter plate in fields below —"]
        self.nb_car_var.set("— enter plate in fields below —")

    def _show_customer_vehicles(self, cust):
        """Hide manual fields and show this customer's registered vehicles."""
        self.nb_wi.grid_remove()
        cars = cust.get("cars", [])
        if cars:
            labels = [
                f"{v['plate']}  {v.get('model', '')}".strip() +
                (f" ({v['year']})" if v.get("year") else "")
                for v in cars]
            self.nb_car_cb["values"] = labels
            self.nb_car_var.set(labels[0])
        else:
            self.nb_car_cb["values"] = ["No vehicles — add via Customers tab"]
            self.nb_car_var.set("No vehicles — add via Customers tab")

    def _refresh_new_booking(self):
        self.nb_type["values"] = self.all_bay_types
        # service uses popup listbox — no combobox values to update
        mech_opts = ["— None —"] + [m["name"] for m in self.mechanics]
        self.nb_mech["values"] = mech_opts
        if not self.nb_mech.get():
            self.nb_mech.set(mech_opts[0])

    # ── Service type search ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # Same popup-listbox pattern as customer search.
    # "Other (custom)" reveals a free-text entry below.

    def _nb_svc_set_placeholder(self):
        self.nb_service_entry.delete(0, "end")
        self.nb_service_entry.insert(0, "Type to search service…")
        self.nb_service_entry.config(fg=C["muted"])
        self._nb_svc_is_placeholder = True

    def _nb_svc_focus_in(self, _=None):
        if self._nb_svc_is_placeholder:
            self.nb_service_entry.delete(0, "end")
            self.nb_service_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
            self._nb_svc_is_placeholder = False
        self._nb_svc_do_search()

    def _nb_svc_focus_out(self, _=None):
        self.after(200, self._nb_svc_close_popup)

    def _nb_svc_close_popup(self):
        if self._nb_svc_popup:
            try: self._nb_svc_popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_svc_popup = None
        # If nothing selected, restore placeholder
        val = self.nb_service_var.get().strip()
        if not val or self._nb_svc_is_placeholder:
            self._nb_svc_set_placeholder()
            self._nb_svc_hide_custom()

    def _nb_svc_search(self, _=None):
        if self._nb_svc_is_placeholder:
            return
        self._nb_svc_do_search()

    def _nb_svc_do_search(self):
        q        = self.nb_service_var.get().strip().lower()
        filtered = [n for n in SERVICE_NAMES if not q or q in n.lower()]
        self._nb_svc_show_popup(filtered)

    def _nb_svc_show_popup(self, names):
        if self._nb_svc_popup:
            try: self._nb_svc_popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_svc_popup = None

        if not self.nb_service_entry.winfo_ismapped():
            return

        if not names:
            return

        popup = tk.Toplevel(self)
        popup.overrideredirect(True)
        popup.attributes("-topmost", True)
        popup.configure(bg=C["border"])

        visible = min(len(names), 10)
        lb = tk.Listbox(
            popup, font=F(10),
            bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
            selectbackground=C["info_bg"], selectforeground=C["info"],
            relief="flat", bd=0, height=visible,
            highlightthickness=0, activestyle="none")
        lb.pack(fill="both", padx=1, pady=1)

        for name in names:
            lb.insert("end", name)
        # Highlight "Other (custom)" differently
        for i, n in enumerate(names):
            if n == "Other (custom)":
                lb.itemconfig(i, fg=C["warning"])

        self.nb_service_entry.update_idletasks()
        x = self.nb_service_entry.winfo_rootx()
        y = self.nb_service_entry.winfo_rooty() + self.nb_service_entry.winfo_height() + 1
        w = max(self.nb_service_entry.winfo_width(), 280)
        popup.geometry(f"{w}x{visible * 27 + 4}+{x}+{y}")

        def on_select(_=None):
            sel = lb.curselection()
            if not sel: return
            name = names[sel[0]]
            try: popup.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._nb_svc_popup = None
            self._nb_svc_pick(name)

        lb.bind("<<ListboxSelect>>", on_select)
        lb.bind("<Return>", on_select)
        self._nb_svc_popup = popup

    def _nb_svc_pick(self, name):
        """Called when user picks a service from the popup."""
        self.nb_service_var.set(name)
        self.nb_service_entry.config(fg=C["text"])
        self._nb_svc_is_placeholder = False
        # Auto-fill price
        price = SERVICE_MAP.get(name)
        if price is not None and price > 0:
            self.nb_cost.delete(0, "end")
            self.nb_cost.insert(0, str(price))
        # Show/hide custom entry
        if name == "Other (custom)":
            self._nb_svc_show_custom()
        else:
            self._nb_svc_hide_custom()

    def _nb_svc_show_custom(self):
        """Show the free-text entry for Other (custom) service."""
        self.nb_service_custom.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew",
                                    pady=(4, 0))
        self.nb_service_custom.focus_set()
        self.nb_service_custom.delete(0, "end")
        # Add placeholder hint
        self.nb_service_custom.insert(0, "Describe the service…")
        self.nb_service_custom.config(fg=C["muted"])
        def _clear(e):
            if self.nb_service_custom.get() == "Describe the service…":
                self.nb_service_custom.delete(0, "end")
                self.nb_service_custom.config(fg=C["text"])
        self.nb_service_custom.bind("<FocusIn>", _clear)

    def _nb_svc_hide_custom(self):
        """Hide the custom service text entry."""
        try:
            self.nb_service_custom.grid_remove()
            self.nb_service_custom_var.set("")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Legacy stubs kept so nothing else breaks
    def _nb_service_search(self, _=None):
        pass

    def _nb_service_selected(self, _=None):
        pass


    def _gather_booking_fields(self):
        """Collect + validate form fields. Returns dict or None on error."""
        # _nb_selected_cust_idx: 0 = walk-in, 1+ = index into self.customers (1-based)
        cust_idx = getattr(self, "_nb_selected_cust_idx", 0)
        walk_in  = (cust_idx == 0)

        # If "Other (custom)" was selected, use the free-text description
        _raw_svc = self.nb_service_var.get().strip()
        if _raw_svc == "Other (custom)":
            _custom = self.nb_service_custom_var.get().strip()
            if not _custom or _custom == "Describe the service…":
                self._toast("Please describe the custom service.", "error"); return None
            service = _custom
        elif self._nb_svc_is_placeholder or not _raw_svc:
            service = ""
        else:
            service = _raw_svc
        bay_type = self.nb_type.get()
        date_val = self.nb_date.get().strip() if self.nb_date else today_str()
        time_val = self.nb_time.get() if self.nb_time else "09:00"
        notes    = self.nb_notes.get("1.0", "end").strip()
        dur  = 1.0
        try:    cost = float(self.nb_cost.get())
        except: cost = 0.0
        mech_idx = self.nb_mech.current()
        mechanic = (self.mechanics[mech_idx-1]["name"]
                    if mech_idx > 0 and self.mechanics else "")

        if not service:
            self._toast("Please enter or select a service type.", "error"); return None
        if not bay_type:
            self._toast("Please select a bay type.", "error"); return None
        if not date_val:
            self._toast("Please enter a date.", "error"); return None

        if walk_in:
            cust_name  = self.nb_name.get().strip()
            cust_phone = self.nb_phone.get().strip()
            cust_email = self.nb_email.get().strip()
            plate      = self.nb_plate.get().strip().upper()
            car_desc   = self.nb_cardesc.get().strip()
            cust_id = car_id = None
            if not cust_name:
                self._toast("Please enter the customer name.", "error"); return None
            if not plate:
                self._toast("Please enter a license plate.", "error"); return None
        else:
            if cust_idx - 1 >= len(self.customers):
                self._toast("Customer not found. Please search again.", "error"); return None
            cust       = self.customers[cust_idx - 1]
            cust_name  = cust["name"]
            cust_phone = cust.get("phone", "")
            cust_email = cust.get("email", "")
            cust_id    = cust["id"]
            car_idx_v  = self.nb_car_cb.current()
            cars       = cust.get("cars", [])
            if not cars or car_idx_v < 0:
                self._toast("Please select a vehicle.", "error"); return None
            car      = cars[car_idx_v]
            plate    = car["plate"]
            car_desc = car.get("model", "")
            car_id   = car["id"]

        return dict(cust_id=cust_id, cust_name=cust_name,
                    cust_phone=cust_phone, cust_email=cust_email,
                    plate=plate, car_id=car_id, car_desc=car_desc,
                    service=service, type=bay_type,
                    date=date_val, time=time_val,
                    notes=notes, mechanic=mechanic,
                    est_cost=cost)

    def _clear_booking_form(self):
        for w in [self.nb_name, self.nb_phone, self.nb_email,
                  self.nb_plate, self.nb_cardesc]:
            w.delete(0, "end")
        self.nb_notes.delete("1.0", "end")
        self.nb_type.set("")
        self.nb_service_var.set("")
        self._nb_svc_is_placeholder = True
        self._nb_svc_set_placeholder()
        self._nb_svc_hide_custom()
        try:
            self.nb_date.delete(0, "end")
            self.nb_date.insert(0, today_str())
        except Exception:
            pass  # DateEntry doesn't need manual reset
        self.nb_time.set("09:00")
        self.nb_cost.delete(0, "end"); self.nb_cost.insert(0, "0")
        # Reset customer field to placeholder state
        self._nb_selected_cust_idx = 0
        self._nb_set_placeholder()
        self._show_walkin_fields()

    def _create_booking(self):
        if not LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_bookings):
            active = len([b for b in self.bookings
                          if b.get("status") in ("Booked","Arrived")])
            if active >= LIMITS.max_bookings:
                self._show_upgrade_prompt("bookings", LIMITS.max_bookings); return
        fields = self._gather_booking_fields()
        if not fields: return

        ref = booking_ref()
        bk = dict(id=uid(), ref=ref, status="Booked",
                  bay_id=None, bay_num=None,
                  actual_cost=0.0, checked_in=None, checked_out=None,
                  created_at=now_str(), **fields)
        self.bookings.append(bk)
        self._save()

        if fields["cust_email"]:
            ws = self.settings.get("workshop_name","")
            sender = self.settings.get("email_sender","")
            subject = f"Booking Confirmation [{bk['ref']}] — {ws}"
            body = (f"Dear {fields['cust_name']},\n\nYour booking has been confirmed.\n\n"
                    f"Booking Reference: {bk['ref']}\n"
                    f"Vehicle: {fields['plate']} {fields['car_desc']}\n"
                    f"Service: {fields['service']}\n"
                    f"Bay type: {fields['type']}\n"
                    f"Date: {fmt_date(fields['date'])} at {fields['time']}\n"
                    ""
                    f"Estimated cost: INR {fields['est_cost']:,.0f}\n\n"
                    f"To cancel or reschedule, please call us or reply to this email.\n\n"
                    f"Thank you,\n{ws}\n{sender}")
            send_email_async(self.settings, fields["cust_email"], subject, body,
                             callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                                 "Confirmation email sent!" if not e else f"Email failed: {e}",
                                 "success" if not e else "warning"))

        self._clear_booking_form()
        self._toast(
            f"Booking created — {fields['cust_name']}  ·  "
            f"{fields['plate']}  ·  {fmt_date(fields['date'])}", "success")
        # Reset date to today and worker to none
        try:
            self.nb_date.delete(0,"end"); self.nb_date.insert(0, today_str())
        except Exception: pass
        try: self.nb_time.set("09:00")
        except Exception: pass
        mech_opts = ["— None —"] + [m["name"] for m in self.mechanics]
        self.nb_mech["values"] = mech_opts; self.nb_mech.set(mech_opts[0])
        self.refresh_all()

    def _walkin_now(self):
        """Walk-in: create booking for today/now and immediately check in if a bay is free."""
        fields = self._gather_booking_fields()
        if not fields: return

        # Override date/time to right now
        fields["date"] = today_str()
        fields["time"] = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M")

        ref = booking_ref()
        bk = dict(id=uid(), ref=ref, status="Booked",
                  bay_id=None, bay_num=None,
                  actual_cost=0.0, checked_in=None, checked_out=None,
                  created_at=now_str(), **fields)
        self.bookings.append(bk)
        self._save()
        self._clear_booking_form()

        # Try to immediately assign a bay
        available = [b for b in self.bays
                     if b["type"] == fields["type"]
                     and b["status"] == "Available"
                     and not b.get("maintenance")]
        if available:
            self._save()
            self.refresh_all()
            self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=True)
        else:
            # No bay free — add to queue (no duplicates)
            plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
            if any(q["plate"].upper() == plate_up for q in self.queue):
                self._toast(f"{bk['plate']} is already in the waiting queue.", "info")
            else:
                self.queue.append(dict(
                    id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
                    plate=bk["plate"], cust_name=bk["cust_name"],
                    type=bk["type"], notes=bk.get("notes",""),
                    added_at=now_str()))
                self._save()
                self._toast(
                    f"No {fields['type']} bay available. "
                    f"{fields['plate']} added to the waiting queue.", "warning")
            self.refresh_all()

    # =========================================================================
    # BOOKINGS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_bookings(self):
        panel = self.panels["bookings"]
        bar = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12, 4))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Search (plate / name / BRN / service):", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.bk_q = tk.StringVar()
        self.bk_q.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_bookings())
        tk.Entry(bar, textvariable=self.bk_q, font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                 width=30).pack(side="left", padx=(4, 14))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Status:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.bk_sf = tk.StringVar(value="All")
        ttk.Combobox(bar, textvariable=self.bk_sf, state="readonly",
                     values=["All","Booked","Arrived","Checked out","Cancelled"],
                     font=F(10), width=13).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        self.bk_sf.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_bookings())

        cols = ("ref","plate","cust","service","date","time","dur","cost","mechanic","status","bay")
        self.bk_tree = ttk.Treeview(panel, columns=cols, show="headings", height=15)
        hdrs   = dict(ref="Ref", plate="Plate", cust="Customer", service="Service",
                      date="Date", time="Time", dur="Hrs", cost="Cost",
                      mechanic="Mechanic", status="Status", bay="Bay")
        widths = dict(ref=75, plate=80, cust=120, service=130, date=90, time=50,
                      dur=40, cost=60, mechanic=100, status=75, bay=52)
        for c in cols:
            self.bk_tree.heading(c, text=hdrs[c])
            self.bk_tree.column(c, width=widths[c], minwidth=36)
        self.bk_tree.tag_configure("booked",      background=C["info_bg"],    foreground=C["info"])
        self.bk_tree.tag_configure("arrived",     background=C["warning_bg"], foreground=C["warning"])
        self.bk_tree.tag_configure("checked out", background=C["success_bg"], foreground=C["success"])
        self.bk_tree.tag_configure("cancelled",   background=C["bg"],         foreground=C["muted"])
        self.bk_tree.tag_configure("parked",      background=C["purple_bg"], foreground=C["purple"])
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(panel, orient="vertical", command=self.bk_tree.yview)
        self.bk_tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        self.bk_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                          padx=(16, 0), pady=(4, 0), side="left")
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y", pady=(4, 0), padx=(0, 16))

        ab = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); ab.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8, side="bottom")
        Btn(ab, "🧾  View invoice",    cmd=self._bk_view_invoice,     style="purple").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(ab, "✏  Edit",            cmd=self._bk_edit,             style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(ab, "📧  Send reminder",   cmd=self._bk_reminder,         style="info").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(ab, "✕  Cancel",          cmd=self._bk_cancel,           style="danger").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(ab, "🗑  Delete",           cmd=self._bk_delete,           style="danger").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 12))
        Btn(ab, "💾  Export CSV",       cmd=self._export_bookings_csv, style="default").pack(side="right")
        self.bk_tree.bind("<Double-1>", lambda e: self._bk_view_invoice())

    def _refresh_bookings(self):
        q  = self.bk_q.get().lower().strip()
        sf = self.bk_sf.get()
        self.bk_tree.delete(*self.bk_tree.get_children())
        for bk in sorted(self.bookings, key=lambda b: b.get("date", ""), reverse=True):
            if sf != "All" and bk["status"].lower() != sf.lower(): continue
            ref = bk.get("ref", "")
            if q and not any(q in s.lower() for s in [
                    bk.get("plate",""), bk.get("cust_name",""),
                    ref, bk.get("service",""), bk.get("type","")]):
                continue
            bay     = f"Bay {bk['bay_num']}" if bk.get("bay_num") else "—"
            service = bk.get("service") or bk.get("type") or "—"
            self.bk_tree.insert("", "end", iid=bk["id"],
                                tags=(bk["status"].lower(),),
                                values=(ref,
                                        bk["plate"], bk["cust_name"],
                                        service,
                                        fmt_date(bk.get("date", "")),
                                        bk.get("time", "—"),
                                        f"INR {bk.get('est_cost', 0):.0f}",
                                        bk.get("mechanic", "—"),
                                        bk["status"], bay))

    def _get_bk(self, tree):
        sel = tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            self._toast("Select a booking first.", "info"); return None
        return next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == sel[0]), None)

    def _free_bay(self, bk):
        if bk.get("bay_id"):
            bay = next((b for b in self.bays if b["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
            if bay:
                bay["status"] = "Available"
                bay["plate"]  = None
                bay["booking_id"] = None
        bk["bay_id"] = None; bk["bay_num"] = None

    def _bk_view_invoice(self):
        """Open invoice for selected booking — editor if new, viewer if exists."""
        bk = self._get_bk(self.bk_tree)
        if not bk: return
        job = next((j for j in self.jobs if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), None)
        has_items = job and (job.get("parts") or job.get("service_charges"))
        if has_items:
            self._show_invoice_win(bk, job)
        else:
            self._open_invoice_editor(bk, job)

    def _show_invoice_win(self, bk: dict, job: dict | None):
        """Pop up a scrollable invoice window — always shows latest data."""
        win = tk.Toplevel(self)
        win.title(f"Invoice — {bk['plate']}  {bk.get('ref','')}")
        win.configure(bg=C["surface"])
        win.geometry("700x680")
        sx = self.winfo_x() + (self.winfo_width()  - 700) // 2
        sy = self.winfo_y() + (self.winfo_height() - 680) // 2
        win.geometry(f"700x680+{sx}+{sy}")

        # ── Toolbar ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        toolbar = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"])
        toolbar.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12,0))

        def refresh_invoice():
            # Re-fetch fresh job data in case parts were added
            fresh_job = next((j for j in self.jobs
                              if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), job)
            _draw(fresh_job)

        Btn(toolbar, "🔄  Refresh",   cmd=refresh_invoice, style="info",  small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(toolbar, "📄  Save PDF",  cmd=lambda: self._save_invoice_from_win(bk, job),  style="purple", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(toolbar, "📧  Email now", cmd=lambda: self._email_invoice_now(bk, job),       style="success",small=True).pack(side="left")
        Btn(toolbar, "✕  Close",      cmd=win.destroy, style="default", small=True).pack(side="right")

        tk.Frame(win, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8)

        # ── Scrollable content ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        sf = ScrollableFrame(win); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=16, pady=(0,12))
        content = sf.inner

        def _draw(j):
            for w in content.winfo_children(): w.destroy()

            ws      = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "My Garage")
            address = self.settings.get("workshop_address", "")
            ref     = bk.get("ref", bk["id"][:8].upper())

            # ── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            hdr = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["surface"]); hdr.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,12))
            logo_path = self.settings.get("invoice_logo","")
            if logo_path and os.path.exists(logo_path):
                try:
                    img = PILImage.open(logo_path)
                    img.thumbnail((120,60))
                    self._inv_logo_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
                    tk.Label(hdr, image=self._inv_logo_img,
                             bg=C["surface"]).pack(side="left", padx=(0,12))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            title_f = tk.Frame(hdr, bg=C["surface"]); title_f.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            tk.Label(title_f, text=ws, font=F(16,"bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w")
            if address:
                for line in address.splitlines():
                    tk.Label(title_f, text=line.strip(), font=F(9),
                             bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(hdr, text="INVOICE", font=F(22,"bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="right", padx=(0,4))

            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(0,10))

            # ── Meta row ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            meta = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["surface"]); meta.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,12))
            for col,(label,value) in enumerate([
                ("Invoice Ref", ref),
                ("Date",        ((j.get("created_at","") if j else "") or now_str())[:11]),
                ("Mechanic",    ", ".join((j or {}).get("mechanics",[]) or 
                            ([(j or {}).get("mechanic","—")] if (j or {}).get("mechanic") else ["—"]))),
                ("Status",      bk.get("status","—")),
            ]):
                f = tk.Frame(meta, bg=C["info_bg"], padx=10, pady=8)
                f.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(0,4))
                tk.Label(f, text=label.upper(), font=F(7,"bold"),
                         bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"]).pack(anchor="w")
                tk.Label(f, text=value, font=F(10,"bold"),
                         bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w")

            # ── Customer & vehicle ────────────────────────────────────────────
            cv = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["surface"]); cv.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,12))
            cv.columnconfigure((0,1), weight=1, uniform="cv")
            for col,(section,rows) in enumerate([
                ("Customer", [
                    ("Name",  bk.get("cust_name","")),
                    ("Phone", bk.get("cust_phone","—")),
                    ("Email", bk.get("cust_email","—")),
                ]),
                ("Vehicle", [
                    ("Plate",   bk.get("plate","")),
                    ("Service", bk.get("service") or bk.get("type","—")),
                    ("Date",    f"{bk.get('date','')}  {bk.get('time','')}"),
                ]),
            ]):
                box = tk.Frame(cv, bg=C["bg"], padx=10, pady=8)
                box.grid(row=0, column=col, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if col else 0, 4 if col==0 else 0))
                tk.Label(box, text=section.upper(), font=F(8,"bold"),
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,4))
                for lbl,val in rows:
                    r = tk.Frame(box, bg=C["bg"]); r.pack(fill="x", pady=1)
                    tk.Label(r, text=lbl+":", font=F(9), bg=C["bg"],
                             fg=C["muted"], width=8, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                    tk.Label(r, text=val, font=F(9,"bold"), bg=C["bg"],
                             fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")

            # ── Items table ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(4,8))
            tk.Label(content, text="ITEMISED CHARGES", font=F(9,"bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,4))

            # Table header
            th = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["text"]); th.pack(fill="x")
            for txt,w,anchor in [("Description",0,"w"),("Qty",50,"e"),("Unit price",80,"e"),("Amount",80,"e")]:
                tk.Label(th, text=txt, font=F(8,"bold"), bg=C["text"], fg=C["bg"],
                         padx=8, pady=5, width=w//8 if w else 0, anchor=anchor).pack(
                    side="left" if anchor=="w" else "right",
                    fill="x" if anchor=="w" else None, expand=(anchor=="w"))

            grand = 0.0
            parts = (j or {}).get("parts", [])
            svcs  = (j or {}).get("service_charges", [])
            row_bg = [C["surface"], C["bg"]]

            for ri,p in enumerate(parts):
                qty   = int(p.get("qty",1))
                price = float(p.get("invoice_price", p.get("unit_price",0)))
