                total = price * qty; grand += total
                bg = row_bg[ri % 2]
                rw = tk.Frame(content, bg=bg); rw.pack(fill="x")
                tk.Label(rw, text=p.get("name","?"), font=F(9), bg=bg, fg=C["text"],
                         padx=8, pady=4, anchor="w").pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
                for val in [str(qty), f"INR {price:,.2f}", f"INR {total:,.2f}"]:
                    tk.Label(rw, text=val, font=F(9), bg=bg, fg=C["text"],
                             padx=8, pady=4, width=10, anchor="e").pack(side="right")

            offset = len(parts)
            for ri,sc in enumerate(svcs):
                price = float(sc.get("price",0)); grand += price
                bg = row_bg[(offset+ri) % 2]
                rw = tk.Frame(content, bg=bg); rw.pack(fill="x")
                tk.Label(rw, text=sc.get("name","Service charge"), font=F(9),
                         bg=bg, fg=C["info"], padx=8, pady=4, anchor="w").pack(
                    side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
                for val in ["—", "—", f"INR {price:,.2f}"]:
                    tk.Label(rw, text=val, font=F(9), bg=bg, fg=C["text"],
                             padx=8, pady=4, width=10, anchor="e").pack(side="right")

            if not parts and not svcs:
                tk.Label(content, text="  No items recorded yet.",
                         font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                         pady=8).pack(anchor="w")

            # Totals block
            tax_pct  = float(self.settings.get("tax_percent", 18))
            disc_pct = float((j or {}).get("discount_pct", 0))
            subtotal = grand
            disc_amt = round(subtotal * disc_pct / 100, 2)
            taxable  = subtotal - disc_amt
            tax_amt  = round(taxable * tax_pct / 100, 2)
            total    = taxable + tax_amt

            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(6,2))
            for lbl,val,bold in [
                ("Subtotal",               f"INR {subtotal:,.2f}",  False),
                (f"Discount ({disc_pct:.0f}%)", f"−INR {disc_amt:,.2f}", False),
                ("Taxable Value",          f"INR {taxable:,.2f}",   False),
                (f"GST ({tax_pct:.0f}%)",  f"INR {tax_amt:,.2f}",  False),
                ("TOTAL DUE",              f"INR {total:,.2f}",     True),
            ]:
                rr = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["surface"]); rr.pack(fill="x")
                fn = F(11,"bold") if bold else F(10)
                fg = C["success"] if bold else C["text"]
                tk.Label(rr, text=lbl, font=fn, bg=C["surface"],
                         fg=C["muted"] if not bold else C["text"]).pack(side="left", padx=8, pady=3)
                tk.Label(rr, text=val, font=fn, bg=C["surface"],
                         fg=fg).pack(side="right", padx=8, pady=3)
            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(2,4))

            # Payment status
            pstat = (j or {}).get("payment_status","Outstanding")
            pclr  = {"Paid":C["success"],"Partially Paid":C["warning"]}.get(pstat,C["danger"])
            tk.Label(content, text=f"Payment status: {pstat}",
                     font=F(10,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=pclr).pack(anchor="w", padx=8, pady=4)

            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(4,10))
            tk.Label(content,
                     text=f"Thank you for choosing {ws}. Please retain this invoice for your records.",
                     font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", padx=4)

        _draw(job)

    def _preview_photos(self, job: dict):
        """Open a window showing all photos for this job."""
        photos = job.get("photos", [])
        if not photos:
            self._toast("No photos on this job yet.", "info"); return
        win = tk.Toplevel(self)
        win.title(f"Photos — {job.get('plate','')}")
        win.configure(bg=C["surface"])
        win.geometry("800x600")
        cx = self.winfo_x() + (self.winfo_width()  - 800) // 2
        cy = self.winfo_y() + (self.winfo_height() - 600) // 2
        win.geometry(f"800x600+{cx}+{cy}")

        # Toolbar
        tb = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"]); tb.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12,4))
        tk.Label(tb, text=f"Photos — {job.get('plate','')}  ({len(photos)} photo(s))",
                 font=F(12,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
        Btn(tb, "💾 Save photos PDF", cmd=lambda: self._save_photos_pdf(job),
            style="purple", small=True).pack(side="right")
        tk.Frame(win, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(4,8))

        sf = ScrollableFrame(win); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=16)
        content = sf.inner
        for i, ph in enumerate(photos):
            row = tk.Frame(content, bg=C["bg"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,12))
            # Label
            tk.Label(row, text=f"{i+1}. {ph.get('label','Photo')}",
                     font=F(11,"bold"), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["info"]).pack(anchor="w", padx=8, pady=(8,4))
            # Description
            desc = ph.get("description","")
            if desc:
                tk.Label(row, text=desc, font=F(10), bg=C["bg"],
                         fg=C["muted"], wraplength=700, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", padx=8, pady=(0,6))
            # Image
            b64 = ph.get("data","")
            if b64 and _PIL:
                try:
                    import io as _bio
                    raw = base64.b64decode(b64)
                    img = PILImage.open(_bio.BytesIO(raw))
                    img.thumbnail((700,500))
                    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
                    lbl = tk.Label(row, image=tk_img, bg=C["bg"])
                    lbl.image = tk_img
                    lbl.pack(padx=8, pady=(0,8))
                except Exception as e:
                    tk.Label(row, text=f"Could not load image: {e}",
                             font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["danger"]).pack(anchor="w", padx=8)
            tk.Frame(content, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(0,8))

    def _save_photos_pdf(self, job: dict):
        """Save the photos PDF to disk."""
        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == job.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not _RL:
            self._toast("reportlab not installed — cannot generate PDF.", "error"); return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".pdf",
            filetypes=[("PDF","*.pdf"),("All","*.*")],
            title="Save photos PDF",
            initialfile=f"Photos_{job.get('plate','')}.pdf")
        if not path: return
        try:
            pdf = generate_photos_pdf_bytes(job, bk, self.settings)
            with open(path,"wb") as fh: fh.write(pdf)
            self._toast("Photos PDF saved.", "success")
        except Exception as e:
            self._toast(f"Error: {e}", "error")

    def _save_invoice_from_win(self, bk, job):
        fresh = next((j for j in self.jobs if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), job)
        export_job_card_pdf(fresh, bk, self.settings, self)

    def _email_invoice_now(self, bk, job):
        fresh = next((j for j in self.jobs if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), job)
        send_invoice_email(fresh, bk, self.settings)
        self._toast("Invoice emailed to customer.", "success")

    def _open_invoice_editor(self, bk: dict, job: dict | None):
        """Full create/edit invoice modal — per-booking bank details, VIN,
        discount %, HSN/SAC per part, payment status, service charges."""
        if job is None:
            self._toast("No job card exists for this booking yet.", "info")
            return

        # Load or initialise the per-booking invoice profile stored on the job
        prof = job.get("invoice_profile", {})

        win = tk.Toplevel(self)
        win.title(f"Invoice — {bk['plate']}  {bk.get('ref','')}")
        win.configure(bg=C["surface"])
        win.geometry("780x780")
        cx = self.winfo_x() + (self.winfo_width()  - 780) // 2
        cy = self.winfo_y() + (self.winfo_height() - 780) // 2
        win.geometry(f"780x780+{cx}+{cy}")

        # ── Scrollable body ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        sf = ScrollableFrame(win); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=0, pady=0)
        f  = sf.inner
        f.configure(bg=C["surface"])
        pad = dict(padx=20, pady=4, sticky="ew")

        def section(txt):
            tk.Label(f, text=txt, font=F(8,"bold"), fg=C["muted"],
                     bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", padx=20, pady=(14,2))

        def entry_row(label, default="", wide=False):
            row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=2)
            tk.Label(row, text=label, font=F(8,"bold"), fg=C["muted"],
                     bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
            e = tk.Entry(row, font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                         width=60 if wide else 32)
            e.pack(anchor="w"); e.insert(0, str(default))
            return e

        def two_entries(l1, d1, l2, d2):
            row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=2)
            row.columnconfigure((0,1), weight=1, uniform="ie")
            def make(col, lbl, dfl):
                c = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["surface"]); c.grid(row=0, column=col, sticky="ew", padx=(0,8 if col==0 else 0))
                tk.Label(c, text=lbl, font=F(8,"bold"), fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
                e = tk.Entry(c, font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                             insertbackground=C["text"],
                             highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"], width=28)
                e.pack(fill="x"); e.insert(0, str(dfl)); return e
            return make(0, l1, d1), make(1, l2, d2)

        # ── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        tk.Label(f, text=f"Invoice — {bk['plate']}  ·  {bk.get('cust_name','')}",
                 font=F(13,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(
            anchor="w", padx=20, pady=(16,4))

        # ── Vehicle info ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        section("VEHICLE")
        e_vin,  e_plate = two_entries("VIN",   prof.get("vin",""),   "Plate", bk.get("plate",""))
        e_make, e_model = two_entries("Make",  prof.get("make",""),  "Model", prof.get("model",""))

        # ── Discount ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        section("DISCOUNT")
        disc_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); disc_row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=2)
        tk.Label(disc_row, text="Discount % (applies equally to all parts):",
                 font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
        e_disc = tk.Entry(disc_row, font=F(10), width=7, relief="flat", bd=0,
                          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                          highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_disc.pack(side="left", padx=8)
        e_disc.insert(0, str(prof.get("discount_pct", job.get("discount_pct", 0))))

        # ── Parts table ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        section("PARTS & SERVICE CHARGES")
        parts_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["bg"]); parts_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=4)

        # We'll track live edits in these lists
        parts_data = [dict(p) for p in job.get("parts", [])]
        svcs_data  = [dict(s) for s in job.get("service_charges", [])]
        part_widgets = []  # (name_var, hsn_var, qty_var, price_var)
        svc_widgets  = []  # (name_var, price_var)

        def rebuild_table():
            nonlocal part_widgets, svc_widgets
            part_widgets.clear(); svc_widgets.clear()
            for w in parts_frame.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
            # Header
            hdr = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["text"]); hdr.pack(fill="x")
            for txt, w in [("Part / Item",180),("HSN/SAC",70),("Qty",35),("Unit INR ",65),("",0),]:
                tk.Label(hdr, text=txt, font=F(8,"bold"), bg=C["text"], fg=C["bg"],
                         padx=5, pady=4, width=w//7 if w else 4, anchor="w").pack(
                    side="left", fill="x" if not w else None, expand=(not w))
            tk.Label(hdr, text="✕", font=F(8,"bold"), bg=C["text"], fg=C["bg"],
                     padx=5, pady=4, width=3).pack(side="right")

            bg_alt = [C["surface"], C["bg"]]
            for ri, pt in enumerate(parts_data):
                bg = bg_alt[ri % 2]
                row = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=bg); row.pack(fill="x")
                nv = tk.StringVar(value=pt.get("name",""))
                hv = tk.StringVar(value=str(pt.get("hsn","")))
                qv = tk.StringVar(value=str(pt.get("qty",1)))
                pv = tk.StringVar(value=f"{float(pt.get('invoice_price', pt.get('unit_price',0))):.2f}")
                part_widgets.append((nv,hv,qv,pv))
                for var, w in [(nv,170),(hv,65),(qv,32),(pv,62)]:
                    e = tk.Entry(row, textvariable=var, font=F(9), width=w//7,
                                 relief="flat", bd=0, bg=bg, fg=C["text"],
                                 insertbackground=C["text"],
                                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                    e.pack(side="left", padx=1, pady=2)
                def rm_part(i=ri):
                    parts_data.pop(i); rebuild_table()
                tk.Button(row, text="✕", font=F(8), fg=C["danger"], bg=bg,
                          relief="flat", bd=0, cursor="hand2",
                          command=rm_part, padx=4).pack(side="right")

            for ri, sc in enumerate(svcs_data):
                bg = bg_alt[(len(parts_data)+ri) % 2]
                row = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=bg); row.pack(fill="x")
                nv = tk.StringVar(value=sc.get("name","Service charge"))
                pv = tk.StringVar(value=f"{float(sc.get('price',0)):.2f}")
                svc_widgets.append((nv, pv))
                tk.Label(row, text="SERVICE:", font=F(8,"bold"), fg=C["info"],
                         bg=bg, padx=5).pack(side="left")
                for var, w in [(nv, 160), (pv, 70)]:
                    e = tk.Entry(row, textvariable=var, font=F(9), width=w//7,
                                 relief="flat", bd=0, bg=bg, fg=C["text"],
                                 insertbackground=C["text"],
                                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                    e.pack(side="left", padx=1, pady=2)
                def rm_svc(i=ri):
                    svcs_data.pop(i); rebuild_table()
                tk.Button(row, text="✕", font=F(8), fg=C["danger"], bg=bg,
                          relief="flat", bd=0, cursor="hand2",
                          command=rm_svc, padx=4).pack(side="right")

        rebuild_table()

        # Add part / add service charge
        add_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); add_row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=(6,2))
        inv_names = ["— From inventory —"] + [i["name"] for i in self.inventory]
        cb_inv = ttk.Combobox(add_row, values=inv_names, state="readonly", font=F(9), width=22)
        cb_inv.pack(side="left", padx=(0,4)); cb_inv.set(inv_names[0])
        tk.Label(add_row, text="Qty:", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_aqty = tk.Entry(add_row, font=F(9), width=4, relief="flat", bd=0,
                          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                          highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_aqty.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_aqty.insert(0,"1")
        def add_inv_part():
            idx = cb_inv.current()
            if idx <= 0: return
            inv = self.inventory[idx-1]
            try: qty = int(e_aqty.get())
            except: qty = 1
            parts_data.append(dict(inv_id=inv["id"], name=inv["name"], qty=qty,
                                   hsn=inv.get("hsn",""), unit_price=inv.get("sell_price",0),
                                   invoice_price=inv.get("sell_price",0)))
            rebuild_table()
        Btn(add_row, "+ Add part", cmd=add_inv_part, style="success", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))

        cust_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); cust_row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=2)
        e_cname = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=18, relief="flat", bd=0,
                           bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                           highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cname.pack(side="left", padx=(0,3)); e_cname.insert(0,"Custom item")
        tk.Label(cust_row, text="Qty:", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_cqty = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=4, relief="flat", bd=0,
                          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                          highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cqty.pack(side="left", padx=(2,3)); e_cqty.insert(0,"1")
        tk.Label(cust_row, text="INR", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_cprice = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=7, relief="flat", bd=0,
                            bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                            highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cprice.pack(side="left", padx=(2,3)); e_cprice.insert(0,"0")
        def add_custom_part():
            name = e_cname.get().strip()
            if not name or name=="Custom item": return
            try: qty=int(e_cqty.get())
            except: qty=1
            try: price=float(e_cprice.get())
            except: price=0.0
            parts_data.append(dict(inv_id=None,name=name,qty=qty,hsn="",
                                   unit_price=price,invoice_price=price))
            rebuild_table()
        Btn(cust_row, "+ Custom", cmd=add_custom_part, style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(3,8))

        svc_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); svc_row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=2)
        e_sname = tk.Entry(svc_row, font=F(9), width=20, relief="flat", bd=0,
                           bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                           highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_sname.pack(side="left", padx=(0,3)); e_sname.insert(0,"Labour charge")
        tk.Label(svc_row, text="INR", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_sprice = tk.Entry(svc_row, font=F(9), width=8, relief="flat", bd=0,
                            bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                            highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_sprice.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_sprice.insert(0,"0")
        def add_svc():
            name = e_sname.get().strip() or "Labour charge"
            try: price=float(e_sprice.get())
            except: price=0.0
            svcs_data.append(dict(name=name, price=price))
            rebuild_table()
        Btn(svc_row, "+ Service charge", cmd=add_svc, style="purple", small=True).pack(side="left")

        # ── Payment status ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        section("PAYMENT")
        pay_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); pay_row.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=4)
        tk.Label(pay_row, text="Payment status:", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        pay_var = tk.StringVar(value=prof.get("payment_status", job.get("payment_status","Outstanding")))
        for opt in ("Outstanding","Partially Paid","Paid"):
            tk.Radiobutton(pay_row, text=opt, variable=pay_var, value=opt,
                           font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                           selectcolor=C["bg"], activebackground=C["surface"]).pack(side="left", padx=8)

        # ── Bank details (per-invoice) ─────────────────────────────────────────
        section("BANK DETAILS  (saved per invoice)")
        e_bname,   e_bacc  = two_entries("Bank name",     prof.get("bank_name",""),
                                          "Account number", prof.get("bank_account",""))
        e_bifsc,   e_bbranch= two_entries("IFSC code",    prof.get("bank_ifsc",""),
                                          "Branch",         prof.get("bank_branch",""))
        e_bsort,   e_pterms = two_entries("Sort/SWIFT code",prof.get("bank_sort",""),
                                          "Payment terms",  prof.get("payment_terms",""))

        # ── Notes / terms ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        section("NOTES / TERMS (printed on invoice)")
        t_notes = tk.Text(f, height=3, font=F(9), relief="flat", bd=0,
                          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                          highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        t_notes.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=(0,4))
        t_notes.insert("1.0", prof.get("notes",""))

        # ── Footer buttons ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        tk.Frame(f, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=8)
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(fill="x", padx=20, pady=(0,16))

        def save_invoice(close=True):
            # Flush part_widgets back to parts_data
            for i,(nv,hv,qv,pv) in enumerate(part_widgets):
                if i < len(parts_data):
                    parts_data[i]["name"] = nv.get()
                    parts_data[i]["hsn"]  = hv.get()
                    try: parts_data[i]["qty"] = int(qv.get())
                    except: pass
                    try: parts_data[i]["invoice_price"] = float(pv.get())
                    except: pass
            for i,(nv,pv) in enumerate(svc_widgets):
                if i < len(svcs_data):
                    svcs_data[i]["name"] = nv.get()
                    try: svcs_data[i]["price"] = float(pv.get())
                    except: pass
            try: disc = float(e_disc.get())
            except: disc = 0.0

            job["parts"]           = parts_data
            job["service_charges"] = svcs_data
            job["discount_pct"]    = disc
            job["payment_status"]  = pay_var.get()
            job["invoice_profile"] = {
                "vin":          e_vin.get().strip(),
                "make":         e_make.get().strip(),
                "model":        e_model.get().strip(),
                "discount_pct": disc,
                "payment_status": pay_var.get(),
                "bank_name":    e_bname.get().strip(),
                "bank_account": e_bacc.get().strip(),
                "bank_ifsc":    e_bifsc.get().strip(),
                "bank_branch":  e_bbranch.get().strip(),
                "bank_sort":    e_bsort.get().strip(),
                "payment_terms":e_pterms.get().strip(),
                "notes":        t_notes.get("1.0","end").strip(),
            }
            self._save(); self._toast("Invoice saved.", "success")
            self.refresh_all()
            if close: win.destroy()

        def save_and_view():
            save_invoice(close=False)
            fresh = next((j for j in self.jobs if j.get("booking_id")==bk["id"]), job)
            self._show_invoice_win(bk, fresh)

        def save_and_email():
            save_invoice(close=False)
            fresh = next((j for j in self.jobs if j.get("booking_id")==bk["id"]), job)
            send_invoice_email(fresh, bk, self.settings)
            self._toast("Invoice emailed.", "success")

        Btn(foot, "Cancel",        cmd=win.destroy,    style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(foot, "💾 Save",        cmd=save_invoice,   style="primary").pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(foot, "👁 Save & View", cmd=save_and_view,  style="purple").pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(foot, "📧 Save & Email",cmd=save_and_email, style="success").pack(side="left")

    def _bk_edit(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.bk_tree)
        if bk: self._booking_edit_win(bk)

    def _booking_edit_win(self, bk):
        win = self._modal("Edit booking", w=640, h=580)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1, uniform="eb")

        tk.Label(f, text="Edit booking", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 10))

        e_plate = lbl_entry(f, "License plate",  1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_cust  = lbl_entry(f, "Customer name",  1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_phone = lbl_entry(f, "Phone",          3, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_email = lbl_entry(f, "Email",          3, 1, bg_key="surface")

        for widget, key in [(e_plate,"plate"),(e_cust,"cust_name"),
                            (e_phone,"cust_phone"),(e_email,"cust_email")]:
            val = bk.get(key, "")
            widget.insert(0, val.upper() if key == "plate" else val)

        cb_type = lbl_combo(f, "Bay type", 5, 0,
                            values=self.all_bay_types, bg_key="surface")
        cb_type.set(bk["type"])

        # Date with calendar
        e_date = self._date_field(f, 5, 1, label="Date",
                                  default=bk.get("date", today_str()))

        # Time dropdown
        t_time = self._time_field(f, 7, 0, label="Time",
                                  default=bk.get("time", "09:00"))



        e_cost = lbl_entry(f, "Est. cost (INR )", 9, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_cost.insert(0, str(bk.get("est_cost", "0")))

        mech_vals = ["— None —"] + [m["name"] for m in self.mechanics]
        cb_mech   = lbl_combo(f, "Mechanic", 9, 1,
                              values=mech_vals, bg_key="surface")
        cb_mech.set(bk.get("mechanic", "— None —") or "— None —")

        tk.Label(f, text="NOTES", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=11, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1))
        t_notes = tk.Text(f, height=3, font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                          highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        t_notes.grid(row=12, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew")
        t_notes.insert("1.0", bk.get("notes", ""))

        def save():
            new_type = cb_type.get()
            if not new_type:
                self._toast("Select a bay type.", "error"); return
            if bk.get("bay_id") and new_type != bk["type"]:
                old = next((b for b in self.bays if b["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
                if old:
                    old["status"] = "Available"
                    old["plate"]  = None
                    old["booking_id"] = None
                bk["bay_id"] = None; bk["bay_num"] = None
                if bk["status"] == "Arrived":
                    bk["status"] = "Booked"
            bk["plate"]      = e_plate.get().strip().upper()
            bk["cust_name"]  = e_cust.get().strip()
            bk["cust_phone"] = e_phone.get().strip()
            bk["cust_email"] = e_email.get().strip()
            bk["type"]       = new_type
            bk["date"]       = e_date.get().strip()
            bk["time"]       = t_time.get()

            try:    bk["est_cost"]     = float(e_cost.get())
            except: pass
            mi = cb_mech.current()
            bk["mechanic"] = (self.mechanics[mi-1]["name"]
                              if mi > 0 and self.mechanics else "")
            bk["notes"] = t_notes.get("1.0", "end").strip()
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast("Booking updated.", "success")
            self.refresh_all()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=13, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="e", pady=(14, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel",       cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save changes", cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _bk_reminder(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.bk_tree)
        if not bk: return
        ws     = self.settings.get("workshop_name","")
        sender = self.settings.get("email_sender","")
        ref    = bk.get("ref", "")
        subject = f"Reminder [{ref}] — Your booking on {fmt_date(bk.get('date',''))}"
        body = (f"Dear {bk['cust_name']},\n\nThis is a reminder of your upcoming appointment.\n\n"
                f"Booking Reference: {ref}\n"
                f"Vehicle: {bk['plate']}\nService: {bk.get('service', bk['type'])}\n"
                f"Date: {fmt_date(bk.get('date',''))} at {bk.get('time','')}\n\n"
                f"To cancel or reschedule, please call us or reply to this email.\n\n"
                f"Thank you,\n{ws}\n{sender}")
        send_email_async(self.settings, bk.get("cust_email",""), subject, body,
                         callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                             "Reminder sent!" if not e else f"Email failed: {e}",
                             "success" if not e else "error"))
        self._toast("Sending reminder...", "info")

    def _bk_cancel(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.bk_tree)
        if not bk: return
        if bk["status"] == "Cancelled":
            self._toast("Already cancelled.", "info"); return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Cancel booking",
                f"Cancel booking for {bk['plate']} ({bk['cust_name']})?\n"
                "The bay will be freed if assigned."): return
        self._free_bay(bk); bk["status"] = "Cancelled"
        self._save()
        self._toast(f"Booking for {bk['plate']} cancelled.", "info")
        self.refresh_all()

    def _bk_delete(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.bk_tree)
        if not bk: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete", "Permanently delete this booking?"): return
        self._free_bay(bk)
        self.bookings = [b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] != bk["id"]]
        self._save(); self.refresh_all()

    def _export_bookings_csv(self):
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".csv", filetypes=[("CSV","*.csv")],
            title="Export bookings")
        if not path: return
        with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["Plate","Customer","Phone","Email","Bay type","Date","Time",
                        "Est. hrs","Est. cost","Actual cost","Mechanic",
                        "Status","Bay","Notes","Created"])
            for bk in self.bookings:
                w.writerow([bk["plate"], bk["cust_name"],
                            bk.get("cust_phone",""), bk.get("cust_email",""),
                            bk["type"], bk.get("date",""), bk.get("time",""),
                            bk.get("est_cost",""),
                            bk.get("actual_cost",""), bk.get("mechanic",""),
                            bk["status"],
                            f"Bay {bk['bay_num']}" if bk.get("bay_num") else "",
                            bk.get("notes",""), bk.get("created_at","")])
        self._toast(f"Exported to {os.path.basename(path)}", "success")

    # =========================================================================
    # CHECK-IN
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_checkin(self):
        panel = self.panels["checkin"]
        bar = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12, 4))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Search:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.ci_q = tk.StringVar()
        self.ci_q.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_checkin())
        tk.Entry(bar, textvariable=self.ci_q, font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                 width=26).pack(side="left", padx=(4, 14))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Show:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.ci_sf = tk.StringVar(value="Booked")
        ttk.Combobox(bar, textvariable=self.ci_sf, state="readonly",
                     values=["Booked","Arrived","All active"],
                     font=F(10), width=12).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        self.ci_sf.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_checkin())

        cols = ("plate","cust","phone","type","date","time","mechanic","notes","status","bay")
        self.ci_tree = ttk.Treeview(panel, columns=cols, show="headings", height=14)
        hdrs   = dict(plate="Plate", cust="Customer", phone="Phone", type="Bay type",
                      date="Date", time="Time", mechanic="Mechanic",
                      notes="Notes", status="Status", bay="Bay")
        widths = dict(plate=80, cust=120, phone=100, type=110, date=88, time=52,
                      mechanic=100, notes=140, status=65, bay=50)
        for c in cols:
            self.ci_tree.heading(c, text=hdrs[c])
            self.ci_tree.column(c, width=widths[c], minwidth=36)
        self.ci_tree.tag_configure("booked",  background=C["info_bg"],    foreground=C["info"])
        self.ci_tree.tag_configure("arrived", background=C["warning_bg"], foreground=C["warning"])
        self.ci_tree.tag_configure("parked",  background=C["purple_bg"],  foreground=C["purple"])
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(panel, orient="vertical", command=self.ci_tree.yview)
        self.ci_tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        self.ci_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                          padx=(16, 0), pady=(4, 0), side="left")
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y", pady=(4, 0), padx=(0, 16))

        ab = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); ab.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8, side="bottom")
        Btn(ab, "✓  Check in & assign bay", cmd=self._ci_checkin,  style="success").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(ab, "↔  Move bay",              cmd=self._ci_move,     style="warning").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(ab, "🚪  Check out",             cmd=self._ci_checkout, style="danger").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(ab, "✏  Edit",                  cmd=self._ci_edit,     style="default").pack(side="left")

    def _refresh_checkin(self):
        q  = self.ci_q.get().lower()
        sf = self.ci_sf.get()
        self.ci_tree.delete(*self.ci_tree.get_children())
        for bk in sorted(self.bookings, key=lambda b: b.get("date", "")):
            if bk["status"] == "Cancelled": continue
            if sf == "Booked"     and bk["status"] != "Booked":  continue
            if sf == "Arrived"    and bk["status"] != "Arrived": continue
            if sf == "All active" and bk["status"] not in ("Booked","Arrived"): continue
            if q and q not in bk["plate"].lower() and q not in bk["cust_name"].lower():
                continue
            bay = f"Bay {bk['bay_num']}" if bk.get("bay_num") else "—"
            self.ci_tree.insert("", "end", iid=bk["id"],
                                tags=(bk["status"].lower(),),
                                values=(bk["plate"], bk["cust_name"],
                                        bk.get("cust_phone",""), bk["type"],
                                        fmt_date(bk.get("date","")),
                                        bk.get("time","—"),
                                        bk.get("mechanic","—"),
                                        bk.get("notes","")[:35],
                                        bk["status"], bay))

    def _ci_edit(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.ci_tree)
        if bk: self._booking_edit_win(bk)

    def _ci_checkin(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.ci_tree)
        if not bk: return
        if bk["status"] == "Arrived":
            self._toast("Already checked in. Use 'Move bay' to reassign.", "info"); return
        if bk["status"] != "Booked":
            self._toast("Only 'Booked' bookings can be checked in.", "info"); return
        self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=True)

    def _ci_move(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.ci_tree)
        if not bk: return
        if bk["status"] != "Arrived":
            self._toast("Only 'Arrived' cars can be moved.", "info"); return
        self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=False)

    def _ci_checkout(self):
        bk = self._get_bk(self.ci_tree)
        if not bk: return
        self._checkout(bk)

    def _checkout(self, bk):
        if bk["status"] not in ("Arrived", "Booked"):
            self._toast("This booking cannot be checked out.", "info"); return
        win = self._modal("Check out", w=440, h=290)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1, uniform="co")
        tk.Label(f, text="Check out", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(f, text=f"{bk['plate']}  ·  {bk['cust_name']}",
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).grid(
            row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 10))
        e_cost  = lbl_entry(f, "Actual cost (INR )", 2, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_cost.insert(0, str(bk.get("est_cost", "0")))
        e_notes = lbl_entry(f, "Completion notes", 2, 1, bg_key="surface")

        def do_co():
            try:    bk["actual_cost"] = float(e_cost.get())
            except: bk["actual_cost"] = 0.0
            bk["checkout_notes"] = e_notes.get().strip()
            bk["status"]       = "Checked out"
            bk["checked_out"]  = now_str()
            self._free_bay(bk)
            job = next((j for j in self.jobs
                        if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), None)
            if job:
                job["status"] = "Complete"
                job["completed_at"] = now_str()
            self._save()

            # Promote next in queue for this bay type
            self._promote_from_queue(bk.get("type",""))

            if bk.get("cust_email"):
                ws  = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "")
                ref = bk.get("ref", bk["id"][:8].upper())
                subject = f"Thank you — vehicle collected [{ref}] — {ws}"
                body = (f"Dear {bk['cust_name']},\n\n"
                        f"Thank you for choosing {ws}!\n\n"
                        f"Booking Ref: {ref}\n"
                        f"Vehicle: {bk['plate']} {bk.get('car_desc','')}\n"
                        f"Service: {bk.get('service', bk['type'])}\n"
                        f"Total charged: INR {bk['actual_cost']:.2f}\n\n"
                        f"We hope to see you again. Drive safely!\n\n"
                        f"Kind regards,\n{ws}")
                send_email_async(self.settings, bk["cust_email"], subject, body,
                                 callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                                     "Thank-you email sent!" if not e else f"Email: {e}",
                                     "success" if not e else "warning"))
            win.destroy()
            self._toast(f"{bk['plate']} checked out.", "success")
            self.refresh_all()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=4, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="e", pady=(14, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel",           cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Confirm checkout", cmd=do_co, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _assign_bay_win(self, bk, is_checkin: bool):
        title = "Check in & assign bay" if is_checkin else "Move to different bay"
        win = self._modal(title, w=500, h=420)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        tk.Label(f, text=title, font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 4))
        tk.Label(f,
                 text=(f"{bk['cust_name']}  ·  {bk['plate']}  ·  "
                       f"{fmt_date(bk.get('date',''))}  {bk.get('time','')}"),
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 4))

        if bk.get("notes"):
            tk.Label(f, text=f"📝 {bk['notes'][:60]}",
                     font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                     wraplength=440, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))

        tk.Label(f, text="BAY TYPE", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
        cb_type = ttk.Combobox(f, values=self.all_bay_types,
                               state="readonly", font=F(11))
        cb_type.set(bk["type"]); cb_type.pack(fill="x", pady=(2, 12))

        tk.Label(f, text="AVAILABLE BAY", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
        cb_bay = ttk.Combobox(f, state="readonly", font=F(11))
        cb_bay.pack(fill="x", pady=(2, 6))
        avail_lbl = tk.Label(f, text="", font=F(9),
                             fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"])
        avail_lbl.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 14))

        bay_map: dict = {}

        def refresh_bay_list(*_):
            bay_map.clear()
            sel_type = cb_type.get()
            for b in sorted(self.bays, key=lambda x: x["num"]):
                if b["type"] != sel_type or b.get("maintenance"): continue
                is_curr = (not is_checkin and b["id"] == bk.get("bay_id"))
                if b["status"] == "Available" or is_curr:
                    lbl = f"Bay {b['num']}" + ("  (current)" if is_curr else "")
                    bay_map[lbl] = b
            opts = list(bay_map.keys())
            cb_bay["values"] = opts
            if opts:
                if not is_checkin and bk.get("bay_id"):
                    curr = next((k for k, v in bay_map.items()
                                 if v["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
                    cb_bay.set(curr or opts[0])
                else:
                    cb_bay.set(opts[0])
                n = sum(1 for b in self.bays
                        if b["type"] == sel_type
                        and b["status"] == "Available"
                        and not b.get("maintenance"))
                avail_lbl.config(
                    text=f"{n} bay(s) available",
                    fg=C["success"] if n else C["danger"])
            else:
                cb_bay.set("No available bays")
                avail_lbl.config(
                    text=f"No available {sel_type} bays — car will join the queue.",
                    fg=C["danger"])

        cb_type.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", refresh_bay_list)
        refresh_bay_list()

        def do_assign():
            sel_type  = cb_type.get()
            sel_label = cb_bay.get()
            if not sel_type:
                self._toast("Select a bay type.", "error"); return
            if sel_label not in bay_map:
                # No bay — add to queue instead (no duplicates)
                plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
                if any(q["plate"].upper() == plate_up for q in self.queue):
                    self._toast(f"{bk['plate']} is already in the waiting queue.", "info")
                else:
                    self.queue.append(dict(
                        id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
                        plate=bk["plate"], cust_name=bk["cust_name"],
                        type=sel_type, notes=bk.get("notes",""),
                        added_at=now_str()))
                    bk["type"] = sel_type
                    self._save()
                    self._toast(
                        f"No bay available. {bk['plate']} added to the waiting queue.",
                        "warning")
                win.destroy(); self.refresh_all(); return

            new_bay = bay_map[sel_label]
            if bk.get("bay_id") and bk["bay_id"] != new_bay["id"]:
                old = next((b for b in self.bays if b["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
                if old:
                    old["status"] = "Available"
                    old["plate"]  = None
                    old["booking_id"] = None
            new_bay["status"]     = "Occupied"
            new_bay["plate"]      = bk["plate"]
            new_bay["booking_id"] = bk["id"]
            bk["type"]       = sel_type
            bk["bay_id"]     = new_bay["id"]
            bk["bay_num"]    = new_bay["num"]
            bk["status"]     = "Arrived"
            bk["checked_in"] = now_str()

            if is_checkin and not any(j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]
                                      for j in self.jobs):
                self.jobs.append(dict(
                    id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
                    plate=bk["plate"], cust_name=bk["cust_name"],
                    type=bk["type"], mechanic=bk.get("mechanic",""),
                    description=bk.get("notes",""),
                    status="Pending", parts=[], labour_notes="",
                    created_at=now_str(), completed_at=None))

            self._save(); win.destroy()
            verb = "checked in" if is_checkin else "moved"
            self._toast(
                f"{bk['plate']} {verb}  →  {sel_type}  Bay {new_bay['num']}",
                "success")
            self.refresh_all()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Cancel",     cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Assign bay", cmd=do_assign, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    # =========================================================================
    # WAITING QUEUE
    # =========================================================================

    def _promote_from_queue(self, bay_type: str):
        """When a bay frees, check the queue and notify the next car."""
        matching = [q for q in self.queue if q.get("type") == bay_type]
        if not matching: return
        next_q = matching[0]
        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings
                   if b["id"] == next_q.get("booking_id")), None)
        if bk:
            email = bk.get("cust_email", "")
            if email:
                subject = f"Bay available — {self.settings.get('workshop_name','')}"
                body = (f"Dear {bk['cust_name']},\n\n"
                        f"A {bay_type} bay is now available for your vehicle {bk['plate']}.\n"
                        f"Please come to reception.\n\n"
                        f"Thank you,\n{self.settings.get('workshop_name','')}")
                send_email_async(self.settings, email, subject, body)
        self._toast(
            f"Bay free! Notifying next in queue: {next_q['plate']}",
            "info")

    def _build_queue(self):
        panel = self.panels["queue"]
        tk.Label(panel, text="Waiting Queue", font=F(14, "bold"),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(padx=16, pady=(16, 4), anchor="w")
        tk.Label(panel,
                 text=("Cars added here when no bays are available. "
                       "When a bay frees up, the customer is emailed automatically."),
                 font=F(10), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"],
                 wraplength=800, justify="left").pack(padx=16, pady=(0, 8), anchor="w")

        cols = ("pos","plate","cust","type","notes","added")
        self.q_tree = ttk.Treeview(panel, columns=cols, show="headings", height=16)
        hdrs   = dict(pos="#", plate="Plate", cust="Customer",
                      type="Bay type", notes="Notes", added="Added at")
        widths = dict(pos=35, plate=90, cust=150, type=130, notes=220, added=130)
        for c in cols:
            self.q_tree.heading(c, text=hdrs[c])
            self.q_tree.column(c, width=widths[c], minwidth=30)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(panel, orient="vertical", command=self.q_tree.yview)
        self.q_tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        self.q_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                         padx=(16, 0), pady=(4, 0), side="left")
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y", pady=(4, 0), padx=(0, 16))

        ab = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); ab.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8, side="bottom")
        Btn(ab, "✓  Assign bay now",   cmd=self._q_assign,   style="success").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(ab, "↑  Move up",          cmd=lambda: self._q_move(-1), style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(ab, "↓  Move down",        cmd=lambda: self._q_move(1),  style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(ab, "🗑  Remove from queue", cmd=self._q_remove,  style="danger").pack(side="left")

    def _refresh_queue(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "q_tree"): return
        self.q_tree.delete(*self.q_tree.get_children())
        for pos, q in enumerate(self.queue, 1):
            self.q_tree.insert("", "end", iid=q["id"],
                               values=(pos, q["plate"], q["cust_name"],
                                       q["type"], q.get("notes","")[:40],
                                       q.get("added_at","—")))

    def _q_selected(self):
        sel = self.q_tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            self._toast("Select a queue entry first.", "info"); return None, -1
        qid = sel[0]
        idx = next((i for i, q in enumerate(self.queue) if q["id"] == qid), -1)
        return self.queue[idx] if idx >= 0 else None, idx

    def _q_assign(self):
        q, idx = self._q_selected()
        if q is None: return
        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings
                   if b["id"] == q.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not bk:
            self._toast("Booking not found.", "error"); return
        # Remove from queue then open assign window
        self.queue.pop(idx)
        self._save()
        self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=True)

    def _q_move(self, direction: int):
        q, idx = self._q_selected()
        if q is None: return
        new_idx = idx + direction
        if new_idx < 0 or new_idx >= len(self.queue):
            return
        self.queue.insert(new_idx, self.queue.pop(idx))
        self._save(); self._refresh_queue()
        self.q_tree.selection_set(q["id"])

    def _q_remove(self):
        q, idx = self._q_selected()
        if q is None: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Remove from queue",
                f"Remove {q['plate']} from the waiting queue?"): return
        self.queue.pop(idx)
        self._save(); self._refresh_queue()

    # =========================================================================
    # JOB CARDS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_jobs(self):
        panel = self.panels["jobs"]
        bar = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12, 4))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Search:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.jb_q = tk.StringVar()
        self.jb_q.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_jobs())
        tk.Entry(bar, textvariable=self.jb_q, font=F(10), relief="flat", bd=0,
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                 width=24).pack(side="left", padx=(4, 14))
        tk.Label(bar, text="Status:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.jb_sf = tk.StringVar(value="Active")
        ttk.Combobox(bar, textvariable=self.jb_sf, state="readonly",
                     values=["Active","All"] + JOB_STATUSES,
                     font=F(10), width=16).pack(side="left", padx=4)
        self.jb_sf.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_jobs())

        cols = ("plate","cust","type","mechanics","desc","status","parts","created")
        self.jb_tree = ttk.Treeview(panel, columns=cols, show="headings", height=14)
        hdrs   = dict(plate="Plate", cust="Customer", type="Service",
                      mechanics="Mechanic(s)", desc="Description",
                      status="Status", parts="Parts", created="Created")
        widths = dict(plate=80, cust=120, type=110, mechanics=120,
                      desc=160, status=110, parts=55, created=110)
        for c in cols:
            self.jb_tree.heading(c, text=hdrs[c])
            self.jb_tree.column(c, width=widths[c], minwidth=40)
        for st,(bk,fk) in JOB_STATUS_COLOURS.items():
            self.jb_tree.tag_configure(st.lower(), background=C[bk], foreground=C[fk])
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(panel, orient="vertical", command=self.jb_tree.yview)
        self.jb_tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        self.jb_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                          padx=(16, 0), pady=(4, 0), side="left")
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y", pady=(4, 0), padx=(0, 16))

        ab = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); ab.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8, side="bottom")
        Btn(ab, "✏  Edit job",          cmd=self._edit_job,                                        style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "▶  Start",             cmd=lambda: self._set_job_status("In Progress"),           style="info").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "✓  Ready",             cmd=lambda: self._set_job_status("Ready for Delivery"),    style="success").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "🚗  Move bay",          cmd=self._jb_move_bay,                                    style="warning").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "👷  Add mechanic",      cmd=self._jb_add_mechanic,                                style="purple").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "🖨  Invoice",           cmd=self._print_job_card,                                  style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        Btn(ab, "🗑  Delete",             cmd=self._delete_job,                                     style="danger").pack(side="left")

    def _refresh_jobs(self):
        q  = self.jb_q.get().lower()
        sf = self.jb_sf.get()
        done = {"Delivered","Cancelled"}
        self.jb_tree.delete(*self.jb_tree.get_children())
        for j in sorted(self.jobs,
                        key=lambda x: x.get("created_at",""), reverse=True):
            if sf == "Active" and j["status"] in done: continue
            if sf not in ("Active","All") and j["status"] != sf: continue
            if q and q not in j["plate"].lower() and q not in j["cust_name"].lower():
                continue
            parts_count = len(j.get("parts", []))
            # Show all mechanics
            mechs = j.get("mechanics", [])
            if not mechs and j.get("mechanic"):
                mechs = [j["mechanic"]]
            mechs_str = ", ".join(mechs) if mechs else "—"
            self.jb_tree.insert("", "end", iid=j["id"],
                                tags=(j["status"].lower(),),
                                values=(j["plate"], j["cust_name"],
                                        j.get("type",""),
                                        mechs_str,
                                        j.get("description","")[:50],
                                        j["status"],
                                        f"{parts_count} part(s)",
                                        j.get("created_at","—")[:11]))

    def _get_job(self):
        sel = self.jb_tree.selection()
        if not sel:
            self._toast("Select a job first.", "info"); return None
        return next((j for j in self.jobs if j["id"] == sel[0]), None)

    def _set_job_status(self, status):
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return

        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == j.get("booking_id")), None)
        ws  = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "")
        ref = (bk or {}).get("ref", j.get("id","")[:8].upper())

        # When marking In Progress — ask to assign bay if not already in one
        if status == "In Progress" and bk and not bk.get("bay_id"):
            if messagebox.askyesno("Assign bay",
                    f"{(bk or {}).get('plate','Vehicle')} has no bay assigned.\n"
                    "Assign a bay now before starting work?"):
                j["status"] = status
                j["started_at"] = now_str()
                bk["status"] = "Booked"
                self._save()
                self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=True)
                return

        j["status"] = status
        if status == "In Progress":       j["started_at"]   = now_str()
        if status == "Ready for Delivery":j["completed_at"] = now_str()
        if status == "Delivered":         j["delivered_at"] = now_str()

        # Ready for Delivery — ask to select a bay for parked car
        if status == "Ready for Delivery" and bk:
            if bk.get("bay_id"):
                bay = next((b for b in self.bays if b["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
                if bay:
                    bay["status"] = "Available"; bay["plate"] = None; bay["booking_id"] = None
            bk["bay_id"] = None; bk["bay_num"] = None
            # Ask user to assign a parking bay
            if messagebox.askyesno("Assign waiting bay",
                    "Job marked Ready for Delivery.\n"
                    "Do you want to move the car to a specific waiting bay?"):
                bk["status"] = "Booked"
                self._save(); self.refresh_all()
                self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=True)
                return
            else:
                bk["status"] = "Arrived"

        self._save(); self.refresh_all()
        self._toast(f"Job: {status}.", "success")

        # ── Emails ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        email = (bk or {}).get("cust_email", "") or ""
        mechs = j.get("mechanics") or ([j["mechanic"]] if j.get("mechanic") else [])
        mechs_str = ", ".join(mechs) or "—"
        if email and status == "In Progress":
            subj = f"Work started on your vehicle [{ref}] — {ws}"
            body = (f"Dear {j['cust_name']},\n\n"
                    f"We have started work on your vehicle {j['plate']}.\n\n"
                    f"Booking Ref : {ref}\n"
                    f"Service     : {j.get('type','')}\n"
                    f"Mechanic(s) : {mechs_str}\n\n"
                    f"We'll notify you when it's ready for collection.\n\n"
                    f"Thank you,\n{ws}")
            send_email_async(self.settings, email, subj, body,
                callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                    "Job-started email sent!" if not e else f"Email: {e}",
                    "success" if not e else "warning"))
        elif email and status == "Ready for Delivery" and bk:
            include_photos = j.get("include_photos_in_email", False)
            send_invoice_email(j, bk, self.settings,
                               include_photos=include_photos)
            msg = "Invoice + photos emailed." if include_photos and j.get("photos") else "Invoice emailed."
            self._toast(msg, "info")

    def _delete_job(self):
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete job", "Permanently delete this job card?"): return
        self.jobs = [x for x in self.jobs if x["id"] != j["id"]]
        self._save(); self.refresh_all()

    def _jb_move_bay(self):
        """Move bay from Job Cards tab."""
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == j.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not bk:
            self._toast("No booking linked to this job.", "error"); return
        if bk.get("status") not in ("Arrived","Booked"):
            self._toast("Car must be checked in to move bays.", "info"); return
        self._assign_bay_win(bk, is_checkin=False)

    def _jb_add_mechanic(self):
        """Add a mechanic to a job mid-work."""
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        if not self.mechanics:
            self._toast("No mechanics added yet.", "info"); return
        win = self._modal("Add mechanic to job", w=420, h=260)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text=f"Job: {j['plate']} — {j.get('type','')}",
                 font=F(12,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))
        # Current mechanics
        current = j.get("mechanics", [])
        if not current and j.get("mechanic"):
            current = [j["mechanic"]]
        tk.Label(f, text=f"Current: {', '.join(current) or '—'}",
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,10))
        tk.Label(f, text="ADD MECHANIC", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,4))
        mech_opts = [m["name"] for m in self.mechanics if m["name"] not in current]
        if not mech_opts:
            tk.Label(f, text="All mechanics already assigned.",
                     font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")
            Btn(f, "Close", cmd=win.destroy).pack(anchor="e", pady=(16,0))
            return
        cb = ttk.Combobox(f, values=mech_opts, state="readonly", font=F(11))
        cb.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,12)); cb.set(mech_opts[0])
        def add():
            name = cb.get()
            if not name: return
            j.setdefault("mechanics", list(current))
            if name not in j["mechanics"]:
                j["mechanics"].append(name)
            j["mechanic"] = j["mechanics"][0]
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast(f"{name} added to job.", "success")
            self.refresh_all()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(foot, "Add mechanic", cmd=add, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _email_job_update(self):
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        bk     = next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == j.get("booking_id")), None)
        email  = bk.get("cust_email","") if bk else ""
        subject = f"Job update — {j['plate']} — {self.settings.get('workshop_name','')}"
        parts_txt = "\n".join(
            f"  - {p['name']} x{p.get('qty',1)}" for p in j.get("parts",[])
        ) or "  None"
        body = (f"Dear {j['cust_name']},\n\nUpdate on your vehicle {j['plate']}:\n\n"
                f"Status: {j['status']}\nService: {j['type']}\n"
                f"Mechanic: {j.get('mechanic','—')}\n"
                f"Parts used:\n{parts_txt}\n\n"
                f"Notes: {j.get('labour_notes','')}\n\n"
                f"Thank you,\n{self.settings.get('workshop_name','')}")
        send_email_async(self.settings, email, subject, body,
                         callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                             "Update sent!" if not e else f"Email: {e}",
                             "success" if not e else "error"))
        self._toast("Sending update...", "info")

    def _print_job_card(self):
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        bk = next((b for b in self.bookings if b["id"] == j.get("booking_id")), None)
        export_job_card_pdf(j, bk, self.settings, self)

    def _edit_job(self):
        j = self._get_job()
        if not j: return
        win = self._modal("Edit job card", w=660, h=680)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1, uniform="ej")

        tk.Label(f, text=f"Job card — {j['plate']}", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 8))

        # Multi-mechanic
        tk.Label(f, text="MECHANIC(S)", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(8,2))
        mech_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); mech_frame.grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="ew")
        # Current mechanics list
        cur_mechs = list(j.get("mechanics", []))
        if not cur_mechs and j.get("mechanic"):
            cur_mechs = [j["mechanic"]]
        mech_list_var = tk.StringVar(value=", ".join(cur_mechs) or "None assigned")
        mech_list_lbl = tk.Label(mech_frame, textvariable=mech_list_var,
                                  font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                                  anchor="w", wraplength=240)
        mech_list_lbl.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        avail_mechs = [m["name"] for m in self.mechanics]
        def add_mech_to_job():
            name = cb_add_mech.get()
            if not name or name == "— Add —": return
            if name not in cur_mechs:
                cur_mechs.append(name)
                mech_list_var.set(", ".join(cur_mechs))
        def rem_mech_from_job():
            name = cb_add_mech.get()
            if name in cur_mechs:
                cur_mechs.remove(name)
                mech_list_var.set(", ".join(cur_mechs) or "None assigned")
        cb_add_mech = ttk.Combobox(mech_frame, values=["— Add —"]+avail_mechs,
                                    state="readonly", font=F(9), width=14)
        cb_add_mech.pack(side="left", padx=(6,3)); cb_add_mech.set("— Add —")
        Btn(mech_frame, "+", cmd=add_mech_to_job, style="success", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,2))
        Btn(mech_frame, "−", cmd=rem_mech_from_job, style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")

        # Status — row 3, spans both columns
        tk.Label(f, text="JOB STATUS", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=3, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(10,2))
        cb_status = ttk.Combobox(f, values=JOB_STATUSES, state="readonly", font=F(11))
        cb_status.grid(row=4, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(0,6))
        cb_status.set(j.get("status", JOB_STATUSES[0]))

        # Payment status — row 5
        tk.Label(f, text="PAYMENT STATUS", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=5, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(4,2))
        cb_payment = ttk.Combobox(f, values=["Outstanding","Partially Paid","Paid"],
                                   state="readonly", font=F(11))
        cb_payment.grid(row=6, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(0,6))
        cb_payment.set(j.get("payment_status","Outstanding"))

        tk.Label(f, text="DESCRIPTION", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=7, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1))
        t_desc = tk.Text(f, height=3, font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        t_desc.grid(row=8, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew")
        t_desc.insert("1.0", j.get("description",""))

        tk.Label(f, text="LABOUR NOTES", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=9, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(8, 1))
        t_labour = tk.Text(f, height=3, font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                           bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                           highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        t_labour.grid(row=10, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew")
        t_labour.insert("1.0", j.get("labour_notes",""))

        # ── PARTS & CHARGES ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        tk.Label(f, text="PARTS & CHARGES", font=F(8, "bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=11, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(10, 2))

        # Simple frame for parts list (no canvas — avoids Mac Tk segfault)
        parts_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["bg"])
        parts_frame.grid(row=12, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew")

        # Track price entries so we can collect them on save
        price_vars = []  # list of (idx, type, tk.StringVar)

        def refresh_parts():
            nonlocal price_vars
            price_vars.clear()
            for w in parts_frame.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
            # Header
            hdr = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["bg"]); hdr.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,2))
            for txt, w in [("Item", 220),("Qty",40),("Unit price (INR )",100),("Total",80),("",50)]:
                tk.Label(hdr, text=txt, font=F(8,"bold"),
                         fg=C["muted"], bg=C["bg"], width=w//8, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
            tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(0,3))

            # Parts from inventory
            for idx, pt in enumerate(j.get("parts", [])):
                pr = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["bg"]); pr.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
                tk.Label(pr, text=f"{pt.get('name','?')} ×{pt.get('qty',1)}",
                         font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                         width=28, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                qty   = pt.get("qty", 1)
                # invoice_price is per-job override; falls back to inventory sell_price
                cur_p = pt.get("invoice_price",
                         pt.get("unit_price",
                         next((i.get("sell_price",0) for i in self.inventory
                               if i["id"]==pt.get("inv_id")), 0)))
                pv = tk.StringVar(value=f"{float(cur_p):.2f}")
                price_vars.append((idx, "part", pv))
                pe = tk.Entry(pr, textvariable=pv, font=F(9), width=9,
                              relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                              highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                pe.pack(side="left", padx=(4,4))
                def _upd_total(var=pv, q=qty, row=pr):
                    try:
                        t = float(var.get()) * q
                        for w in row.winfo_children():
                            if getattr(w,"_is_total",False):
                                w.config(text=f"INR {t:.2f}")
                    except: pass
                pv.trace_add("write", lambda *a, fn=_upd_total: fn())
                tot_lbl = tk.Label(pr, text=f"INR {float(cur_p)*qty:.2f}",
                                   font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], width=8, anchor="e")
                tot_lbl._is_total = True
                tot_lbl.pack(side="left", padx=(4,8))
                Btn(pr, "✕", cmd=lambda i=idx: remove_part(i),
                    style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")

            # Service charges
            for idx, sc in enumerate(j.get("service_charges", [])):
                sr = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["bg"]); sr.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
                tk.Label(sr, text=sc.get("name","Service charge"),
                         font=F(9), bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                         width=28, anchor="w", padx=4).pack(side="left")
                sv = tk.StringVar(value=f"{float(sc.get('price',0)):.2f}")
                price_vars.append((idx, "svc", sv))
                se = tk.Entry(sr, textvariable=sv, font=F(9), width=9,
                              relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                              highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                se.pack(side="left", padx=(4,4))
                sv_lbl = tk.Label(sr, text="", font=F(9), bg=C["bg"],
                                  fg=C["text"], width=8, anchor="e")
                sv_lbl.pack(side="left", padx=(4,8))
                def _upd_sv(var=sv, lbl=sv_lbl):
                    try: lbl.config(text=f"INR {float(var.get()):.2f}")
                    except: pass
                sv.trace_add("write", lambda *a, fn=_upd_sv: fn())
                _upd_sv()
                Btn(sr, "✕", cmd=lambda i=idx: remove_svc(i),
                    style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")

            # Grand total
            if j.get("parts") or j.get("service_charges"):
                tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(4,2))
                tot_row = tk.Frame(parts_frame, bg=C["bg"]); tot_row.pack(fill="x")
                tk.Label(tot_row, text="TOTAL", font=F(10,"bold"),
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="right", padx=(0,62))
                total_val = sum(
                    float(pt.get("invoice_price", pt.get("unit_price",
                          next((i.get("sell_price",0) for i in self.inventory
                                if i["id"]==pt.get("inv_id")),0)))) * pt.get("qty",1)
                    for pt in j.get("parts",[])
                ) + sum(float(sc.get("price",0)) for sc in j.get("service_charges",[]))
                tk.Label(tot_row, text=f"INR {total_val:,.2f}", font=F(10,"bold"),
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["success"]).pack(side="right", padx=(0,8))

        # ── Add from inventory row ─────────────────────────────────────────
        add_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        add_row.grid(row=13, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(6, 2))
        tk.Label(add_row, text="Add from inventory:", font=F(9,"bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        inv_names = ["— Select item —"] + [i["name"] for i in self.inventory]
        cb_inv = ttk.Combobox(add_row, values=inv_names, state="readonly",
                              font=F(9), width=24)
        cb_inv.pack(side="left", padx=(0,4)); cb_inv.set(inv_names[0])
        tk.Label(add_row, text="Qty:", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_qty = tk.Entry(add_row, font=F(9), width=4, relief="flat", bd=0,
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                         highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_qty.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_qty.insert(0,"1")
        Btn(add_row, "+ Add", cmd=lambda: add_part(), style="success", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))

        # ── Add custom item row ────────────────────────────────────────────
        cust_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        cust_row.grid(row=14, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(2, 2))
        tk.Label(cust_row, text="Add custom item:", font=F(9,"bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        e_cust_name = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=20, relief="flat", bd=0,
                               bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                               highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cust_name.pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        e_cust_name.insert(0, "Item name")
        e_cust_name.bind("<FocusIn>", lambda e: (e_cust_name.delete(0,"end")
                         if e_cust_name.get()=="Item name" else None))
        tk.Label(cust_row, text="Qty:", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_cust_qty = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=4, relief="flat", bd=0,
                              bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                              highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cust_qty.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_cust_qty.insert(0,"1")
        tk.Label(cust_row, text="INR", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_cust_price = tk.Entry(cust_row, font=F(9), width=7, relief="flat", bd=0,
                                bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                                highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_cust_price.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_cust_price.insert(0,"0")
        Btn(cust_row, "+ Add", cmd=lambda: add_custom(), style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))

        # ── Service charge row ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        svc_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        svc_row.grid(row=15, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(2, 4))
        tk.Label(svc_row, text="Add service charge:", font=F(9,"bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        e_svc_name = tk.Entry(svc_row, font=F(9), width=18, relief="flat", bd=0,
                              bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                              highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_svc_name.pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        e_svc_name.insert(0, "Service charge")
        tk.Label(svc_row, text="INR", font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        e_svc_price = tk.Entry(svc_row, font=F(9), width=8, relief="flat", bd=0,
                               bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                               highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e_svc_price.pack(side="left", padx=(2,4)); e_svc_price.insert(0,"0")
        Btn(svc_row, "+ Add", cmd=lambda: add_svc_charge(), style="purple", small=True).pack(side="left")

        def add_part():
            inv_idx = cb_inv.current()
            if inv_idx <= 0: return
            inv_item = self.inventory[inv_idx - 1]
            try:    qty = int(e_qty.get())
            except: qty = 1
            if inv_item["qty"] < qty:
                self._toast(f"Only {inv_item['qty']} in stock.", "error"); return
            inv_item["qty"] -= qty
            j.setdefault("parts",[]).append(dict(
                inv_id=inv_item["id"], name=inv_item["name"], qty=qty,
                unit_price=inv_item.get("sell_price",0),
                invoice_price=inv_item.get("sell_price",0)))
            refresh_parts()

        def add_custom():
            name = e_cust_name.get().strip()
            if not name or name == "Item name": return
            try:    qty   = int(e_cust_qty.get())
            except: qty   = 1
            try:    price = float(e_cust_price.get())
            except: price = 0.0
            j.setdefault("parts",[]).append(dict(
                inv_id=None, name=name, qty=qty,
                unit_price=price, invoice_price=price))
            e_cust_name.delete(0,"end"); e_cust_name.insert(0,"Item name")
            e_cust_qty.delete(0,"end"); e_cust_qty.insert(0,"1")
            e_cust_price.delete(0,"end"); e_cust_price.insert(0,"0")
            refresh_parts()

        def add_svc_charge():
            name = e_svc_name.get().strip() or "Service charge"
            try:    price = float(e_svc_price.get())
            except: price = 0.0
            j.setdefault("service_charges",[]).append(dict(name=name, price=price))
            e_svc_price.delete(0,"end"); e_svc_price.insert(0,"0")
            refresh_parts()

        def remove_part(idx):
            pt = j["parts"].pop(idx)
            # Restore stock only if from inventory
            if pt.get("inv_id"):
                inv_item = next((i for i in self.inventory
                                 if i["id"] == pt["inv_id"]), None)
                if inv_item: inv_item["qty"] += pt.get("qty", 1)
            refresh_parts()

        def remove_svc(idx):
            j.get("service_charges",[]).pop(idx)
            refresh_parts()

        refresh_parts()

        def save():
            # Collect edited prices from price_vars before saving
            for (idx, typ, pv) in price_vars:
                try: val = float(pv.get())
                except: val = 0.0
                if typ == "part" and idx < len(j.get("parts",[])):
                    j["parts"][idx]["invoice_price"] = val
