                elif typ == "svc" and idx < len(j.get("service_charges",[])):
                    j["service_charges"][idx]["price"] = val
            j["mechanics"]       = list(cur_mechs)
            j["mechanic"]        = cur_mechs[0] if cur_mechs else ""
            j["status"]          = cb_status.get()
            j["payment_status"]  = cb_payment.get()
            if j["status"] in ("Ready for Delivery","Delivered") and not j.get("completed_at"):
                j["completed_at"] = now_str()
            j["description"]  = t_desc.get("1.0","end").strip()
            j["labour_notes"] = t_labour.get("1.0","end").strip()
            j["include_photos_in_email"] = self._include_photos_var.get()
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast("Job updated.", "success")
            self.refresh_all()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        # ── Photos section ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        tk.Label(f, text="PHOTOS (before/after)", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).grid(
            row=17, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(10,2))

        photos_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["bg"]); photos_frame.grid(
            row=18, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew")

        def refresh_photos():
            for w in photos_frame.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
            photos = j.get("photos", [])
            if not photos:
                tk.Label(photos_frame, text="No photos yet.", font=F(9),
                         bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", padx=4, pady=4)
                return
            grid = tk.Frame(photos_frame, bg=C["bg"]); grid.pack(fill="x", padx=4)
            for idx, ph in enumerate(photos):
                col_frame = tk.Frame(grid, bg=C["surface"],
                                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                col_frame.grid(row=0, column=idx, padx=(0,6), pady=4, sticky="n")
                # Thumbnail
                b64 = ph.get("data","")
                if b64 and _PIL:
                    try:
                        import io as _bio
                        raw = base64.b64decode(b64)
                        img = PILImage.open(_bio.BytesIO(raw))
                        img.thumbnail((120,90))
                        photo_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
                        lbl = tk.Label(col_frame, image=photo_img,
                                       bg=C["surface"])
                        lbl.image = photo_img
                        lbl.pack(padx=4, pady=(4,2))
                    except Exception:
                        tk.Label(col_frame, text="🖼", font=F(20),
                                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(padx=8, pady=8)
                else:
                    tk.Label(col_frame, text="🖼", font=F(20),
                             bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(padx=8, pady=8)
                # Label
                tk.Label(col_frame, text=ph.get("label","Photo"),
                         font=F(8,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["info"],
                         padx=4).pack(anchor="w")
                # Description
                desc_var = tk.StringVar(value=ph.get("description",""))
                de = tk.Entry(col_frame, textvariable=desc_var, font=F(8),
                              relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                              insertbackground=C["text"], width=16,
                              highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
                de.pack(padx=4, pady=(0,2))
                def save_desc(var=desc_var, i=idx):
                    j["photos"][i]["description"] = var.get()
                de.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda e, fn=save_desc: fn())
                Btn(col_frame, "✕ Remove",
                    cmd=lambda i=idx: (j["photos"].pop(i), refresh_photos()),
                    style="danger", small=True).pack(pady=(0,4), padx=4)

        def add_photo(label="Photo"):
            path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
                title=f"Select {label}",
                filetypes=[("Images","*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.gif *.bmp"),
                           ("All","*.*")])
            if not path: return
            try:
                import io as _bio
                img = PILImage.open(path) if _PIL else None
                if img:
                    img.thumbnail((1200,900))
                    buf = _bio.BytesIO()
                    img.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=80)
                    b64 = base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode()
                else:
                    with open(path,"rb") as fh:
                        b64 = base64.b64encode(fh.read()).decode()
                j.setdefault("photos",[]).append(
                    dict(label=label, data=b64, description=""))
                refresh_photos()
            except Exception as e:
                self._toast(f"Photo error: {e}", "error")

        photo_btns = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); photo_btns.grid(
            row=19, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(4,0))
        Btn(photo_btns, "📷 Add Before photo",
            cmd=lambda: add_photo("Before"), style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(photo_btns, "📷 Add After photo",
            cmd=lambda: add_photo("After"),  style="success", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(photo_btns, "📷 Add photo",
            cmd=lambda: add_photo("Photo"),  style="default", small=True).pack(side="left")

        # Include photos PDF checkbox
        self._include_photos_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=j.get("include_photos_in_email", False))
        chk_row = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); chk_row.grid(
            row=20, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(6,0))
        tk.Checkbutton(chk_row, text="Include photos PDF in email when job is complete",
                       variable=self._include_photos_var,
                       font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                       selectcolor=C["bg"], activebackground=C["surface"],
                       activeforeground=C["text"]).pack(side="left")

        refresh_photos()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=21, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="e", pady=(8, 0))
        Btn(foot, "👁 Preview photos", cmd=lambda: self._preview_photos(j),
            style="purple").pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    # =========================================================================
    # SCHEDULE
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_schedule(self):
        panel = self.panels["schedule"]
        top = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); top.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12, 4))
        tk.Label(top, text="Week of:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.sched_date = tk.StringVar(value=today_str())
        e = tk.Entry(top, textvariable=self.sched_date, font=F(10), width=14,
                     relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e.pack(side="left", padx=(4, 4))
        tk.Button(top, text="📅", font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                  bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"], cursor="hand2",
                  activebackground=C["border"],
                  command=lambda: DatePicker._open(self, e)).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(top, "Show week",   cmd=self._refresh_schedule,        style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(top, "← Prev week", cmd=lambda: self._shift_week(-7),  style="default").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(top, "Next week →", cmd=lambda: self._shift_week(7),   style="default").pack(side="left")
        self.sched_frame = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        self.sched_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=16, pady=(4, 16))

    def _shift_week(self, days):
        try:
            d = datetime.strptime(self.sched_date.get(), "%Y-%m-%d").date() + timedelta(days=days)
            self.sched_date.set(d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"))
            self._refresh_schedule()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _refresh_schedule(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "sched_frame"): return
        for w in self.sched_frame.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        try:    anchor = datetime.strptime(self.sched_date.get(), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except: anchor = date.today()
        week  = [anchor + timedelta(days=i) for i in range(7)]
        ncols = len(week) + 1
        self.sched_frame.columnconfigure(list(range(ncols)), weight=1, uniform="sc")
        tk.Label(self.sched_frame, text="Bay type", font=F(9, "bold"),
                 bg=C["border"], fg=C["text"],
                 relief="flat", pady=6).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=1, pady=1)
        for ci, d in enumerate(week):
            label = d.strftime("%a\n%d %b")
            bg = C["info_bg"] if d == date.today() else C["border"]
            fg = C["info"]    if d == date.today() else C["text"]
            tk.Label(self.sched_frame, text=label, font=F(9, "bold"),
                     bg=bg, fg=fg, relief="flat", pady=6).grid(
                row=0, column=ci+1, sticky="ew", padx=1, pady=1)
        for ri, bay_type in enumerate(self.all_bay_types):
            tk.Label(self.sched_frame, text=bay_type, font=F(9),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                     anchor="w", padx=6, pady=4).grid(
                row=ri+1, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=1, pady=1)
            for ci, d in enumerate(week):
                ds      = d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                day_bks = [b for b in self.bookings
                           if b.get("date") == ds
                           and b["type"] == bay_type
                           and b["status"] != "Cancelled"]
                bg  = (C["bg"] if not day_bks else
                       (C["danger_bg"] if len(day_bks) >= 2 else C["warning_bg"]))
                txt = "\n".join(b["plate"] for b in day_bks) if day_bks else ""
                tk.Label(self.sched_frame, text=txt, font=F(8),
                         bg=bg, fg=C["text"], anchor="nw",
                         padx=4, pady=4, justify="left",
                         relief="flat", wraplength=80).grid(
                    row=ri+1, column=ci+1,
                    sticky="nsew", padx=1, pady=1)

    # =========================================================================
    # CUSTOMERS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_customers(self):
        panel = self.panels["customers"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        p = sf.inner
        self._section(p, "Add customer")
        card = self._card(p); card.columnconfigure((0,1,2), weight=1, uniform="cc")
        self.cu_name  = lbl_entry(card, "Full name", 0, 0, bg_key="surface")
        self.cu_phone = lbl_entry(card, "Phone",     0, 1, bg_key="surface")
        self.cu_email = lbl_entry(card, "Email",     0, 2, bg_key="surface")
        br = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"])
        br.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=(10, 0))
        Btn(br, "Add customer", cmd=self._add_customer, style="primary").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Frame(br, width=1, bg=C["border"]).pack(side="left", fill="y", padx=(0, 8))
        tk.Label(br, text="Global search:", font=F(10),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        self.gs_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.gs_var.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_customers())
        tk.Entry(br, textvariable=self.gs_var, font=F(10), width=22,
                 relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                 highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"]).pack(
            side="left", padx=(4, 0))
        self._section(p, "Customer database")
        self.cu_list = tk.Frame(p, bg=C["bg"])
        self.cu_list.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(4, 20))

    def _add_customer(self):
        name  = self.cu_name.get().strip()
        phone = self.cu_phone.get().strip()
        email = self.cu_email.get().strip()
        if not name: self._toast("Please enter a name.", "error"); return
        self.customers.append(dict(id=uid(), name=name, phone=phone, email=email,
                                   cars=[], created_at=now_str()))
        self._save()
        for w in [self.cu_name, self.cu_phone, self.cu_email]: w.delete(0, "end")
        self._toast(f'Customer "{name}" added.', "success")
        self.refresh_all()

    def _refresh_customers(self):
        for w in self.cu_list.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        q = (self.gs_var.get() if hasattr(self, "gs_var") else "").lower()
        filtered = [c for c in self.customers
                    if not q
                    or q in c["name"].lower()
                    or q in c.get("phone","").lower()
                    or q in c.get("email","").lower()
                    or any(q in v["plate"].lower() for v in c.get("cars",[]))]
        if not filtered:
            tk.Label(self.cu_list, text="No customers found.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(pady=20); return
        for cust in filtered: self._render_cust_card(cust)

    def _render_cust_card(self, cust):
        outer = tk.Frame(self.cu_list, bg=C["border"])
        outer.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 7))
        card = tk.Frame(outer, bg=C["surface"])
        card.pack(fill="x", padx=1, pady=1)

        hdr = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"]); hdr.pack(fill="x", padx=14, pady=(12, 6))
        initials = "".join(w[0] for w in cust["name"].split() if w)[:2].upper()
        tk.Label(hdr, text=initials, font=F(11, "bold"),
                 bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                 padx=10, pady=4).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))
        ic = tk.Frame(hdr, bg=C["surface"]); ic.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        tk.Label(ic, text=cust["name"], font=F(11, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w")
        contact = "  ·  ".join(filter(None, [cust.get("phone",""), cust.get("email","")]))
        tk.Label(ic, text=contact or "No contact info", font=F(9),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")
        acts = tk.Frame(hdr, bg=C["surface"]); acts.pack(side="right")
        Btn(acts, "📋 History", cmd=lambda c=cust: self._show_cust_history(c), small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(acts, "✏ Edit",    cmd=lambda c=cust: self._edit_customer(c),     small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(acts, "🗑 Delete",  cmd=lambda c=cust: self._delete_customer(c),   small=True, style="danger").pack(side="left")

        vf = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"]); vf.pack(fill="x", padx=14, pady=(0, 12))
        tk.Label(vf, text=f"VEHICLES ({len(cust.get('cars',[]))})",
                 font=F(8, "bold"), fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 4))
        for car in cust.get("cars", []):
            row = tk.Frame(vf, bg=C["bg"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
            tk.Label(row, text=car["plate"], font=F(9, "bold"),
                     bg=C["border"], fg=C["text"],
                     padx=8, pady=2).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
            desc = "  ·  ".join(filter(None, [
                car.get("model",""),
                str(car["year"]) if car.get("year") else "",
                car.get("color","")]))
            tk.Label(row, text=desc or "—", font=F(9),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
            Btn(row, "📋",
                cmd=lambda c=cust, vid=car["id"]: self._show_vehicle_history(c, vid),
                small=True).pack(side="right", padx=(0, 4))
            Btn(row, "🗑",
                cmd=lambda c=cust, vid=car["id"]: self._delete_car(c, vid),
                style="danger", small=True).pack(side="right", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(vf, "+ Add vehicle",
            cmd=lambda c=cust: self._add_car_win(c), small=True).pack(anchor="w", pady=(6, 0))

    def _show_cust_history(self, cust):
        win = self._modal(f"Booking history — {cust['name']}", w=700, h=500)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=16, pady=16)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text=f"All bookings for {cust['name']}", font=F(12, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
        cols = ("plate","type","date","status","cost","bay")
        tree = ttk.Treeview(f, columns=cols, show="headings", height=15)
        for c, lbl, w in [("plate","Plate",80),("type","Service",120),
                           ("date","Date",90),("status","Status",75),
                           ("cost","Cost",60),("bay","Bay",55)]:
            tree.heading(c, text=lbl); tree.column(c, width=w, minwidth=36)
        hist = [b for b in self.bookings
                if b.get("cust_id") == cust["id"] or b["cust_name"] == cust["name"]]
        for bk in sorted(hist, key=lambda b: b.get("date",""), reverse=True):
            bay  = f"Bay {bk['bay_num']}" if bk.get("bay_num") else "—"
            cost = f"INR {bk.get('actual_cost', bk.get('est_cost',0)):.0f}"
            tree.insert("","end", values=(bk["plate"], bk["type"],
                                          fmt_date(bk.get("date","")),
                                          bk["status"], cost, bay))
        tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    def _show_vehicle_history(self, cust, car_id):
        car = next((v for v in cust.get("cars",[]) if v["id"] == car_id), None)
        if not car: return
        win = self._modal(f"Service history — {car['plate']}", w=680, h=480)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=16, pady=16)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text=f"Service history for {car['plate']} ({car.get('model','')})",
                 font=F(12, "bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
        cols = ("date","type","mechanic","status","cost","notes")
        tree = ttk.Treeview(f, columns=cols, show="headings", height=14)
        for c, lbl, w in [("date","Date",90),("type","Service",120),
                           ("mechanic","Mechanic",100),("status","Status",75),
                           ("cost","Cost",60),("notes","Notes",160)]:
            tree.heading(c, text=lbl); tree.column(c, width=w, minwidth=36)
        hist = [b for b in self.bookings
                if b.get("car_id") == car_id or b["plate"] == car["plate"]]
        for bk in sorted(hist, key=lambda b: b.get("date",""), reverse=True):
            cost = f"INR {bk.get('actual_cost', bk.get('est_cost',0)):.0f}"
            tree.insert("","end", values=(fmt_date(bk.get("date","")), bk["type"],
                                          bk.get("mechanic","—"), bk["status"],
                                          cost, bk.get("notes","")[:40]))
        tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    def _edit_customer(self, cust):
        win = self._modal("Edit customer", w=480, h=210)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0,1,2), weight=1, uniform="ec")
        tk.Label(f, text="Edit customer", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=(0, 4))
        e_name  = lbl_entry(f, "Full name", 1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_phone = lbl_entry(f, "Phone",     1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_email = lbl_entry(f, "Email",     1, 2, bg_key="surface")
        for widget, key in [(e_name,"name"),(e_phone,"phone"),(e_email,"email")]:
            widget.insert(0, cust.get(key,""))
        def save():
            cust["name"]  = e_name.get().strip()
            cust["phone"] = e_phone.get().strip()
            cust["email"] = e_email.get().strip()
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast("Customer updated.", "success"); self.refresh_all()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="e", pady=(12, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _delete_customer(self, cust):
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete customer",
                f"Delete {cust['name']} and all their vehicles?"): return
        self.customers = [c for c in self.customers if c["id"] != cust["id"]]
        self._save(); self.refresh_all()

    def _add_car_win(self, cust):
        win = self._modal("Add vehicle", w=500, h=270)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0,1), weight=1, uniform="ac")
        tk.Label(f, text="Add vehicle", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 4))
        e_plate = lbl_entry(f, "License plate", 1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_model = lbl_entry(f, "Make & model",  1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_year  = lbl_entry(f, "Year",          3, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_color = lbl_entry(f, "Colour",        3, 1, bg_key="surface")
        def save():
            plate = e_plate.get().strip().upper()
            if not plate: self._toast("Enter a license plate.", "error"); return
            cust.setdefault("cars",[]).append(dict(
                id=uid(), plate=plate,
                model=e_model.get().strip(),
                year=e_year.get().strip(),
                color=e_color.get().strip()))
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast(f"Vehicle {plate} added.", "success"); self.refresh_all()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=5, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="e", pady=(14, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel",      cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Add vehicle", cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _delete_car(self, cust, car_id):
        cust["cars"] = [v for v in cust.get("cars",[]) if v["id"] != car_id]
        self._save(); self.refresh_all()

    # =========================================================================
    # MECHANICS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_mechanics(self):
        panel = self.panels["mechanics"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        p = sf.inner
        self._section(p, "Add worker")
        card = self._card(p); card.columnconfigure((0,1,2), weight=1, uniform="mc")
        self.mc_name    = lbl_entry(card, "Full name",  0, 0, bg_key="surface")
        self.mc_phone   = lbl_entry(card, "Phone",      0, 1, bg_key="surface")
        self.mc_special = lbl_entry(card, "Speciality", 0, 2, bg_key="surface")
        self.mc_name.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._add_mechanic())
        br = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"])
        br.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=(10, 0))
        Btn(br, "+ Add worker", cmd=self._add_mechanic, style="primary").pack(side="left")
        n_mechs = len(self.mechanics)
        mech_cap = "∞" if LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_workers) else str(LIMITS.max_workers)
        self._section(p, f"Workers  ({n_mechs} / {mech_cap} used)")
        self.mc_list = tk.Frame(p, bg=C["bg"])
        self.mc_list.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(4, 20))

    def _add_mechanic(self):
        if not LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_workers) and len(self.mechanics) >= LIMITS.max_workers:
            self._show_upgrade_prompt("workers", LIMITS.max_workers); return
        name = self.mc_name.get().strip()
        if not name: self._toast("Enter a name.", "error"); return
        self.mechanics.append(dict(id=uid(), name=name,
            phone=self.mc_phone.get().strip(),
            speciality=self.mc_special.get().strip(),
            active=True, created_at=now_str()))
        self._save()
        for w in [self.mc_name, self.mc_phone, self.mc_special]: w.delete(0, "end")
        self._toast(f'Worker "{name}" added.', "success")
        self._refresh_mechanics()
        self._refresh_new_booking()

    def _refresh_mechanics(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "mc_list"): return
        for w in self.mc_list.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        if not self.mechanics:
            tk.Label(self.mc_list, text="No mechanics added yet.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(pady=20); return
        for mech in self.mechanics:
            active = sum(1 for j in self.jobs
                         if j.get("mechanic") == mech["name"]
                         and j["status"] not in ("Complete","Cancelled"))
            outer = tk.Frame(self.mc_list, bg=C["border"]); outer.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))
            card  = tk.Frame(outer, bg=C["surface"]); card.pack(fill="x", padx=1, pady=1)
            row   = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", padx=14, pady=10)
            initials = "".join(w[0] for w in mech["name"].split() if w)[:2].upper()
            tk.Label(row, text=initials, font=F(11, "bold"),
                     bg=C["success_bg"], fg=C["success"],
                     padx=10, pady=4).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))
            ic = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["surface"]); ic.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            tk.Label(ic, text=mech["name"], font=F(11, "bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w")
            detail = "  ·  ".join(filter(None, [mech.get("phone",""), mech.get("speciality","")]))
            tk.Label(ic, text=detail or "No details", font=F(9),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(row, text=f"{active} active job(s)", font=F(9, "bold"),
                     bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                     padx=8, pady=3).pack(side="right", padx=(0, 8))
            acts = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["surface"]); acts.pack(side="right")
            Btn(acts, "✏", cmd=lambda m=mech: self._edit_mechanic(m), small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
            Btn(acts, "🗑", cmd=lambda m=mech: self._delete_mechanic(m), style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")

    def _edit_mechanic(self, mech):
        win = self._modal("Edit worker", w=460, h=200)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0,1,2), weight=1, uniform="em")
        tk.Label(f, text="Edit worker", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="w", pady=(0, 4))
        e_name  = lbl_entry(f, "Full name",  1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_phone = lbl_entry(f, "Phone",      1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_spec  = lbl_entry(f, "Speciality", 1, 2, bg_key="surface")
        for widget, key in [(e_name,"name"),(e_phone,"phone"),(e_spec,"speciality")]:
            widget.insert(0, mech.get(key,""))
        def save():
            mech["name"]       = e_name.get().strip()
            mech["phone"]      = e_phone.get().strip()
            mech["speciality"] = e_spec.get().strip()
            self._save(); win.destroy()
            self._toast("Mechanic updated.", "success")
            self._refresh_mechanics()
            self._refresh_new_booking()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=3, sticky="e", pady=(12, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _delete_mechanic(self, mech):
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete worker", f"Delete worker {mech['name']}?"): return
        self.mechanics = [m for m in self.mechanics if m["id"] != mech["id"]]
        self._save()
        self._refresh_mechanics()
        self._refresh_new_booking()

    # =========================================================================
    # BAY STATUS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_bays(self):
        panel = self.panels["bays"]
        bar = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(12, 4))
        self.bay_ft = tk.StringVar(value="All types")
        self.bay_fs = tk.StringVar(value="All")
        ttk.Combobox(bar, textvariable=self.bay_ft, state="readonly",
                     values=["All types"] + self.all_bay_types,
                     font=F(10), width=22).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        ttk.Combobox(bar, textvariable=self.bay_fs, state="readonly",
                     values=["All","Available","Occupied","Maintenance"],
                     font=F(10), width=14).pack(side="left")
        self.bay_ft.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_bays())
        self.bay_fs.trace_add("write", lambda *_: self._refresh_bays())
        self.bay_stats_f = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        self.bay_stats_f.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(8, 6))
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.bays_inner = sf.inner

    def _refresh_bays(self):
        for w in self.bay_stats_f.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        total  = len(self.bays)
        avail  = sum(1 for b in self.bays if b["status"]=="Available" and not b.get("maintenance"))
        occ    = sum(1 for b in self.bays if b["status"]=="Occupied")
        maint  = sum(1 for b in self.bays if b.get("maintenance"))
        active = sum(1 for b in self.bookings if b["status"] in ("Booked","Arrived"))
        for label, val, col in [
            ("Total bays",      total,  C["text"]),
            ("Available",       avail,  C["success"]),
            ("Occupied",        occ,    C["danger"]),
            ("Maintenance",     maint,  C["warning"]),
            ("Active bookings", active, C["info"]),
        ]:
            s = tk.Frame(self.bay_stats_f, bg=C["bg"]); s.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 20))
            tk.Label(s, text=str(val), font=F(22, "bold"), bg=C["bg"], fg=col).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(s, text=label,    font=F(9),          bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")

        for w in self.bays_inner.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        ft = self.bay_ft.get(); fs = self.bay_fs.get()
        grouped: dict = {}
        for b in self.bays:
            if ft != "All types" and b["type"] != ft: continue
            if fs == "Available"   and not (b["status"]=="Available" and not b.get("maintenance")): continue
            if fs == "Occupied"    and b["status"] != "Occupied": continue
            if fs == "Maintenance" and not b.get("maintenance"): continue
            grouped.setdefault(b["type"],[]).append(b)


        for bay_type, bay_list in grouped.items():
            n_avail = sum(1 for b in bay_list
                          if b["status"]=="Available" and not b.get("maintenance"))
            hdr = tk.Frame(self.bays_inner, bg=C["bg"]); hdr.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 5))
            tk.Label(hdr, text=bay_type, font=F(10, "bold"),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
            tk.Label(hdr, text=f"  {n_avail}/{len(bay_list)} available",
                     font=F(9), bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
            row_f = tk.Frame(self.bays_inner, bg=C["bg"])
            row_f.pack(fill="x", padx=2)
            for b in bay_list:
                maint = b.get("maintenance", False)
                occ   = (b["status"] == "Occupied")
                bg = C["warning_bg"] if maint else (C["danger_bg"] if occ else C["success_bg"])
                fg = C["warning"]    if maint else (C["danger"]    if occ else C["success"])
                chip = tk.Frame(row_f, bg=bg, highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=fg)
                chip.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6), pady=(0, 6))
                tk.Label(chip, text=f"Bay {b['num']}", font=F(10, "bold"),
                         bg=bg, fg=fg, padx=12, pady=4).pack()
                status_txt = "MAINT" if maint else (b["plate"] if occ else "Available")
                tk.Label(chip, text=status_txt, font=F(8),
                         bg=bg, fg=fg, padx=12, pady=2).pack()
                if b.get("note"):
                    tk.Label(chip, text=b["note"][:14], font=F(7),
                             bg=bg, fg=fg, padx=12, pady=2, wraplength=90).pack()
                btn_row = tk.Frame(chip, bg=bg); btn_row.pack(pady=(2, 4))
                Btn(btn_row, "Note",
                    cmd=lambda bay=b: self._bay_note(bay), small=True).pack(side="left", padx=2)
                maint_lbl = "✓ Maint" if not maint else "✕ Maint"
                Btn(btn_row, maint_lbl,
                    cmd=lambda bay=b: self._toggle_maintenance(bay),
                    style="warning", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=2)

    def _bay_note(self, bay):
        win = self._modal("Bay note", w=380, h=180)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=20, pady=16)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text=f"{bay['type']} — Bay {bay['num']}", font=F(12, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 8))
        e = tk.Entry(f, font=F(11), relief="flat", bd=0,
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e.pack(fill="x"); e.insert(0, bay.get("note",""))
        def save():
            bay["note"] = e.get().strip(); self._save(); win.destroy(); self.refresh_all()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e", pady=(10, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _toggle_maintenance(self, bay):
        if bay["status"] == "Occupied" and not bay.get("maintenance"):
            self._toast("Cannot set maintenance on an occupied bay.", "error"); return
        bay["maintenance"] = not bay.get("maintenance", False)
        if bay["maintenance"]:
            bay["status"] = "Available"; bay["plate"] = None; bay["booking_id"] = None
        self._save(); self.refresh_all()

    # =========================================================================
    # INVENTORY
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_inventory(self):
        panel = self.panels["inventory"]
        panel.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        panel.rowconfigure(1, weight=1)

        # Top: add-item form (fixed height)
        top = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        top.pack(fill="x", side="top")
        self._section(top, "Add item")
        card = self._card(top); card.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3,4), weight=1, uniform="inv")
        card.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3,4), weight=1, uniform="inv")
        card.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3,4,5), weight=1, uniform="inv")
        self.inv_name  = lbl_entry(card, "Item name",    0, 0, bg_key="surface")
        self.inv_cat   = lbl_entry(card, "Category",     0, 1, bg_key="surface")
        self.inv_loc   = lbl_entry(card, "Location",     0, 2, bg_key="surface")
        self.inv_qty   = lbl_entry(card, "Quantity",     0, 3, bg_key="surface")
        self.inv_low   = lbl_entry(card, "Low stock at", 0, 4, default="5", bg_key="surface")
        self.inv_price = lbl_entry(card, "Sell price (INR )", 0, 5, default="0", bg_key="surface")
        br = tk.Frame(card, bg=C["surface"])
        br.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=6, sticky="w", pady=(10, 0))
        Btn(br, "Add item",    cmd=self._add_inv,        style="primary").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        Btn(br, "💾 Export CSV", cmd=self._export_inv_csv, style="default").pack(side="left")

        # Section label
        sec = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        sec.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=(14, 2))
        tk.Label(sec, text="Inventory", font=F(11, "bold"),
                 bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")
        tk.Frame(sec, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(
            side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(10, 0), pady=5)

        # Bottom: treeview fills remaining space
        tbl = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        tbl.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=16, pady=(0, 0))

        cols = ("name","category","location","qty","low","price","status")
        self.inv_tree = ttk.Treeview(tbl, columns=cols, show="headings")
        for c, lbl, w in [("name","Item",200),("category","Category",110),
                           ("location","Location",90),("qty","Qty",55),
                           ("low","Low at",50),("price","Price (INR )",75),("status","Status",75)]:
            self.inv_tree.heading(c, text=lbl); self.inv_tree.column(c, width=w, minwidth=36)
        self.inv_tree.tag_configure("low", background=C["danger_bg"], foreground=C["danger"])
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(tbl, orient="vertical", command=self.inv_tree.yview)
        self.inv_tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        self.inv_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True, side="left")
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")

        ab = tk.Frame(panel, bg=C["bg"])
        ab.pack(fill="x", padx=16, pady=8, side="bottom")
        Btn(ab, "−  Decrease",     cmd=lambda: self._adj_qty(-1)).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(ab, "+  Increase",     cmd=lambda: self._adj_qty(1)).pack(side="left",  padx=(0, 14))
        Btn(ab, "✏  Edit",         cmd=self._edit_inv).pack(side="left",             padx=(0, 4))
        Btn(ab, "🗑  Delete",       cmd=self._del_inv,           style="danger").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 14))
        Btn(ab, "📦  Parts order", cmd=self._parts_order, style="warning").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))

    def _refresh_inv_suppliers(self):
        """Refresh the supplier combobox in the inventory add form."""
        opts = ["— None —"] + [s["name"] for s in self.suppliers]
        if hasattr(self, "inv_sup_cb"):
            self.inv_sup_cb["values"] = opts
            if not self.inv_sup_var.get():
                self.inv_sup_var.set(opts[0])

    def _add_inv(self):
        name = self.inv_name.get().strip(); cat = self.inv_cat.get().strip()
        loc  = self.inv_loc.get().strip()
        try:    qty = int(self.inv_qty.get().strip())
        except: qty = 0
        try:    low = int(self.inv_low.get().strip())
        except: low = 5
        if not name: self._toast("Enter an item name.", "error"); return
        try:    price = float(self.inv_price.get().strip())
        except: price = 0.0
        self.inventory.append(dict(id=uid(), name=name, category=cat,
                                   location=loc, qty=qty, low_stock=low,
                                   sell_price=price))
        self._save()
        for w in [self.inv_name, self.inv_cat, self.inv_loc,
                  self.inv_qty, self.inv_low, self.inv_price]: w.delete(0,"end")
        self.inv_low.insert(0, "5"); self.inv_price.insert(0, "0")
        self._toast(f'"{name}" added.', "success"); self._refresh_inventory()

    def _refresh_inventory(self):
        self.inv_tree.delete(*self.inv_tree.get_children())
        for item in self.inventory:
            low    = item.get("low_stock", 5); qty = item["qty"]
            status = "LOW STOCK" if qty <= low else "OK"
            tags   = ("low",) if qty <= low else ()
            price = item.get("sell_price", 0)
            self.inv_tree.insert("","end", iid=item["id"], tags=tags,
                                 values=(item["name"], item.get("category","—"),
                                         item.get("location","—"),
                                         qty, low,
                                         f"INR {price:.0f}" if price else "—",
                                         status))

    def _sel_inv(self):
        sel = self.inv_tree.selection()
        if not sel: self._toast("Select an item first.", "info"); return None
        return next((i for i in self.inventory if i["id"] == sel[0]), None)

    def _adj_qty(self, delta):
        item = self._sel_inv()
        if not item: return
        item["qty"] = max(0, item["qty"] + delta)
        self._save(); self._refresh_inventory()

    def _edit_inv(self):
        item = self._sel_inv()
        if not item: return
        win = self._modal("Edit item", w=600, h=240)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        f.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3,4), weight=1, uniform="ei")
        tk.Label(f, text="Edit item", font=F(13, "bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).grid(
            row=0, column=0, columnspan=5, sticky="w", pady=(0, 4))
        f.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3,4,5), weight=1, uniform="ei")
        e_name  = lbl_entry(f, "Name",         1, 0, bg_key="surface")
        e_cat   = lbl_entry(f, "Category",     1, 1, bg_key="surface")
        e_loc   = lbl_entry(f, "Location",     1, 2, bg_key="surface")
        e_qty   = lbl_entry(f, "Quantity",     1, 3, bg_key="surface")
        e_low   = lbl_entry(f, "Low stock",    1, 4, bg_key="surface")
        e_price = lbl_entry(f, "Sell price (INR )", 1, 5, bg_key="surface")

        e_name.insert(0, item["name"]); e_cat.insert(0, item.get("category",""))
        e_loc.insert(0, item.get("location",""))
        e_qty.insert(0, str(item["qty"])); e_low.insert(0, str(item.get("low_stock",5)))
        e_price.insert(0, str(item.get("sell_price", 0)))

        def save():
            item["name"]      = e_name.get().strip()
            item["category"]  = e_cat.get().strip()
            item["location"]  = e_loc.get().strip()
            try:    item["qty"]        = int(e_qty.get())
            except: pass
            try:    item["low_stock"]  = int(e_low.get())
            except: pass
            try:    item["sell_price"] = float(e_price.get())
            except: pass
            self._save(); win.destroy(); self._refresh_inventory()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"])
        foot.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=6, sticky="e", pady=(12, 0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")

    def _del_inv(self):
        item = self._sel_inv()
        if not item: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete item", f'Delete "{item["name"]}"?'): return
        self.inventory = [i for i in self.inventory if i["id"] != item["id"]]
        self._save(); self._refresh_inventory()

    def _parts_order(self):
        """Generate a parts order list for all items at or below low stock threshold."""
        low_items = [i for i in self.inventory if i["qty"] <= i.get("low_stock", 5)]
        if not low_items:
            self._toast("No items are below their low stock threshold.", "info")
            return
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".txt",
            filetypes=[("Text file","*.txt"), ("CSV","*.csv")],
            title="Save parts order",
            initialfile=f"PartsOrder_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.txt")
        if not path: return

        lines = [
            "=" * 50,
            f"  PARTS ORDER — {self.settings.get('workshop_name','')}",
            f"  Generated: {now_str()}",
            "=" * 50,
            "",
            f"  {'Item':<30} {'Category':<20} {'In stock':>8} {'Low at':>6} {'Order qty':>9}",
            "-" * 50,
        ]
        for item in sorted(low_items, key=lambda i: i.get("category","")):
            order_qty = max(item.get("low_stock", 5) * 2 - item["qty"], 1)
            lines.append(
                f"  {item['name']:<30} {item.get('category',''):<20} "
                f"{item['qty']:>8} {item.get('low_stock',5):>6} {order_qty:>9}")
        lines += ["", "=" * 50, f"  Total items to order: {len(low_items)}", "=" * 50]

        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("\n".join(lines))
        self._toast(f"Parts order saved — {len(low_items)} item(s).", "success")

    def _email_parts_order(self):
        """Email parts orders to each linked supplier automatically, CC manager."""
        low_items = [i for i in self.inventory if i["qty"] <= i.get("low_stock", 5)]
        if not low_items:
            self._toast("No items are below their low stock threshold.", "info"); return

        ws          = self.settings.get("workshop_name", "")
        manager_cc  = self.settings.get("manager_email", "").strip()

        # Group low items by supplier
        sup_map = {s["id"]: s for s in self.suppliers}
        groups  = {}   # supplier_id (or None) -> [items]
        for item in low_items:
            sid = item.get("supplier_id")
            groups.setdefault(sid, []).append(item)

        sent_count = 0
        for sid, items in groups.items():
            sup = sup_map.get(sid)
            sup_email = sup["email"].strip() if sup and sup.get("email") else ""
            sup_name  = sup["name"]          if sup else "— unassigned —"

            order_lines = [
                f"Parts reorder request from {ws}",
                f"Date: {now_str()}",
                f"Supplier: {sup_name}", "",
                f"{'Item':<32} {'Category':<18} {'In stock':>8} {'Reorder qty':>11}",
                "-" * 75,
            ]
            for item in sorted(items, key=lambda i: i.get("category", "")):
                reorder = max(item.get("low_stock", 5) * 2 - item["qty"], 1)
                order_lines.append(
                    f"{item['name']:<32} {item.get('category',''):<18} "
                    f"{item['qty']:>8} {reorder:>11}")
            order_lines += ["", f"Total items: {len(items)}", "",
                            f"Please process this order at your earliest convenience.",
                            f"Thank you,", ws]
            body    = "\n".join(order_lines)
            subject = f"Parts reorder — {ws} — {now_str()}"

            if sup_email:
                send_email_async(self.settings, sup_email, subject, body,
                                 cc_addr=manager_cc,
                                 callback=lambda err, sn=sup_name: self._toast(
                                     f"Order sent to {sn}!" if not err else f"Failed ({sn}): {err}",
                                     "success" if not err else "error"))
                sent_count += 1
            elif manager_cc:
                # No supplier email — send to manager with note
                body = f"NOTE: Items below have no supplier email assigned.\n\n" + body
                send_email_async(self.settings, manager_cc, subject, body,
                                 callback=lambda err: self._toast(
                                     "Unassigned items sent to manager!" if not err else f"Email failed: {err}",
                                     "warning" if not err else "error"))
                sent_count += 1

        if sent_count:
            self._toast(f"Sending {sent_count} supplier order(s)…", "info")
        else:
            # No emails configured — fall back to dialog
            win = self._modal("Email parts order", w=420, h=200)
            f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
            f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            tk.Label(f, text="No supplier emails configured.", font=F(12,"bold"),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["warning"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,6))
            tk.Label(f, text="Add suppliers with emails in Settings, then link items in Inventory.",
                     font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                     wraplength=360, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,12))
            Btn(f, "OK", cmd=win.destroy, style="primary").pack(anchor="e")

    def _export_inv_csv(self):
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".csv", filetypes=[("CSV","*.csv")],
            title="Export inventory")
        if not path: return
        with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(["Item","Category","Quantity","Low stock threshold"])
            for item in self.inventory:
                w.writerow([item["name"], item.get("category",""),
                            item["qty"], item.get("low_stock",5)])
        self._toast(f"Exported to {os.path.basename(path)}", "success")

    # =========================================================================
    # REPORTS
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_reports(self):
        panel = self.panels["reports"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._reports_sf = sf

    def _refresh_reports(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "_reports_sf"): return
        p = self._reports_sf.inner
        for w in p.winfo_children(): w.destroy()

        self._section(p, "Revenue")
        rev_card = self._card(p)
        rev_card.columnconfigure((0,1,2,3), weight=1, uniform="rv")
        total_rev = sum(b.get("actual_cost",0) for b in self.bookings if b["status"]=="Checked out")
        est_rev   = sum(b.get("est_cost",0) for b in self.bookings if b["status"] in ("Booked","Arrived"))
        today_rev = sum(b.get("actual_cost",0) for b in self.bookings
                        if b["status"]=="Checked out"
                        and b.get("checked_out","").startswith(datetime.today().strftime("%d %b %Y")))
        this_month = [b for b in self.bookings if b["status"]=="Checked out"
                      and b.get("date","").startswith(datetime.today().strftime("%Y-%m"))]
        month_rev = sum(b.get("actual_cost",0) for b in this_month)
        for col_i,(lbl,val,clr) in enumerate([
            ("Today's revenue", f"INR {today_rev:.2f}", "success"),
            ("This month",      f"INR {month_rev:.2f}", "info"),
            ("All time",        f"INR {total_rev:.2f}", "text"),
            ("Est. pipeline",   f"INR {est_rev:.2f}",   "warning"),
        ]):
            s = tk.Frame(rev_card, bg=C["surface"])
            s.grid(row=0, column=col_i, padx=(0,12), sticky="w")
            tk.Label(s, text=val, font=F(22,"bold"), bg=C["surface"], fg=C[clr]).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(s, text=lbl, font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")

        self._section(p, "Bay utilisation")
        bay_card = self._card(p, padx=12, pady=10)
        total_bks = len([b for b in self.bookings if b["status"] != "Cancelled"])
        if total_bks > 0:
            counts: dict = {}
            for b in self.bookings:
                if b["status"] != "Cancelled":
                    counts[b["type"]] = counts.get(b["type"],0) + 1
            for bt, cnt in sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
                pct = cnt / total_bks * 100
                row = tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=3)
                tk.Label(row, text=bt, font=F(10),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                         width=24, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                bar_bg = tk.Frame(row, bg=C["border"], height=14, width=300)
                bar_bg.pack(side="left", padx=(8,8)); bar_bg.pack_propagate(False)
                tk.Frame(bar_bg, bg=C["info"], height=14,
                         width=int(pct*3)).pack(side="left", fill="y")
                tk.Label(row, text=f"{cnt} ({pct:.0f}%)", font=F(9),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        else:
            tk.Label(bay_card, text="No bookings yet.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")

        self._section(p, "Mechanic workload")
        mw_card = self._card(p, padx=12, pady=10)
        if self.mechanics:
            for mech in self.mechanics:
                jobs_done   = sum(1 for j in self.jobs
                                  if j.get("mechanic")==mech["name"] and j["status"]=="Complete")
                jobs_active = sum(1 for j in self.jobs
                                  if j.get("mechanic")==mech["name"]
                                  and j["status"] in ("Pending","In Progress"))
                row = tk.Frame(mw_card, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=3)
                tk.Label(row, text=mech["name"], font=F(10,"bold"),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"], width=20, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                tk.Label(row, text=f"{jobs_active} active", font=F(9),
                         bg=C["warning_bg"], fg=C["warning"], padx=6, pady=2).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
                tk.Label(row, text=f"{jobs_done} complete", font=F(9),
                         bg=C["success_bg"], fg=C["success"], padx=6, pady=2).pack(side="left")
        else:
            tk.Label(mw_card, text="No mechanics added.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")

        self._section(p, "Top customers")
        tc_card = self._card(p, padx=12, pady=10)
        cust_counts: dict = {}; cust_rev: dict = {}
        for b in self.bookings:
            if b["status"] == "Cancelled": continue
            key = b.get("cust_id") or b["cust_name"]
            cust_counts[key] = cust_counts.get(key,0) + 1
            cust_rev[key]    = cust_rev.get(key,0) + b.get("actual_cost",0)
        top = sorted(cust_counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:8]
        if top:
            for key, cnt in top:
                cust = next((c for c in self.customers if c["id"]==key), None)
                name = cust["name"] if cust else key
                rev  = cust_rev.get(key, 0)
                row  = tk.Frame(tc_card, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=3)
                tk.Label(row, text=name, font=F(10),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                         width=24, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
                tk.Label(row, text=f"{cnt} visit(s)", font=F(9),
                         bg=C["info_bg"], fg=C["info"],
                         padx=6, pady=2).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
                tk.Label(row, text=f"INR {rev:.0f}", font=F(9,"bold"),
                         bg=C["surface"], fg=C["success"]).pack(side="left")
        else:
            tk.Label(tc_card, text="No data yet.", font=F(10),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w")

        self._section(p, "Data & Backup")
        bk_card = self._card(p)
        tk.Label(bk_card, text=f"Last backup: {self.settings.get('last_backup','Never')}",
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))
        br_bk = tk.Frame(bk_card, bg=C["surface"]); br_bk.pack(anchor="w")
        Btn(br_bk, "💾  Backup now", cmd=self._manual_backup,
            style="info").pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(br_bk, "🗑  Clear local data", cmd=self._clear_local_data,
            style="danger").pack(side="left")

        # ── CSV Import ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._section(p, "CSV Import")
        csv_card = self._card(p)
        tk.Label(csv_card, text="Import customers, inventory, bookings or mechanics from a CSV file.",
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))

        # Column reference table
        ref = tk.Frame(csv_card, bg=C["bg"]); ref.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,10))
        for label, cols in [
            ("Customers",  "name  ·  phone  ·  email  ·  address  ·  notes"),
            ("Inventory",  "name  ·  category  ·  location  ·  qty  ·  low_stock  ·  sell_price  ·  hsn"),
            ("Bookings",   "plate  ·  cust_name  ·  date  ·  time  ·  type  ·  notes  ·  est_cost"),
            ("Mechanics",  "name  ·  role  ·  phone"),
        ]:
            row = tk.Frame(ref, bg=C["bg"]); row.pack(fill="x", padx=8, pady=2)
            tk.Label(row, text=f"{label}:", font=F(9,"bold"),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["info"], width=12, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
            tk.Label(row, text=cols, font=F(8),
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")

        tk.Label(csv_card,
                 text="Headers are case-insensitive. Type is auto-detected from headers present.",
                 font=F(8), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,10))

        csv_row = tk.Frame(csv_card, bg=C["surface"]); csv_row.pack(anchor="w")
        Btn(csv_row, "📥  Import CSV", cmd=self._import_csv,
            style="success").pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(csv_row, "📄  Download templates", cmd=self._export_csv_templates,
            style="default").pack(side="left")

    def _import_csv(self):
        """Import customers, inventory or bookings from a CSV file.
        Recognised column headers (case-insensitive):
          Customers : name, phone, email, address, notes
          Inventory : name, category, location, qty, low_stock, sell_price, hsn
          Bookings  : plate, cust_name, date, time, type, notes, est_cost
        The importer auto-detects which type from the headers present."""
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Import CSV",
            filetypes=[("CSV files","*.csv"),("All files","*.*")])
        if not path: return
        try:
            import csv as _csv
            rows = []
            with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:
                reader = _csv.DictReader(fh)
                rows = [r for r in reader]
            if not rows:
                self._toast("CSV file is empty.", "error"); return
            # Normalise headers
            def norm(row): return {k.strip().lower(): v.strip() for k,v in row.items()}
            rows = [norm(r) for r in rows]
            headers = set(rows[0].keys())

            imported = 0
            if "phone" in headers or "email" in headers:
                # Customers
                for r in rows:
                    name = r.get("name","").strip()
                    if not name: continue
                    phone = r.get("phone",""); email = r.get("email","")
                    addr  = r.get("address",""); notes = r.get("notes","")
                    # Skip duplicates by phone or name
                    exists = any(
                        c.get("name","").lower()==name.lower() or
                        (phone and c.get("phone","")==phone)
                        for c in self.customers)
                    if not exists:
                        self.customers.append(dict(
                            id=uid(), name=name, phone=phone,
                            email=email, address=addr, notes=notes))
                        imported += 1
                self._save()
                self._toast(f"Imported {imported} customer(s).", "success")

            elif "qty" in headers or "sell_price" in headers or "category" in headers:
                # Inventory
                for r in rows:
                    name = r.get("name","").strip()
                    if not name: continue
                    try: qty = int(float(r.get("qty","0") or 0))
                    except: qty = 0
                    try: price = float(r.get("sell_price","0") or 0)
                    except: price = 0.0
                    try: low = int(float(r.get("low_stock","5") or 5))
                    except: low = 5
                    self.inventory.append(dict(
                        id=uid(), name=name,
                        category=r.get("category",""),
                        location=r.get("location",""),
                        qty=qty, low_stock=low,
                        sell_price=price,
                        hsn=r.get("hsn","")))
                    imported += 1
                self._save()
                self._toast(f"Imported {imported} inventory item(s).", "success")

            elif "plate" in headers:
                # Bookings
                for r in rows:
                    plate = (r.get("plate","") or "").strip().upper()
                    cust  = r.get("cust_name","") or r.get("customer","") or r.get("name","")
                    if not plate: continue
                    bdate = r.get("date","") or today_str()
                    btime = r.get("time","") or "09:00"
                    btype = r.get("type","") or r.get("bay_type","General")
                    bnotes= r.get("notes","")
                    try: bcost = float(r.get("est_cost","0") or 0)
                    except: bcost = 0.0
                    ref = "BK-" + uid()[:6].upper()
                    self.bookings.append(dict(
                        id=uid(), ref=ref, plate=plate,
                        cust_name=cust, cust_phone=r.get("phone",""),
                        cust_email=r.get("email",""),
                        date=bdate, time=btime, type=btype,
                        notes=bnotes, est_cost=bcost,
                        status="Booked", bay_id=None, bay_num=None))
                    imported += 1
                self._save()
                self._toast(f"Imported {imported} booking(s).", "success")
            elif "role" in headers or ("name" in headers and "phone" in headers
                                       and "email" not in headers):
                # Mechanics
                for r in rows:
                    name = r.get("name","").strip()
                    if not name: continue
                    exists = any(m.get("name","").lower()==name.lower()
                                 for m in self.mechanics)
                    if not exists:
                        self.mechanics.append(dict(
                            id=uid(), name=name,
                            role=r.get("role",""),
                            phone=r.get("phone","")))
                        imported += 1
                self._save()
                self._toast(f"Imported {imported} mechanic(s).", "success")
            else:
                self._toast(
                    "Could not detect CSV type.\n"
                    "  Customers : name, phone/email\n"
                    "  Inventory : name, qty/sell_price\n"
                    "  Bookings  : plate\n"
                    "  Mechanics : name, role, phone", "error")
                return
            self.refresh_all()
        except Exception as e:
            self._toast(f"Import failed: {e}", "error")

    def _clear_local_data(self):
        """Wipe all local data files + license cache. Optionally pull fresh from Firebase."""
        win = self._modal("Clear Local Data", w=500, h=380)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=24, pady=20)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(f, text="⚠  Clear all local data?",
                 font=F(14,"bold"), fg=C["danger"],
                 bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,10))

        _warn_msg = ("This will permanently delete all locally stored data. This includes all bookings, jobs, customers, inventory, mechanics, bays, settings and the license cache. If Firebase is connected, data will be re-downloaded automatically.")
        tk.Label(f, text=_warn_msg,
                 font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                 justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,14))

        pull_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=firebase_connected())
        tk.Checkbutton(f,
                       text="Re-download data from Firebase after clearing (recommended)",
                       variable=pull_var,
                       font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                       selectcolor=C["bg"],
                       activebackground=C["surface"],
                       state="normal" if firebase_connected() else "disabled"
                       ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,14))

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))

        def _do_clear():
            win.destroy()
            pull = pull_var.get() and firebase_connected()

            # 1. Delete all local data files
            for path in [BAYS_FILE, BOOKINGS_FILE, CUSTOMERS_FILE,
                         INVENTORY_FILE, MECHANICS_FILE, JOBS_FILE,
                         SETTINGS_FILE, SUPPLIERS_FILE,
                         os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "queue.json"),
                         _PENDING_SYNC_FILE]:
                try: os.remove(path)
                except FileNotFoundError: pass

            # 2. Clear license cache
            _lc.clear()

            # 3. Clear in-memory state
            self.bays      = []
            self.bookings  = []
            self.customers = []
            self.inventory = []
            self.mechanics = []
            self.jobs      = []
            self.queue     = []
            self.suppliers = []
            self.settings  = dict(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)
            save_settings(self.settings)

            if pull:
                # 4a. Pull fresh from Firebase
                self._toast("Pulling fresh data from Firebase…", "info")
                def _pull():
                    self._load_all()
                    self.after(0, lambda: (
                        self.refresh_all(),
                        self._toast("Data cleared and re-downloaded from Firebase ✓", "success")))
                threading.Thread(target=_pull, daemon=True).start()
            else:
                # 4b. Start fresh
                self.refresh_all()
                self._toast("All local data cleared. Please re-enter your license key on restart.", "success")
                self.after(2000, lambda: (
                    _lc.clear(),
                    self._toast("Restart the app to re-activate your license.", "info")))

        Btn(foot, "🗑  Yes, clear everything", cmd=_do_clear, style="danger").pack(side="left")

    def _export_csv_templates(self):
        """Save CSV template files so user knows exact column names."""
        folder = filedialog.askdirectory(title="Save CSV templates to folder")
        if not folder: return
        import csv as _csv
        templates = {
            "customers_template.csv": ["name","phone","email","address","notes"],
            "inventory_template.csv": ["name","category","location","qty","low_stock","sell_price","hsn"],
            "bookings_template.csv":  ["plate","cust_name","date","time","type","notes","est_cost"],
            "mechanics_template.csv": ["name","role","phone"],
        }
        for fname, headers in templates.items():
            path = os.path.join(folder, fname)
            with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                w = _csv.writer(fh)
                w.writerow(headers)
                w.writerow(["example"] + [""] * (len(headers)-1))
        self._toast(f"4 template files saved to selected folder.", "success")

    def _manual_backup(self):
        do_backup(self.settings)
        self._toast(f"Backup saved to {BACKUP_DIR}", "success")
        self.refresh_all()

    # =========================================================================

    def _build_settings(self):
        panel = self.panels["settings"]
        sf = ScrollableFrame(panel); sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._settings_sf = sf; self._settings_built = True

    def _refresh_settings(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "_settings_sf"): return
        p = self._settings_sf.inner
        for w in p.winfo_children(): w.destroy()

        # Cloud sync
        self._section(p, "Cloud sync — Firebase")
        cs = self._card(p)

        # Status
        if firebase_connected():
            tk.Label(cs, text="☁  Cloud sync is active — data is synced across all devices.",
                     font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["success"]).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(cs, text="Every save is pushed to Firebase automatically.",
                     font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(2,6))
            Btn(cs, "🔄  Sync now", cmd=self._sync_now, style="info").pack(anchor="w")
        else:
            if not _FB_AVAILABLE:
                status_msg = "firebase-admin not installed. Run: pip install firebase-admin"
            elif os.path.exists(KEY_FILE):
                status_msg = "firebase_key.json found but connection failed. Check the file and internet."
            else:
                status_msg = "No firebase_key.json found. Drop your key file below to connect."
            tk.Label(cs, text="⚠  Offline mode — data saved locally only.",
                     font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["warning"]).pack(anchor="w")
            tk.Label(cs, text=status_msg, font=F(9), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                     wraplength=580, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(2,8))

        # Drag-and-drop / browse for firebase_key.json
        tk.Label(cs, text="FIREBASE KEY FILE", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8,4))
        drop_frame = tk.Frame(cs, bg=C["info_bg"],
                              highlightthickness=2,
                              highlightbackground=C["info"])
        drop_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,6))

        key_exists = os.path.exists(KEY_FILE)
        key_label_text = (f"✓  {os.path.basename(KEY_FILE)} loaded from {BASE_DIR}"
                          if key_exists else
                          "Drop firebase_key.json here  —  or click Browse")
        key_fg = C["success"] if key_exists else C["info"]

        self._fb_key_lbl = tk.Label(drop_frame, text=key_label_text,
                                     font=F(10), bg=C["info_bg"], fg=key_fg,
                                     pady=18, cursor="hand2", wraplength=560)
        self._fb_key_lbl.pack(fill="x")

        # Enable drag-and-drop if tkinterdnd2 is available
        try:
            drop_frame.drop_target_register("DND_Files")
            drop_frame.dnd_bind("<<Drop>>", self._on_firebase_key_drop)
        except Exception:
            pass  # tkinterdnd2 not installed — fallback to browse button

        btn_row = tk.Frame(cs, bg=C["surface"]); btn_row.pack(anchor="w")
        Btn(btn_row, "📂  Browse for key file", cmd=self._browse_firebase_key,
            style="info").pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        if key_exists:
            Btn(btn_row, "🔄  Reconnect", cmd=self._reconnect_firebase,
                style="success").pack(side="left")
        tk.Label(cs,
                 text="Get your key: Firebase Console → Project Settings → "
                      "Service accounts → Generate new private key",
                 font=F(8), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                 wraplength=580, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(6,0))

        # ── My Plan ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        self._section(p, "My Plan")
        plan_card = self._card(p)
        import webbrowser as _wb

        # Plan header row
        _sub = _lc.status()
        ph = tk.Frame(plan_card, bg=C["surface"]); ph.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,10))
        plan_display = _sub.plan_name or _sub.plan or LIMITS.plan or "—"
        if _sub.is_trial: plan_display = f"Free Trial — {plan_display}"
        tk.Label(ph, text=plan_display,
                 font=F(16,"bold"), bg=C["surface"],
                 fg=C["warning"] if _sub.is_trial else C["success"]).pack(side="left")
        if _sub.is_trial and _sub.trial_days_left is not None:
            tk.Label(ph, text=f"  ·  {_sub.trial_days_left} days left",
                     font=F(11), bg=C["surface"],
                     fg=C["danger"] if (_sub.trial_days_left or 99) <= 2 else C["warning"]
                     ).pack(side="left")
        tk.Label(ph, text=f"  ·  {_sub.billing_cycle or LIMITS.billing}",
                 font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"]).pack(side="left")
        if _sub.indefinite:
            tk.Label(ph, text="  ·  Never expires",
                     font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["success"]).pack(side="left")
        elif _sub.expiry:
            dr   = _sub.days_remaining
            col  = C["danger"] if dr is not None and dr < 7 else C["muted"]
            lbl  = "Trial ends" if _sub.is_trial else "Renews on"
            tk.Label(ph,
                     text=f"  ·  {lbl}: {_sub.expiry.strftime('%d %b %Y')} ({dr}d)",
                     font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=col).pack(side="left")
        # Name / Garage
        if _sub.name or _sub.garage_name:
            nr = tk.Frame(plan_card, bg=C["surface"]); nr.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
            if _sub.garage_name:

                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"], width=8, anchor="w").pack(side="left")
            tk.Label(er, text=_sub.email, font=F(10),
                     bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(side="left")

        # Usage bars
        def _usage_row(parent, label, used, limit):

                        highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["warning"])
