                 text="⚠  Changing bay types rebuilds bays. Occupancy of unchanged bays is preserved.",
                 font=F(9), bg=C["warning_bg"], fg=C["warning"],
                 wraplength=700, justify="left", padx=12, pady=8).pack(anchor="w")
        bay_card = self._card(p, padx=12, pady=10)
        hdr = tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["surface"]); hdr.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
        for txt, w in [("Bay type",280),("Count",80),("Actions",120)]:
            tk.Label(hdr, text=txt.upper(), font=F(8,"bold"),
                     fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"],
                     width=w//8, anchor="w").pack(side="left", padx=4)
        tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(0,6))
        self._bay_rows_f = tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["surface"])
        self._bay_rows_f.pack(fill="x")
        self._render_bay_rows()
        tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(10,8))
        add_row = tk.Frame(bay_card, bg=C["surface"]); add_row.pack(fill="x")
        tk.Label(add_row, text="NEW TYPE:", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        self._new_bt_name = tk.Entry(add_row, font=F(11), width=22,
                                     relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                                     insertbackground=C["text"],
                                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        self._new_bt_name.pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        tk.Label(add_row, text="Count:", font=F(10),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(side="left")
        self._new_bt_count = tk.Entry(add_row, font=F(11), width=5,
                                      relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                                      insertbackground=C["text"],
                                      highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        self._new_bt_count.pack(side="left", padx=(4,2))
        def _set_inf():
            self._new_bt_count.delete(0,"end")
            self._new_bt_count.insert(0,"∞")
        Btn(add_row, "∞", cmd=_set_inf, style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(add_row, "+ Add", cmd=self._add_bay_type, style="success").pack(side="left")

    def _render_bay_rows(self):
        for w in self._bay_rows_f.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        for idx, (bt, cnt) in enumerate(self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS)):
            row = tk.Frame(self._bay_rows_f, bg=C["surface"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=3)
            nv = tk.StringVar(value=bt); cv = tk.StringVar(value=str(cnt))
            tk.Entry(row, textvariable=nv, font=F(11), width=28,
                     relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                     insertbackground=C["text"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"]
                     ).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
            tk.Entry(row, textvariable=cv, font=F(11), width=6,
                     relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"],
                     insertbackground=C["text"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"]
                     ).pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
            Btn(row, "Save",
                cmd=lambda i=idx,n=nv,c=cv: self._save_bay_type(i,n,c),
                style="info", small=True).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
            Btn(row, "Delete",
                cmd=lambda i=idx: self._delete_bay_type(i),
                style="danger", small=True).pack(side="left")

    def _save_bay_type(self, idx, nv, cv):
        name = nv.get().strip()
        try:    count = int(cv.get())
        except: self._toast("Count must be a number.", "error"); return
        if not name: self._toast("Bay type name cannot be empty.", "error"); return
        if count < 1: self._toast("Count must be at least 1.", "error"); return
        bay_defs = list(self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS))
        bay_defs[idx] = (name, count)
        self.settings["bay_defs"] = bay_defs; save_settings(self.settings)
        self._rebuild_bays()
        self._toast(f'"{name}" updated.', "success"); self.refresh_all()

    def _delete_bay_type(self, idx):
        bay_defs = list(self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS))
        name = bay_defs[idx][0]
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete bay type",
                f'Delete "{name}" and all its bays?'): return
        bay_defs.pop(idx); self.settings["bay_defs"] = bay_defs; save_settings(self.settings)
        self._rebuild_bays()
        self._toast(f'"{name}" removed.', "info"); self.refresh_all()

    def _add_bay_type(self):
        # Check plan limit first
        current_bays = len(self.bays)
        if not LIMITS.unlimited(LIMITS.max_bays) and current_bays >= LIMITS.max_bays:
            self._show_upgrade_prompt("bays", LIMITS.max_bays); return

        name = self._new_bt_name.get().strip()
        cnt_str = self._new_bt_count.get().strip()
        if cnt_str in ("", "∞", "inf", "0"):
            count = 999
        else:
            try:    count = int(cnt_str)
            except: self._toast("Count must be a number or ∞.", "error"); return
        if not name: self._toast("Enter a bay type name.", "error"); return
        if count < 1: self._toast("Count must be at least 1.", "error"); return
        bay_defs = list(self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS))
        if any(t == name for t, _ in bay_defs):
            self._toast(f'"{name}" already exists.', "error"); return
        bay_defs.append((name, count))
        self.settings["bay_defs"] = bay_defs; save_settings(self.settings)
        self._rebuild_bays()
        self._new_bt_name.delete(0,"end"); self._new_bt_count.delete(0,"end")
        self._toast(f'"{name}" added.', "success"); self.refresh_all()

    def _rebuild_bays(self):
        old = {b["id"]: b for b in self.bays}
        new = make_bays(self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS))
        for nb in new:
            if nb["id"] in old:
                o = old[nb["id"]]
                nb["status"]     = o["status"]
                nb["plate"]      = o["plate"]
                nb["booking_id"] = o["booking_id"]
                nb["note"]       = o.get("note","")
                nb["maintenance"]= o.get("maintenance",False)
        self.bays = new; self._save()

    def _save_email_settings(self):
        self.settings["email_sender"]   = self._em_sender.get().strip()
        self.settings["email_password"] = self._em_pwd.get().strip()
        if hasattr(self, "_em_manager"):
            self.settings["manager_email"] = self._em_manager.get().strip()
        if hasattr(self, "_em_pin"):
            pin = self._em_pin.get().strip()
            if pin:
                self.settings["admin_pin"] = pin
        save_settings(self.settings)
        self._toast("Email & notification settings saved.", "success")

    def _add_supplier(self):
        name  = self._new_sup_name.get().strip()
        email = self._new_sup_email.get().strip()
        if not name: self._toast("Enter a supplier name.", "error"); return
        self.suppliers.append(dict(id=uid(), name=name, email=email))
        self._save()
        self._new_sup_name.delete(0,"end"); self._new_sup_email.delete(0,"end")
        self._toast(f'"{name}" added.', "success")
        self._refresh_settings(); self._refresh_inv_suppliers()

    def _save_supplier(self, sup, nv, ev):
        sup["name"]  = nv.get().strip()
        sup["email"] = ev.get().strip()
        self._save()
        self._toast("Supplier saved.", "success")
        self._refresh_inv_suppliers()

    def _delete_supplier(self, sup):
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Delete supplier",
                f'Delete "{sup["name"]}"? Items linked to this supplier will become unlinked.'): return
        self.suppliers = [s for s in self.suppliers if s["id"] != sup["id"]]
        # Unlink items
        for item in self.inventory:
            if item.get("supplier_id") == sup["id"]:
                item["supplier_id"] = None
        self._save()
        self._toast(f'"{sup["name"]}" deleted.', "info")
        self._refresh_settings(); self._refresh_inv_suppliers()

    def _test_email(self):
        self._save_email_settings()
        sender = self.settings.get("email_sender","")
        if not sender: self._toast("Enter a sender email first.", "error"); return
        self._toast("Sending test email...", "info")
        send_email_async(
            self.settings, sender,
            "Garage Manager — Test email",
            "This is a test email from Garage Manager. Email is working correctly!",
            callback=lambda e: self._toast(
                "Test email sent!" if not e else f"Failed: {e}",
                "success" if not e else "error"))

    def _sync_now(self):
        if not firebase_connected():
            self._toast("Not connected to Firebase.", "error"); return
        self._toast("Syncing from cloud...", "info")
        def do_sync():
            bay_defs   = self.settings.get("bay_defs", DEFAULT_BAY_DEFS)
            saved_bays = _fb_load("bays", None)
            self.bays      = saved_bays if saved_bays else make_bays(bay_defs)
            self.bookings  = _fb_load("bookings",  [])
            self.customers = _fb_load("customers", [])
            self.inventory = _fb_load("inventory", [])
            self.mechanics = _fb_load("mechanics", [])
            self.jobs      = _fb_load("jobs",      [])
            self.queue     = _fb_load("queue",     [])
            for path, data in [
                (BAYS_FILE, self.bays), (BOOKINGS_FILE, self.bookings),
                (CUSTOMERS_FILE, self.customers), (INVENTORY_FILE, self.inventory),
                (MECHANICS_FILE, self.mechanics), (JOBS_FILE, self.jobs),
                (os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "queue.json"), self.queue),
            ]:
                save_json(path, data)
            self.after(0, lambda: (
                self.refresh_all(),
                self._toast("Sync complete.", "success")))
        threading.Thread(target=do_sync, daemon=True).start()

    def _browse_firebase_key(self):
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Select firebase_key.json",
            filetypes=[("JSON files","*.json"),("All files","*.*")])
        if path:
            self._install_firebase_key(path)

    def _on_firebase_key_drop(self, event):
        """Handle drag-and-drop of firebase_key.json onto the drop zone."""
        raw = event.data or ""
        # tkinterdnd2 wraps paths with braces on Mac/Linux if spaces present
        path = raw.strip().strip("{}")
        if path.lower().endswith(".json") and os.path.exists(path):
            self._install_firebase_key(path)
        else:
            self._toast("Please drop a .json file.", "error")

    def _install_firebase_key(self, src_path: str):
        """Copy the key file next to the app and (re)connect Firebase."""
        try:
            import shutil as _sh
            dest = KEY_FILE
            if os.path.abspath(src_path) != os.path.abspath(dest):
                _sh.copy2(src_path, dest)
            self._toast("Key file installed — connecting…", "info")
            self._reconnect_firebase()
        except Exception as e:
            self._toast(f"Could not install key: {e}", "error")

    def _reconnect_firebase(self):
        """Force re-initialise Firebase with the current key file."""
        global _db
        if not _FB_AVAILABLE:
            self._toast("firebase-admin not installed. Run: pip install firebase-admin",
                        "error"); return
        def _do():
            global _db
            try:
                import firebase_admin as _fa
                for app in list(_fa._apps.values()):
                    _fa.delete_app(app)
            except Exception: pass
            _db = None
            ok = _init_firebase()
            if ok:
                # Push all local data to Firebase after reconnecting
                col_map = {
                    "bays": self.bays, "bookings": self.bookings,
                    "customers": self.customers, "inventory": self.inventory,
                    "mechanics": self.mechanics, "jobs": self.jobs,
                    "queue": self.queue,
                }
                sync_pending_to_firebase(col_map)
                self._save()   # ensures all collections are pushed
            self.after(0, lambda: (
                self._toast("☁ Firebase connected and data synced!" if ok
                            else "⚠ Connection failed — check the key file.",
                            "success" if ok else "error"),
                self._refresh_settings()))
        threading.Thread(target=_do, daemon=True).start()

    def _sync_pending(self):
        """Upload any collections modified while offline to Firebase."""
        if not firebase_connected(): return
        pending = _load_pending_sync()
        if not pending: return
        col_map = {
            "bays": self.bays, "bookings": self.bookings,
            "customers": self.customers, "inventory": self.inventory,
            "mechanics": self.mechanics, "jobs": self.jobs,
            "queue": self.queue,
        }
        sync_pending_to_firebase(col_map)

    def _show_upgrade_prompt(self, resource: str, limit: int):
        """Show a modal telling the user they've hit their plan limit."""
        import webbrowser
        win = self._modal("Plan limit reached", w=460, h=220)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=24, pady=20)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text="🔒  Plan limit reached", font=F(13,"bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["warning"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))
        names = {"bays":"bay(s)","workers":"worker(s)","bookings":"concurrent booking(s)"}
        msg = ("Your " + LIMITS.plan + " plan allows " + str(limit) + " " + names.get(resource,resource) + ".\nUpgrade your plan to add more.")
        tk.Label(f, text=msg, font=F(11), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"],
                 wraplength=400, justify="left").pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,16))
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Upgrade plan",
            cmd=lambda: (webbrowser.open("https://garagepro.app/account"),
                         win.destroy()),
            style="success").pack(side="left", padx=(0,8))
        Btn(foot, "Close", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left")

    def _verify_license_now(self):
        """Force an immediate online license check and show result."""
        self._toast("Checking license…", "info")
        def _check():
            sub = _lc.verify(force_online=True)
            def _done():
                if sub.status == "revoked":
                    self._handle_revoked()
                elif sub.active:
                    LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub)
                    self._toast(
                        f"License verified ✓ — {sub.plan_name or sub.plan} plan", "success")
                    self._refresh_settings()
                elif sub.status == "offline":
                    self._toast("Offline — using cached license", "info")
                else:
                    self._toast(f"License issue: {sub.reason or sub.status}", "warning")
            self.after(0, _done)
        threading.Thread(target=_check, daemon=True).start()

    def _edit_license_key(self):
        """Allow user to enter a new license key without wiping the app."""
        win = self._modal("Edit License Key", w=480, h=260)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=24, pady=20)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        cur = _lc.status().license_key or ""
        tk.Label(f, text="CURRENT KEY", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
        tk.Label(f, text=cur or "None", font=F(10),
                 fg=C["info"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,12))

        tk.Label(f, text="NEW LICENSE KEY", font=F(8,"bold"),
                 fg=C["muted"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")
        key_var = tk.StringVar(value="GP-")
        key_ent = tk.Entry(f, textvariable=key_var, font=F(13,"bold"),
                           relief="flat", bd=0, bg=C["bg"], fg=C["success"],
                           insertbackground=C["text"],
                           highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"],
                           justify="center")
        key_ent.pack(fill="x", ipady=8, pady=(4,12))
        key_ent.icursor("end")

        err_var = tk.StringVar()
        tk.Label(f, textvariable=err_var, font=F(9),
                 fg=C["danger"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w")

        def _activate():
            key = key_var.get().strip()
            parts = key.split("-")
            if len(parts) != 5 or parts[0] != "GP" or                     not all(len(p) == 4 for p in parts[1:]):
                err_var.set("Invalid format. Use GP-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX")
                return
            err_var.set("Checking…")
            def _do():
                sub = _lc.activate(key)
                def _done():
                    if sub.active:
                        LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub)
                        win.destroy()
                        self._toast(
                            f"License updated ✓ — {sub.plan_name or sub.plan} plan",
                            "success")
                        self._refresh_settings()
                    else:
                        err_var.set(sub.reason or f"Failed: {sub.status}")
                self.after(0, _done)
            threading.Thread(target=_do, daemon=True).start()

        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e", pady=(4,0))
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(foot, "Activate new key", cmd=_activate, style="primary").pack(side="left")
        win.bind("<Return>", lambda e: _activate())

    def _remove_license(self):
        """Remove license cache — next launch will show activation screen."""
        win = self._modal("Remove License", w=420, h=220)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=24, pady=20)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text="⚠ Remove license?", font=F(13,"bold"),
                 fg=C["warning"], bg=C["surface"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))
        _rm_msg = ("This will delete the locally cached license. "
                  "The app will ask for your license key next time it starts. "
                  "Your workshop data will not be affected.")
        tk.Label(f, text=_rm_msg,
                 font=F(10), bg=C["surface"], fg=C["muted"],
                 justify="left", wraplength=370).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,16))
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        def _do_remove():
            _lc.clear()
            win.destroy()
            self._toast("License removed. Restart the app to re-activate.", "info")
            self._refresh_settings()
        Btn(foot, "Remove", cmd=_do_remove, style="danger").pack(side="left")

    def _save_invoice_settings(self):
        for key, attr in [("garage_phone","_inv_phone"),
                          ("garage_email","_inv_email"),
                          ("gstin","_inv_gstin")]:
            if hasattr(self, attr):
                self.settings[key] = getattr(self, attr).get().strip()
        if hasattr(self, "_inv_tax"):
            try:    self.settings["tax_percent"] = float(self._inv_tax.get())
            except: self.settings["tax_percent"] = 18.0
        save_settings(self.settings)
        self._toast("Invoice settings saved.", "success")

    def _pick_logo(self):
        path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            title="Select logo image",
            filetypes=[("Images","*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.gif"),("All","*.*")])
        if path:
            self._logo_path_var.set(path)

    def _clear_logo(self):
        self._logo_path_var.set("")

    def _save_workshop_details(self):
        self.settings["workshop_address"] = self._ws_address.get("1.0","end").strip()
        self.settings["invoice_logo"]     = self._logo_path_var.get().strip()
        save_settings(self.settings)
        self._toast("Workshop details saved.", "success")
        self._refresh_settings()

    def _change_ws_name(self):
        win = self._modal("Change workshop name", w=380, h=160)
        f   = tk.Frame(win, bg=C["surface"], padx=22, pady=18)
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        tk.Label(f, text="Workshop name", font=F(13,"bold"),
                 bg=C["surface"], fg=C["text"]).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))
        e = tk.Entry(f, font=F(12), relief="flat", bd=0,
                     bg=C["bg"], fg=C["text"], insertbackground=C["text"],
                     highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground=C["border"])
        e.insert(0, self.settings.get("workshop_name",""))
        e.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,10))
        def save():
            name = e.get().strip()
            if not name: return
            self.settings["workshop_name"] = name; save_settings(self.settings)
            self.title("Garage Manager — " + name); win.destroy()
            self._toast("Workshop name updated.", "success"); self.refresh_all()
        foot = tk.Frame(f, bg=C["surface"]); foot.pack(anchor="e")
        Btn(foot, "Cancel", cmd=win.destroy).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        Btn(foot, "Save",   cmd=save, style="primary").pack(side="left")



# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# TABLET WEB SERVER  (Flask — runs on same machine, tablets connect via LAN)
# Workers open http://<PC-IP>:5000 on their tablet browser.
# The desktop app starts this server automatically in a background thread.
# If Flask is not installed the server is silently skipped.
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# Shared reference so the web server can read/write live app data
_app_ref: "GarageApp | None" = None

def _get_local_ip() -> str:
    """Best-effort attempt to get the machine's LAN IP address."""
    try:
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        s.connect(("8.8.8.8", 80))
        ip = s.getsockname()[0]; s.close(); return ip
    except Exception:
        return "127.0.0.1"

def _start_web_server(app_instance: "GarageApp"):
    """Start the Flask tablet server in a daemon thread.
    Accessible from tablets on the same network."""
    global _app_ref
    _app_ref = app_instance
    if not _FLASK:
        print("[server] Flask not installed — tablet server disabled. "
              "Run: pip install flask")
        return None

    flask_app = Flask(__name__)
    # Silence Flask startup noise
    import logging
    log = logging.getLogger("werkzeug"); log.setLevel(logging.ERROR)

    # ── Helper: snapshot of live data (thread-safe shallow copy) ─────────────
    def _snapshot():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return {}
        return dict(
            bookings  = list(a.bookings),
            jobs      = list(a.jobs),
            bays      = list(a.bays),
            queue     = list(a.queue),
            mechanics = list(a.mechanics),
            customers  = list(a.customers),
            bay_types  = list(a.all_bay_types) if hasattr(a, "all_bay_types") else [],
            services   = [list(s) for s in SERVICE_LIST],
            inventory  = [{"id":i["id"],"name":i["name"],"qty":i["qty"],
                           "sell_price":i.get("sell_price",0),
                           "category":i.get("category","")}
                          for i in a.inventory],
            workshop  = a.settings.get("workshop_name", ""),
            admin_pin = a.settings.get("admin_pin", "1234"),
            now       = now_str(),
            today     = today_str(),
        )

    # ── GET /api/data — full snapshot for the tablet ──────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/data")
    def api_data():
        return jsonify(_snapshot())

    # ── POST /api/checkin — check a booking in ────────────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/checkin", methods=["POST"])
    def api_checkin():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        bk = next((b for b in a.bookings if b["id"] == data.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not bk: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Booking not found"}), 404

        # No bay provided → add to queue
        if not data.get("bay_id"):
            plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
            bay_type_override = (data.get("bay_type") or bk.get("type","")).strip()
            if bay_type_override:
                bk["type"] = bay_type_override
            if any(q["plate"].upper() == plate_up for q in a.queue):
                bk["status"] = "In queue"
                a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
                return jsonify({"ok": True, "queued": True,
                                "msg": f"{bk['plate']} is already in the queue"})
            a.queue.append(dict(id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
                                plate=bk["plate"], cust_name=bk["cust_name"],
                                type=bk.get("type",""), notes=bk.get("notes",""),
                                added_at=now_str()))
            bk["status"] = "In queue"
            a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
            return jsonify({"ok": True, "queued": True,
                            "msg": f"{bk['plate']} added to queue"})

        bay = next((b for b in a.bays if b["id"] == data.get("bay_id")), None)
        if not bay: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Bay not found"}), 404
        # Override bay type if requested
        bay_type_override = (data.get("bay_type") or "").strip()
        if bay_type_override:
            bk["type"] = bay_type_override
        bay["status"] = "Occupied"; bay["plate"] = bk["plate"]; bay["booking_id"] = bk["id"]
        bk["status"] = "Arrived"; bk["bay_id"] = bay["id"]
        bk["bay_num"] = bay["num"]; bk["checked_in"] = now_str()
        # ── Remove from queue if this vehicle was queued ───────────────────
        plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
        a.queue = [q for q in a.queue
                   if q["plate"].upper() != plate_up
                   and q.get("booking_id") != bk["id"]]
        # Assign worker if provided
        worker_name = data.get("worker_name", "").strip()
        if worker_name:
            bk["mechanic"] = worker_name
        # Create job card if not exists
        if not any(j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"] for j in a.jobs):
            a.jobs.append(dict(
                id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
                plate=bk["plate"], cust_name=bk["cust_name"],
                type=bk["type"], mechanic=worker_name or bk.get("mechanic",""),
                description=bk.get("notes",""),
                status="Pending", parts=[], labour_notes="",
                created_at=now_str(), completed_at=None))
        else:
            job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), None)
            if job:
                job["status"] = "Pending"   # reset if it was stale
                if worker_name:
                    job["mechanic"] = worker_name
        a._save()
        a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── POST /api/checkout — check out ───────────────────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/checkout", methods=["POST"])
    def api_checkout():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        bk = next((b for b in a.bookings if b["id"] == data.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not bk: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Booking not found"}), 404
        try: bk["actual_cost"] = float(data.get("actual_cost", bk.get("est_cost",0)))
        except: bk["actual_cost"] = 0.0
        bk["status"]      = "Checked out"
        bk["checked_out"] = now_str()
        # Free bay
        bay = next((b for b in a.bays if b["id"] == bk.get("bay_id")), None)
        if bay:
            bay["status"] = "Available"; bay["plate"] = None; bay["booking_id"] = None
        # Remove from queue (belt and braces)
        plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
        a.queue = [q for q in a.queue
                   if q["plate"].upper() != plate_up
                   and q.get("booking_id") != bk["id"]]
        # Complete job
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j.get("booking_id") == bk["id"]), None)
        if job: job["status"] = "Complete"; job["completed_at"] = now_str()
        a._save()
        a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        # Send thank-you email
        if bk.get("cust_email"):
            ws  = a.settings.get("workshop_name","")
            ref = bk.get("ref", bk["id"][:8].upper())
            subj = f"Thank you — [{ref}] — {ws}"
            body = (f"Dear {bk['cust_name']},\n\n"
                    f"Thank you for visiting {ws}!\n\n"
                    f"Booking Ref: {ref}\nVehicle: {bk['plate']}\n"
                    f"Total: \u20b9{bk['actual_cost']:.2f}\n\n"
                    f"Drive safely!\n{ws}")
            send_email_async(a.settings, bk["cust_email"], subj, body)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── POST /api/job_status — update job status ──────────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/job_status", methods=["POST"])
    def api_job_status():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j["id"] == data.get("job_id")), None)
        if not job: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Job not found"}), 404
        status = data.get("status")
        VALID = set(JOB_STATUSES) | {"Pending","Active","In Progress","Complete"}
        if status not in VALID:
            return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Invalid status"}), 400
        job["status"] = status
        if status in ("Active","In Progress"):  job["started_at"]   = now_str()
        if status in ("Complete","Ready for Delivery"): job["completed_at"] = now_str()
        if status == "Delivered":              job["delivered_at"] = now_str()
        bk = next((b for b in a.bookings if b["id"] == job.get("booking_id")), None)
        if status == "Ready for Delivery" and bk:
            if bk.get("bay_id"):
                bay = next((b for b in a.bays if b["id"] == bk["bay_id"]), None)
                if bay:
                    bay["status"] = "Available"; bay["plate"] = None; bay["booking_id"] = None
            bk["bay_id"] = None; bk["bay_num"] = None; bk["status"] = "Arrived"
        a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        email = (bk or {}).get("cust_email","")
        ws    = a.settings.get("workshop_name","")
        ref   = (bk or {}).get("ref", job["id"][:8].upper())
        mechs_str = ", ".join(job.get("mechanics",[]) or ([job["mechanic"]] if job.get("mechanic") else ["—"]))
        if email and status in ("Active","In Progress"):
            send_email_async(a.settings, email,
                f"Work started on your vehicle [{ref}] — {ws}",
                f"Dear {job['cust_name']},\n\nWork has started on your {job['plate']}.\n"
                f"Booking Ref: {ref}\nMechanic(s): {mechs_str}\n\nThank you,\n{ws}")
        elif email and status == "Ready for Delivery" and bk:
            include_photos = job.get("include_photos_in_email", False)
            send_invoice_email(job, bk, a.settings,
                               include_photos=include_photos)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── POST /api/job_note — save labour note ─────────────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/job_add_mechanic", methods=["POST"])
    def api_job_add_mechanic():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"App not ready"}),503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j["id"]==data.get("job_id","")),None)
        if not job: return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"Job not found"}),404
        name = (data.get("mechanic","") or "").strip()
        if not name: return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"Name required"}),400
        cur = list(job.get("mechanics",[]))
        if not cur and job.get("mechanic"):
            cur = [job["mechanic"]]
        if name not in cur:
            cur.append(name)
        job["mechanics"] = cur
        job["mechanic"]  = cur[0]
        a._save(); a.after(0,a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok":True,"mechanics":cur})

    @flask_app.route("/api/job_note", methods=["POST"])
    def api_job_note():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j["id"] == data.get("job_id")), None)
        if not job: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Job not found"}), 404
        job["labour_notes"] = data.get("note","")
        a._save(); return jsonify({"ok": True})

    @flask_app.route("/api/job_photo", methods=["POST"])
    def api_job_photo():
        """Add/remove photos on a job card from the tablet."""
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data   = flask_request.json or {}
        job_id = data.get("job_id","")
        action = data.get("action","add")
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j["id"] == job_id), None)
        if not job: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Job not found"}), 404

        if action == "add":
            label = data.get("label","Photo")
            b64   = data.get("data","")
            if not b64: return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"No image data"}),400
            job.setdefault("photos",[]).append(
                dict(label=label, data=b64, description=""))
        elif action == "remove":
            idx = int(data.get("idx",-1))
            photos = job.get("photos",[])
            if 0 <= idx < len(photos): photos.pop(idx)
        elif action == "set_include":
            job["include_photos_in_email"] = bool(data.get("value", False))

        a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "photos": job.get("photos",[])})

    # ── POST /api/job_parts — manage parts & charges ──────────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/job_parts", methods=["POST"])
    def api_job_parts():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data   = flask_request.json or {}
        job_id = data.get("job_id","")
        action = data.get("action","add")
        job = next((j for j in a.jobs if j["id"] == job_id), None)
        if not job: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Job not found"}), 404

        if action == "add":
            inv_id = data.get("inv_id","")
            qty    = max(int(data.get("qty", 1)), 1)
            inv    = next((i for i in a.inventory if i["id"] == inv_id), None)
            if not inv: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Item not found"}), 404
            if inv["qty"] < qty:
                return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": f"Only {inv['qty']} in stock"}), 409
            inv_price = float(data.get("invoice_price", inv.get("sell_price", 0)))
            job.setdefault("parts",[]).append(dict(
                inv_id=inv_id, name=inv["name"], qty=qty,
                unit_price=inv.get("sell_price",0), invoice_price=inv_price))
            inv["qty"] -= qty
        elif action == "add_custom":
            name  = (data.get("name","") or "").strip()
            qty   = max(int(data.get("qty",1)),1)
            price = float(data.get("invoice_price",0))
            if not name: return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"Name required"}),400
            job.setdefault("parts",[]).append(dict(
                inv_id=None, name=name, qty=qty,
                unit_price=price, invoice_price=price))
        elif action == "remove":
            idx   = int(data.get("part_idx",-1))
            parts = job.get("parts",[])
            if idx < 0 or idx >= len(parts):
                return jsonify({"ok":False,"error":"Invalid index"}),400
            pt = parts.pop(idx)
            if pt.get("inv_id"):
                inv = next((i for i in a.inventory if i["id"]==pt["inv_id"]),None)
                if inv: inv["qty"] += pt.get("qty",1)
        elif action == "update_price":
            idx   = int(data.get("part_idx",-1))
            parts = job.get("parts",[])
            if 0 <= idx < len(parts):
                parts[idx]["invoice_price"] = float(data.get("invoice_price",0))
        elif action == "add_svc":
            name  = (data.get("name","") or "Service charge").strip()
            price = float(data.get("price",0))
            job.setdefault("service_charges",[]).append(dict(name=name,price=price))
        elif action == "remove_svc":
            idx  = int(data.get("svc_idx",-1))
            svcs = job.get("service_charges",[])
            if 0 <= idx < len(svcs): svcs.pop(idx)
        elif action == "update_svc_price":
            idx  = int(data.get("svc_idx",-1))
            svcs = job.get("service_charges",[])
            if 0 <= idx < len(svcs):
                svcs[idx]["price"] = float(data.get("price",0))

        a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        inv_snap = [{"id":i["id"],"name":i["name"],"qty":i["qty"],
                     "sell_price":i.get("sell_price",0),
                     "category":i.get("category","")} for i in a.inventory]
        return jsonify({"ok": True,
                        "parts":           job.get("parts",[]),
                        "service_charges": job.get("service_charges",[]),
                        "inventory":       inv_snap})

    # ── POST /api/walkin — create a walk-in booking and check it straight in ──
    @flask_app.route("/api/walkin", methods=["POST"])
    def api_walkin():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}

        cust_id     = (data.get("cust_id")     or "").strip()  # existing customer id
        cust_name   = (data.get("cust_name")   or "").strip()
        plate       = (data.get("plate")        or "").strip().upper()
        service     = (data.get("service")      or "").strip()
        bay_type    = (data.get("bay_type")     or "").strip()
        bay_id      = (data.get("bay_id")       or "").strip()
        worker_name = (data.get("worker_name")  or "").strip()
        phone       = (data.get("phone")        or "").strip()
        email       = (data.get("email")        or "").strip()
        car_model   = (data.get("car_model")    or "").strip()
        notes       = (data.get("notes")        or "").strip()

        if not cust_name: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Customer name required"}), 400
        if not plate:     return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Plate required"}), 400
        if not service:   return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Service required"}), 400

        bay = next((b for b in a.bays if b["id"] == bay_id), None) if bay_id else None

        # ── Resolve / create customer ───────────────────────────────────────
        cust = None
        if cust_id:
            cust = next((c for c in a.customers if c["id"] == cust_id), None)

        if cust:
            # Existing customer — check if car already on file, add if not
            existing_car = next((c for c in cust.get("cars", [])
                                 if c["plate"].upper() == plate), None)
            if not existing_car:
                new_car = dict(id=uid(), plate=plate, model=car_model, year="", colour="")
                cust.setdefault("cars", []).append(new_car)
                car_id = new_car["id"]
            else:
                car_id = existing_car["id"]
            cust_name  = cust["name"]
            phone      = cust.get("phone", phone)
            email      = cust.get("email", email)
        else:
            # New customer — create and add to database
            new_car  = dict(id=uid(), plate=plate, model=car_model, year="", colour="")
            cust     = dict(id=uid(), name=cust_name, phone=phone, email=email,
                            cars=[new_car], bookings=[], created_at=now_str())
            a.customers.append(cust)
            car_id = new_car["id"]

        # Link booking to customer
        cust_id = cust["id"]

        # ── Create booking ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        ref = booking_ref()
        bk  = dict(
            id=uid(), ref=ref,
            cust_name=cust_name, cust_phone=phone, cust_email=email,
            cust_id=cust_id, car_id=car_id,
            plate=plate, car_desc=car_model,
            service=service, type=bay_type or (bay["type"] if bay else ""),
            date=today_str(), time=now_str()[-5:],
            mechanic=worker_name,
            est_cost=0.0,
            notes=notes,
            status="Arrived" if bay else "Booked",
            bay_id=bay["id"]  if bay else None,
            bay_num=bay["num"] if bay else None,
            checked_in=now_str() if bay else None,
            created_at=now_str(),
        )
        a.bookings.append(bk)
        cust.setdefault("bookings", []).append(bk["id"])

        # ── Occupy bay + clear from queue if applicable ─────────────────────
        if bay:
            bay["status"]     = "Occupied"
            bay["plate"]      = plate
            bay["booking_id"] = bk["id"]
            # Remove from queue if this plate was waiting
            a.queue = [q for q in a.queue if q["plate"].upper() != plate.upper()]

        # ── Create job card ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        a.jobs.append(dict(
            id=uid(), booking_id=bk["id"],
            plate=plate, cust_name=cust_name,
            type=service, mechanic=worker_name,
            description=notes,
            status="Pending", parts=[], labour_notes="",
            created_at=now_str(), completed_at=None,
        ))

        a._save()
        a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        is_new_cust = not bool(data.get("cust_id"))
        return jsonify({"ok": True, "ref": ref, "booking_id": bk["id"],
                        "new_customer": is_new_cust, "cust_id": cust_id})

    # ── POST /api/queue_add — add a vehicle to the waiting queue ─────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/queue_add", methods=["POST"])
    def api_queue_add():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        plate     = (data.get("plate")     or "").strip().upper()
        cust_name = (data.get("cust_name") or "").strip()
        bay_type  = (data.get("bay_type")  or "").strip()
        notes     = (data.get("notes")     or "").strip()
        if not plate:     return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Plate required"}), 400
        if not cust_name: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Name required"}), 400
        # Duplicate check — same plate already in queue
        if any(q["plate"].upper() == plate for q in a.queue):
            return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": f"{plate} is already in the queue"}), 409
        entry = dict(id=uid(), plate=plate, cust_name=cust_name,
                     type=bay_type, notes=notes, added_at=now_str())
        a.queue.append(entry)
        a._save()
        a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── POST /api/booking_update — edit/cancel a booking ────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/booking_update", methods=["POST"])
    def api_booking_update():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data = flask_request.json or {}
        bk = next((b for b in a.bookings if b["id"] == data.get("booking_id")), None)
        if not bk: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Booking not found"}), 404
        action = data.get("action","")
        if action == "cancel":
            bk["status"] = "Cancelled"
            # Free bay if occupied
            bay = next((b for b in a.bays if b["id"] == bk.get("bay_id")), None)
            if bay: bay["status"]="Available"; bay["plate"]=None; bay["booking_id"]=None
            # Remove from queue by both booking_id and plate
            plate_up = bk["plate"].upper()
            a.queue = [q for q in a.queue
                       if q.get("booking_id") != bk["id"]
                       and q["plate"].upper() != plate_up]
        elif action == "edit":
            for field in ["service","type","date","time","mechanic","notes","est_cost"]:
                if field in data:
                    bk[field] = data[field]
        elif action == "reschedule":
            bk["date"]   = data.get("date", bk["date"])
            bk["time"]   = data.get("time", bk["time"])
            bk["status"] = "Booked"
        a._save(); a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── POST /api/queue_remove — remove a vehicle from the queue ─────────────
    @flask_app.route("/api/queue_remove", methods=["POST"])
    def api_queue_remove():
        a = _app_ref
        if not a: return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "App not ready"}), 503
        data  = flask_request.json or {}
        qid   = data.get("queue_id", "")
        plate = (data.get("plate") or "").strip().upper()
        # Match by id or plate
        idx = next((i for i, q in enumerate(a.queue)
                    if q["id"] == qid or q["plate"].upper() == plate), -1)
        if idx < 0:
            return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": "Not found in queue"}), 404
        a.queue.pop(idx)
        a._save()
        a.after(0, a.refresh_all)
        return jsonify({"ok": True})

    # ── GET / — serve tablet.html (bundled or from disk) ────────────────────
    @flask_app.route("/")
    def index():
        # PyInstaller bundles tablet.html into _MEIPASS; plain script reads from disk
        if getattr(_sys, 'frozen', False):
            html_path = os.path.join(_sys._MEIPASS, "tablet.html")
        else:
            html_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "tablet.html")
        with open(html_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            html = f.read()
        return html, 200, {"Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8"}


    port = (app_instance.settings.get("server_port", 5000)
            if app_instance else 5000)
    ip   = _get_local_ip()

    def _run():
        try:
            flask_app.run(host="0.0.0.0", port=port, debug=False,
                          use_reloader=False, threaded=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[server] Failed to start on port {port}: {e}")

    try:
        t = threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True)
        t.start()
        print(f"[server] Tablet interface: http://{ip}:{port}")
        return ip, port
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[server] Could not start server: {e}")
        return None


# ── Entry point ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _run_main_app():
    """Start the main GarageApp window."""
    app = GarageApp()
    # Refresh brand immediately from latest cache (feels instant on restart)
    app.after(100, app._refresh_brand)
    result = _start_web_server(app)
    if result:
        ip, port = result
        app.after(1500, lambda: app._toast(
            f"Tablet server — http://{ip}:{port}", "info"))
    else:
        app.after(1500, lambda: app._toast(
            "Flask not installed — tablet server disabled. "
            "Run: pip install flask", "warning"))

    # Mac: give the window time to fully render before forcing to front
    if _OS == "Darwin":
        app.withdraw()
        app.update_idletasks()
        app.update()
        app.after(500, lambda: (
            app.deiconify(),
            app.lift(),
            app.focus_force(),
            app.attributes("-topmost", True),
            app.after(200, lambda: app.attributes("-topmost", False))
        ))
    else:
        app.update_idletasks()
        app.update()
        app.lift()
        app.focus_force()

    if _OS == "Darwin":
        try:
            import subprocess
            _pid = str(os.getpid())
            _script = (
                'tell application "System Events"\n'
                '  set frontmost of first process whose unix id is '
                + _pid + ' to true\nend tell'
            )
            subprocess.run(["osascript", "-e", _script],
                           timeout=2, capture_output=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    app.mainloop()


def _run_license_flow(on_success):
    """Show license activation screen, then call on_success."""
    screen = LicenseScreen(on_success)
    screen.update(); screen.lift(); screen.focus_force()
    screen.mainloop()


def _check_cached_license() -> SubscriptionStatus:
    """
    Called at startup. Uses LicenseClient.verify().
    Returns a SubscriptionStatus — caller dispatches on .status.
    """
    return _lc.verify(force_online=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if _OS == "Windows":
        try:
            from ctypes import windll
            windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    sub = _check_cached_license()

    STATUS_MESSAGES = {
        "not_activated": None,  # show activation screen
        "revoked":       "This license has been revoked.\nContact support@garagepro.app",
        "expired":       "Your subscription has expired.\nRenew at garagepro.app/account",
        "not_found":     "License key not recognised.\nRe-enter your key or contact support.",
        "grace_expired": "Please reconnect to the internet\nto verify your subscription.",
    }

    def _launch():
        LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub)
        _run_main_app()

    if sub.status in ("active", "offline"):
        # Valid (online or offline-grace) — go straight in
        LIMITS.update_from_sub(sub)
        _run_main_app()

    elif sub.status == "not_activated":
        _run_license_flow(_run_main_app)

    elif sub.status in STATUS_MESSAGES:
        # Show a blocking error then allow re-entry
        def _on_new_key():
            _run_license_flow(_run_main_app)
        rev = RevokedScreen(sub.license_key or "", _on_new_key,
                            message=STATUS_MESSAGES[sub.status])
        rev.mainloop()

    else:
        # Unknown / grace_expired etc — show activation screen
        _run_license_flow(_run_main_app)
